# MASTER CONTEXT FOR GEMINI — EDRIS / ALL AVAILABLE PROJECTS

## Instructions for Gemini
Treat this as a reference archive, not as a request to blindly accept every historical statement. Preserve the user's latest explicit decisions where known; flag contradictions and ask before irreversible decisions. Distinguish explicit user statements, assistant-generated proposals, inferred preferences, and unvalidated engineering concepts. Do not invent missing chat history or claim the archive is complete beyond the available Library files. When technical or commercial advice is requested, clearly separate validated facts from hypotheses.

## Source and completeness note
This document combines the saved context dump with accessible text from the available project files and extracted PDF text where possible. Images and binary source files remain in the companion ZIP archive. It is not a verbatim transcript of all ChatGPT conversations.

# SAVED USER DATA DUMP

# USER DATA DUMP — EDRIS / PERSONAL PROJECTS

**Prepared:** 2026-10-15  
**Purpose:** Portable summary of user-specific facts, preferences, project decisions, and project files available to the assistant in its current saved context and Library.

## Important scope and limitations
This is a best-effort export of the user-specific information currently available to the assistant, not a complete account-level archive of every ChatGPT conversation, hidden system record, or every message ever sent. The assistant cannot independently retrieve every chat in the account as a single transcript. Some details below are explicit user-provided facts; others are summaries of prior project work. Where prior decisions conflict, the latest known canonical decision is preferred and older variants are identified as historical. The included files are existing Library artifacts that were accessible and materialized for this export; not every historical artifact is included.

# 1. Personal background and preferences

## Identity / broad background
- Name used for the fashion-house founder identity: **EDRIS NAZPARVAR**.
- User has described themself as an **Iranian Turk born in Mazandaran**.
- A cultural/geographic imagery path mentioned for the house: **Qaemshahr → Tehran → Paris → Rome → Los Angeles → New York**.
- A prior conversation stated the user was 26 in 2023; this is a historical statement, not a verified current age.

## Personality / creative tendencies inferred from conversations (not a diagnosis)
- Highly imaginative, ambitious, systems-oriented, and interested in turning ideas into real, usable artifacts.
- Strong preference for coherent worlds and unified design languages rather than isolated products.
- Interested in both aesthetics and engineering: tailoring, product systems, vehicles, mechanical systems, digital tools, commercialization, and technical research.
- Likes cinematic, dark, architectural, carefully engineered, distinctive designs; values originality and long-term identity.
- Wants practical deliverables (patterns, technical specifications, PDFs, app prototypes, licensing plans) rather than only high-level discussion.
- Iterative and exacting: frequently refines specifications and expects previous decisions to carry forward.
- Strongly dislikes visual sloppiness, interwoven/overlapping pattern pieces, formatting defects, curved lapel lines, sagging/oversized proportions, and generic or costume-like design.
- Wants design to feel fashionable and functional, not merely decorative.
- These are conversational inferences and may not capture the whole person.

# 2. Project: EDRIS — House of Fashion

## Canonical identity
- Public house: **EDRIS**.
- Descriptor: **A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN**.
- House line: **BUILD AGAINST TIME.**
- Founder / creative identity: **EDRIS NAZPARVAR**.
- Crest: **THE MASKED RABBIT**.
- Internal bespoke / atelier system: **TUX GEN**. TUX GEN is not intended as the public-facing house/sub-brand.

## Philosophy and DNA
Core thought: **“We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.”**

Master idea: civilization against nature, created in the face of mortality. Nature → life → desire → civilization → creation → decay → death; death completes the cycle.

Permanent pillars:
1. **Ancient Soul — Iran:** Iranian history, architecture, gardens, poetry, geometry, landscapes, metalwork and textiles translated subtly into design; Iran is the soul, not costume.
2. **Dark Architecture:** Art Deco geometry as structural grammar; Gothic/ruin atmosphere, monumentality, melancholy and mortality without fantasy costume.
3. **Cinematic Glamour:** Old Hollywood, James Bond precision, Godfather authority, Grand Budapest Hotel theatrical discipline, strong film-language detail.
4. **The Feral Human:** instinct beneath civilization, sensuality, severity, danger, nature without literal animal costume.
5. **The Machine:** functional/engineered detail, mechanical beauty, technology versus humanity.
6. **Mortality:** time, ruins, memory, destiny, death, inheritance, artifacts and legacy.

Influences discussed: Dark Souls mood; BioShock; Metal Gear Solid 4; Watch Dogs; Cyberpunk; steampunk as a restrained accent; Grand Budapest Hotel; James Bond; Vito Corleone; Bruce Wayne; Agent 47; Donnie Darko’s rabbit; HoZho mask concept. Art Deco / 1920s–30s geometry and Old Hollywood masculinity are foundational. Futurism, tactical engineering and cyberpunk are accents, not costume.

Design test: beautiful at first glance, strange at second, meaningful at third; recognizable without the logo; Iranian in soul, architectural and materially convincing, durable enough to become an artifact, useful or emotionally necessary.

## Product universe / catalogue
House universe covers formal and casual clothing, bespoke tailoring, outerwear, footwear, leather goods, eyewear, jewelry, fragrance, accessories, watches/horology, grooming, future home/desk objects, packaging, atelier services, provenance, archive, quality standards and digital experiences.

The catalogue evolved through versions 1.0, 2.0, 2.1, 3.0, ecosystem master v4.2 and execution master v5.0. **EDRIS House Catalogue v3.0** is a canonical product/house master in the available files; **Execution Master v5.0** is the execution-layer document. Older catalogue versions are retained as historical drafts.

Product naming examples from the latest known house system include:
- **001 — THE ONE OF ONE TUXEDO**
- **THE OPERATIVE** — 001 watch concept
- **AFTER THE RAIN** — 001 fragrance concept
- **THE MASKED RABBIT** — house crest
- Product names include STRUCTURE SHIRT, COLUMN, MONUMENT, DOSSIER, RABBIT, SEAL, HOUSE CUFFLINKS, HOUSE STUDS, OPERATIVE, VAULT, 12 CM BOW, FOUNDATION TEE, DISTRICT DENIM, RUIN, HEIRLOOM KNIT, AFTERHOURS, SPIRE, DECO, TWENTY-FOUR, NOIR, VOYAGE, AXIS, CREST, MIDNIGHT, ARCHIVE, PATRON, MONSOON, PORTAL, WILD, OBSIDIAN, FRONTIER, RAIN, RELIC, VECTOR, NOCTURNE, SALON, LINK, BASTION, MASK, RELIQUARY, OBSERVATORY, ALTAR and WET STONE.

## Style / materials
- Dark, architectural, cinematic luxury; near-black/deep forest black, ivory, aged silver and restrained oxblood.
- Art Deco geometry and symmetry, machine-age luxury, gothic/ruin mood, Old Hollywood masculinity.
- Avoid generic “Persian luxury,” Persian costume, costume Gothic, medieval fantasy, literal steampunk/cyberpunk cosplay, military cosplay, logo-covered luxury, fake aristocracy, generic quiet luxury, excessive skulls/gears/claws and cheap trend-chasing.
- Jewelry language: aged/polished silver; Roman/architectural luxury (Bvlgari), Cartier-like refinement/status, Chrome Hearts-like substantial silver/gothic rebel craft; selective gold and dark metal.
- Watch references: Seiko SKX007, Rolex Submariner, Vacheron Constantin; goal is a James Bond-like tactical-dress watch direction.
- Sneaker references now part of DNA: Adidas Originals Adi2000, Niteball II and Yeezy 700.

## Website / app ecosystem
- Website/app should function as an ecosystem, not just a shop.
- Proposed areas: HOUSE (editorial/campaigns/films), SHOP (controlled discovery), PASSPORT (authenticated object records), ATELIER (consultation, measurements, project status, fittings, approvals, messages, repairs), ARCHIVE (private owner archive plus public approved archive).
- Digital passport fields may include piece ID, category, collection, creation date, materials, hardware, construction notes, pattern/version, atelier/craftsperson where appropriate, fitting/alteration history, authenticity/provenance with consent, care, photography, design notes and service history.
- TUX GEN modules: client profile, measurements/body scan data, design concepts, garment configuration, material/hardware library, pattern records, fitting records, alterations, production workflow, QC, digital passport, provenance and archive.
- Bespoke flow: consultation → measurement → design brief → material selection → configuration → pattern → toile/prototype → fitting → correction → final construction → QC → delivery → passport → archive → aftercare.
- Privacy principles: minimum necessary personal data, access control, versioned patterns, timestamped measurements, revision history, production data separated from unnecessary personal data, documented corrections.
- Intended bilingual Persian/English PWA/app. Earlier desired onboarding: up to 6 main questions before body scan plus 6 optional styling questions after scan; quick mode using user as default fit and bespoke mode; body scanning, visual models, textile suggestions by budget, tailoring manual/sketches and interactive modern UI.
- Product concept must not overclaim technical capability: production-grade AI body scanning, accurate 3D cloth simulation, validated 1:1 pattern generation and production infrastructure require separate validated engineering.

## Commercial/operational direction
- Move from concept invention to physical/operational execution: production patterns, prototypes, fitting and revision, golden samples, material/hardware engineering, suppliers, costing/pricing, regulatory/labeling review, packaging, TUX GEN records, website/app data model, product photography/copy and launch.
- No manipulative luxury tactics such as fake scarcity, countdowns or fabricated waitlists.
- Quality gate includes safe construction, material performance, dimensional stability, hardware consistency, fit, closures, packaging, care instructions, provenance, labeling, supplier quality, regulatory review and conformity to approved golden sample.

# 3. Project: TUXEDO 001 — ONE OF ONE

## Design direction (latest canonical spec)
- House: EDRIS; older files still use “Uncle Ed’s” as a historical working name.
- Identity: **001 / ONE OF ONE**.
- Midnight / forest-black luxury wool; very dark green undertone only if extremely subtle, with plain black also considered.
- Single-breasted, one button, long modern Hollywood/Savile Row body.
- Sculpted but not exaggerated Hollywood shoulder; structured shoulder and sleeve head; high armhole; full canvas; horsehair chest.
- Controlled waist; clean extended body; boxy/architectural rather than sagging or oversized.
- **12 cm straight peak lapel — maximum and locked preference.** User strongly dislikes a visibly curved lapel line; straight controlled outer edge, straight/controlled inner edge, sharp symmetrical peak, no decorative “belly,” no exaggerated roll.
- Deep black satin lapel facing; satin treatment considered for formal contrast. Later execution notes include satin facing.
- Double-jetted pockets; double vents.
- White fine formal cotton shirt, formal collar, French cuffs, clean/concealed front, aged-silver shirt studs.
- Large architectural black butterfly bow tie.
- Aged-silver jacket button and cufflinks.
- White boutonniere / lapel rose and white pocket square discussed.
- High-rise flat-front trousers, side adjusters, no belt loops. Historical specs conflict on satin trouser stripe: an earlier final master includes a black satin stripe; a prior user decision explicitly said no matching trouser stripe. Treat this as an unresolved version conflict that should be confirmed before production rather than silently assuming.
- Hidden Iranian geometry; black/deep-forest cupro/Bemberg lining with restrained oxblood interior accents considered.
- Approximate wool target in an earlier specification: 280–320 g/m², low-to-medium controlled sheen, good drape and structure.

## Body and pattern information (user-provided / historical)
Drafting base height: **180 cm** (user once said perhaps 181, but asked to use 180). Desired wearable range approximately 180–185 cm and 80–90 kg; user said current around 90 kg, wants suit to look best around 85 kg and still wearable at 80 and 90 kg. User wants a slimmer appearance; chest is larger; shoulders/biceps may increase; target posture is corrected/ideal posture. User wants to avoid later alterations and accommodate a range of body weights, but a real toile/fitting remains necessary.

A separate raw tailor chart was also supplied in a later conversation: 49 cm, 54 cm, 30 cm, 56 cm, 105 cm, 73 cm, 35 cm, 18 cm, and 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist. The meanings of all entries were not fully mapped in the stored summary; do not treat these as finished-garment measurements without reconciling the tailor's chart.

Historical trouser spec included finished seat target 116–119 cm, thigh 64–66 cm, knee 45–46 cm, calf/ankle body references 43/26 cm, outseam reference 100 cm and a controlled slight break. Earlier spec says no stripe; later execution master includes stripe. Resolve before cutting.

## Pattern / tailoring caveats
- User reported previous generated pattern drawings looked abnormal, with pieces interwoven/overlapping. The goal is one large-format plotter pattern, not tiled A3/A4 sheets.
- A 1:1 drawing/PDF exists in Library, but should not be assumed production-safe solely because it exists. Pattern pieces, grainlines, seam allowances, notches, balance points, pocket positions, button position, lapel break/roll, vents, cut quantities and scale must be checked.
- A physical toile is required because posture, shoulder slope, arm pitch and left/right asymmetry cannot be inferred reliably from circumferences alone.
- Fitting order: front/back balance → shoulder slope → collar/neck → chest → lapel roll → waist/side body → sleeve pitch → sleeve length → trouser rise/seat → thigh/knee/taper → hem/break. Do not suppress waist before balance is correct.

# 4. Project: Adaptive Buoyancy Recovery Platform / Titanic / Fragile Shipwrecks

## Goal and intent
- User described an original engineering concept for recovering fragile shipwrecks, including the Titanic, called an **adaptive buoyancy recovery platform**.
- The central intent is to investigate whether the idea solves a real engineering problem or represents a meaningful engineering leap.
- Commercial goal: potentially license the research/technology to a buyer and earn royalties, rather than necessarily manufacturing the system personally.
- Desired work: technically honest development plan, rigorous research paper, equations and diagrams, prior-art awareness, and commercialization/licensing package.
- User asked for a PDF deliverable and said they would send the original essay/files when they find them.

## Development principles captured
- Do not claim guaranteed patentability or novelty without a proper prior-art search and qualified patent advice.
- Preserve confidentiality before public disclosure where patent rights matter; document inventorship, dates, design rationale, calculations and variants.
- Develop a clear technical disclosure: problem statement, system architecture, buoyancy/force balance, load paths, structural constraints, control/monitoring concept, deployment/recovery sequence, failure modes, safety factors, test plan, uncertainty analysis and comparison to existing salvage methods.
- Research paper should distinguish established engineering principles from hypotheses and unvalidated concept claims.
- Commercial plan should identify potential licensees/buyers, value proposition, evidence needed for due diligence, IP strategy, staged proof-of-concept, and realistic royalty/licensing structures. No guarantee of sale or royalties.

## Known limitation
- The original full essay/specification is not present in the currently retrieved Library results. The concept summary is available, but detailed mechanism/equations cannot be reconstructed faithfully without the user's original notes. A library search did not surface a dedicated Titanic/shipwreck file in the available inventory.

# 5. Project: 206 WRC — Peugeot 206 / Iran

- Project label: **206 WRC**.
- Platform: **IKCO Peugeot 206 four-door sedan**, for Iran.
- Exterior: white; white OZ Rally Racing 4x4 wheels.
- Visual philosophy: keep the car looking relatively stock / silent-look; body changes should be discreet.
- Drivetrain ambition: R32-style all-wheel drive concept.
- Transmission: short-ratio 6-speed manual.
- Engine concept: **EW10J4S twin-charged** (supercharger + turbocharger concept). The intent is a highly engineered dual-mode setup, not just a cosmetic build.
- Operating modes: daily soft/low-consumption mode and a separate race mode.
- Overall intent: usable daily drivability and lower consumption in one mode, high-performance behavior in race mode, with subtle exterior appearance.
- This is a concept memory summary; detailed validated packaging, engine management, thermal, drivetrain, legality and safety engineering remain to be developed. Do not imply that the twin-charged AWD conversion is already engineered or proven.

# 6. Fragrance preferences and personal scent profile

## Core scent direction
Very dark, wet, bitter, inky, woody-green; mossy; wet cold forest; seaweed/salt/mineral water; oud; vetiver; sage; cardamom; ambroxan; tobacco; vanilla in restrained amounts. Strong longevity and prominent projection are desired. User dislikes overly sweet/cloying fragrances. Bitter-orange openings are acceptable if not sour. User wants seductive, dark and dangerous character, and likes rose.

# 9. Current known preferences about how the assistant should work with these projects

- Carry forward prior decisions and context rather than making the user repeat them.
- Distinguish a concept from a validated engineering design.
- Be honest about uncertainties, prior art, manufacturability and technical limits.
- When creating tailoring documents, ensure the drawings are clean, non-overlapping, correctly scaled and usable by a tailor; avoid presenting an unvalidated pattern as ready to cut.
- Keep EDRIS's house identity coherent and avoid reverting to the old “Uncle Ed’s” name as the public brand, while retaining older filenames as historical artifacts.
- For the shipwreck concept, preserve confidentiality and prepare an IP-aware commercialization path; do not promise patentability or a guaranteed buyer.
- For 206 WRC, preserve the twin-charged EW10J4S, AWD concept, short-ratio six-speed, white body/wheels, stock-looking exterior, and separate daily/race modes as the remembered concept.

---

# 10. What is not included / cannot be guaranteed by this dump

- A verbatim transcript of every chat in the account.
- Every message, attachment, image, or deleted/archived conversation.
- Private account data, internal telemetry, hidden system records, or platform-side records inaccessible to the assistant.
- A complete, verified technical specification for the Titanic recovery platform: the original essay/files were not found in the accessible Library inventory.
- Confirmation that every listed historical measurement or design choice is still current; conflicts are noted where known.

For a true account-wide conversation archive, use ChatGPT's own **Settings → Data Controls → Export Data** workflow. That export is separate from this curated project/personality dump.

# SOURCE FILE: 005_EDRIS_App_Prototype_v1.0.md

# EDRIS — THE HOUSE APP v1.0

## Product purpose
A private mobile layer for the EDRIS house: discovery, commerce, digital passport, atelier communication, appointments, aftercare and archive.

## Navigation
HOUSE / SHOP / PASSPORT / ATELIER / ARCHIVE

## HOUSE
- Campaign hero
- Current collection
- Film
- House story
- New object releases
- Fragrance world

## SHOP
- Categories: tailoring, casual, outerwear, leather, footwear, jewelry, eyewear, fragrance, watches, objects
- Filters: material, color, fit, occasion, collection, availability
- Product page mirrors website but adds saved items and private recommendations

## PASSPORT
- My EDRIS objects
- Authenticity
- Provenance
- Care
- Service
- Alterations
- Warranty/records where applicable
- Watch serial/movement/service history
- Bespoke garment pattern and fitting history

## ATELIER
- Book consultation
- Measurement appointment
- Fitting calendar
- Bespoke project status
- Approvals
- Secure atelier messaging
- Repair/alteration request

## ARCHIVE
- Personal collection timeline
- Public house archive
- Campaigns
- Product stories
- Approved historical pieces

## Personalization rules
Use explicit preferences, purchase history and fitting data only where necessary and consented. The app must make privacy visible and controllable. No behavioral surveillance aesthetic, no spam, no artificial scarcity.

## Signature feature: THE OBJECT PASSPORT
Scan/enter the object ID to open its authenticated EDRIS record. The owner sees a private timeline of the object from creation through service and ownership/provenance events that they choose to record.

## Signature feature: ATELIER MIRROR
A private client view of the current bespoke project: silhouette, cloth, hardware, pattern revision, fitting stage, approvals and final archive record.

## Signature feature: THE HOUSE EDIT
A restrained personal edit: 3–8 products that work together based on the client's actual wardrobe, preferences, climate and occasion. Never a feed of endless recommendations.

## Push notification policy
Only meaningful service and client events. No daily promotional spam.

## Visual design
Near-black background, ivory type, aged-silver accents, editorial serif for stories, restrained sans for controls, sharp geometry, no excessive rounded cards, no gamified behavior.

# SOURCE FILE: 006_EDRIS_House_Catalogue_v1.0.md

# EDRIS — HOUSE CATALOGUE v1.0
## A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN
### BUILD AGAINST TIME.

**Status:** Canonical working catalogue / product design master
**House:** EDRIS
**Internal bespoke system:** TUX GEN
**House crest:** The Masked Rabbit

---

## 1. HOUSE PRINCIPLE

EDRIS is a complete luxury house spanning formal and casual clothing, bespoke tailoring, footwear, leather goods, accessories, eyewear, jewelry, fragrance, future horology, grooming and selected home objects.

Core philosophy:

> We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.

Design test:
1. Beautiful at first glance.
2. Slightly strange at second glance.
3. Meaningful at third glance.
4. Recognizably EDRIS without a logo.
5. Iranian in soul, never costume.
6. Architectural, crafted, durable and capable of becoming an artifact.

---

# 2. MASTER DESIGN LANGUAGE

**Iran:** soul and hidden geometry.
**Art Deco:** symmetry, verticality, stepped geometry, radiating lines and machine-age elegance.
**Gothic/ruins:** monumentality, melancholy, mortality and mystery.
**Old Hollywood:** glamour, tuxedo culture, masculine myth.
**Grand Budapest:** immaculate period precision and theatrical discipline.
**Bond:** precision and seduction.
**Godfather:** authority and heritage.
**Scorsese/Tarantino/Fincher:** character, iconic objects, tension, darkness and obsessive control.
**Feral Human:** instinct beneath civilization.
**Machine:** engineered function and human technology.
**Mortality:** time, decay, memory, destiny and legacy.

Core contrasts:
- black × white
- polished × oxidized
- soft wool × hard metal
- animal × mask
- ancient × futuristic
- Iran × Hollywood
- beauty × unease
- life × death

Primary palette:
- Midnight Black
- Black
- Forest Black
- Ink
- Deep Bottle Green
- Midnight Navy
- Deep Burgundy
- Ivory
- Aged Silver
- Gunmetal

---

# 3. HOUSE SYMBOL SYSTEM

## THE MASKED RABBIT

Front-facing, symmetrical, vertically elongated, severe and intelligent. Long narrow ears; architectural facial planes; deep recessed eyes; angular elongated muzzle; subtle Iranian geometry; Art Deco portal frame; lower geometry suggesting an obelisk/tomb.

Three official marks:
1. FULL CREST — rabbit + portal
2. MASKED RABBIT — rabbit only
3. RABBIT SILHOUETTE — simplified mark

Master physical treatment: blackened/aged sterling silver with polished high points, brushed surfaces and oxidized recesses.

The rabbit is a house signature, not a decorative animal motif.

---

Environment:
- Black stone
- Dark wood
- Blackened metal
- Selective greenery
- Warm controlled light
- Iranian symmetry interpreted through Art Deco geometry
- Monumental/Gothic scale without theatrical theme-park décor

Client journey:
**arrival → consultation → material discovery → design → fitting → reveal → packaging → aftercare → archive**

The atelier is not simply a store. It is the physical heart of EDRIS.

# 17. COMMUNICATION

Photography:
- Cinematic
- Tactile
- Architectural
- Strong negative space
- Controlled blacks
- Character-driven casting

Film:
- Short cinematic narratives
- Rain, stone, machinery, fabric, silence and human presence
- Avoid excessive CGI and literal symbolism

Voice:
- Precise
- Poetic
- Restrained
- Intelligent
- Mysterious without over-explaining

# 18. PRODUCT HIERARCHY

**ONE-OF-ONE** — unique, numbered, archived.
**BESPOKE** — individualized through TUX GEN and atelier craft.
**MADE-TO-MEASURE** — personalized house blocks.
**READY-TO-WEAR** — scalable house-standard blocks.
**LIMITED EDITION** — fixed quantity and individually numbered.
**CORE** — permanent products that define EDRIS.

---

# 19. TUX GEN — INTERNAL HOUSE ENGINE

Core modules:
- Client profile
- Measurement/body-scan data
- Design concepts
- Garment configuration
- Material library
- Hardware library
- Pattern records
- Fitting records
- Alterations
- Production workflow
- QC
- Digital passport
- Provenance
- Archive

Bespoke workflow:
**consultation → measurement → brief → material selection → configuration → pattern → toile/prototype → fitting → correction → final construction → QC → delivery → passport → archive → aftercare**

Every garment receives a unique internal identifier and versioned design/pattern record.

---

# 20. DIGITAL PASSPORT

Each significant piece can record:
- Piece ID
- Category
- Collection
- Creation date
- Materials
- Hardware
- Construction notes
- Pattern/version
- Craftsperson/atelier where appropriate
- Fitting/alteration history
- Authenticity
- Provenance where consented
- Care instructions
- Product photography
- Design notes

Private client data must remain access-controlled and minimized.

---

# 21. ARCHIVE

Preserve:
- Crest masters
- Wordmark masters
- Hardware masters
- Patterns
- Technical drawings
- Fragrance formula revisions
- Packaging dielines
- Campaigns
- Photography
- Sketches
- Supplier/material records
- Golden samples
- Product stories

The archive preserves not only what an object was, but why it was created.

---

# 22. QUALITY STANDARD

Every product requires:
- Technical specification
- Approved material
- Approved hardware
- Prototype/golden sample
- Defined tolerances
- QC checklist
- Packaging specification
- Care specification
- Archive record

Reject shortcuts that are invisible in a spec sheet but visible in the hand.

---

# 23. REGULATORY GATE

Before commercial release, qualified specialists must validate applicable requirements for:
- Textile labeling and care
- Flammability
- Jewelry alloy/hallmarking and restricted substances
- Eyewear/optical safety
- Fragrance/cosmetic safety, IFRA and labeling
- Leather chemical restrictions
- Packaging/environmental requirements
- Market-specific consumer/product regulations

This catalogue is a design master, not a substitute for legal or regulatory approval.

---

# 24. FORBIDDEN DIRECTIONS

EDRIS must never become:
- Generic Persian luxury
- Persian costume
- Costume Gothic
- Medieval fantasy
- Literal steampunk
- Cyberpunk cosplay
- Military cosplay
- Generic streetwear
- Logo-covered luxury
- Fake aristocracy
- Generic quiet luxury
- Trend-chasing
- Movie/game merchandise
- Excessive skulls/gears/claws
- Cheap dark aesthetic

---

# 25. PRODUCT DEVELOPMENT RULE

A new EDRIS product is approved only if:
1. It feels EDRIS without the logo.
2. It has a meaningful relationship to the house world.
3. It has architectural discipline.
4. It respects the Iranian soul.
5. It contributes to the civilization/feral tension where appropriate.
6. It can be made to the required quality.
7. It can age into an artifact.
8. It belongs naturally in the client's life.

# 27. HOUSE NON-NEGOTIABLES

- EDRIS is the public house.
- TUX GEN is the internal bespoke engine.
- The Masked Rabbit is the house crest.
- BUILD AGAINST TIME. is the house line.
- Iran is the soul, not costume.
- 001 uses a 12 cm straight peak lapel.
- 001 uses a white formal shirt.
- Aged-silver hardware is the principal formal hardware language.
- Beauty comes first; darkness is atmosphere, not a gimmick.
- Craftsmanship must be real.
- Products must be worthy of becoming artifacts.

---

# 28. MASTER HOUSE STATEMENT

EDRIS explores the tension between civilization and the feral human through clothing, objects, fragrance, jewelry and architecture. Iranian soul is hidden within an international language of Art Deco geometry, Gothic monumentality, cinematic glamour, engineered craft and controlled darkness.

We are born feral.
We build civilization around ourselves.
We make beauty, machines, architecture and ritual to give meaning to existence.
But civilization decays.
The body returns to nature.
The object becomes an artifact.
The memory becomes a story.
The story becomes legacy.

**We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.**

---

## CATALOGUE STATUS

This catalogue is the canonical EDRIS product-design foundation. Individual technical drawings, CAD, patterns, bill of materials, manufacturing tolerances, fragrance formula percentages, regulatory files and final production samples are controlled downstream records and should be attached to the relevant product ID in TUX GEN / the EDRIS archive.

# SOURCE FILE: 007_EDRIS_House_Catalogue_v2.0.md

# EDRIS — HOUSE CATALOGUE v2.0
## A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN
### BUILD AGAINST TIME.

**Status:** Canonical working house master / product + brand + digital system
**Version:** 2.0 — October 2026
**House:** EDRIS
**Founder / Creative Director:** EDRIS NAZPARVAR
**Public house:** EDRIS
**Internal bespoke engine:** TUX GEN
**House crest:** THE MASKED RABBIT

---

# 0. WHAT CHANGED IN v2.0

This edition expands EDRIS from a product catalogue into a complete house operating system: product universe, current-trend strategy, digital identity, website architecture, app architecture, commerce, clienteling, content, AI discoverability, provenance, atelier operations, launch hierarchy and future categories.

The 2026 market signal is clear: luxury is being forced back toward craftsmanship and creativity; jewelry is unusually strong; smart eyewear is emerging; menswear is mixing formal and casual codes; slimmer silhouettes are returning; rugged luxury and utility are becoming more sophisticated; neck accessories and brooch/tie details are rising; and AI is increasingly mediating product discovery. EDRIS adopts these signals selectively rather than chasing them. The house remains timeless first, current second. citeturn0search2turn0search1turn0search0turn0search4

**EDRIS rule:** trends may change the expression, never the identity.

---

# 1. HOUSE IDENTITY

## Name
**EDRIS**

## Descriptor
**A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN**

## House line
**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**

## Core philosophy
> We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.

## Master idea
**CIVILIZATION AGAINST NATURE, CREATED IN THE FACE OF MORTALITY.**

EDRIS is an Iranian luxury house using international visual language to explore civilization, ferality, time, decay, memory, desire and legacy.

Iran is the soul, not costume.

---

# 2. PERMANENT HOUSE DNA

1. **ANCIENT SOUL — IRAN** — architecture, geometry, gardens, poetry, metalwork, textiles, landscapes, rain, mountains, deserts, cities and history translated into hidden structure.
2. **DARK ARCHITECTURE** — Art Deco as structural grammar; Gothic/ruin as mood, monumentality and mortality.
3. **CINEMATIC GLAMOUR** — Old Hollywood, Bond precision, Grand Hotel theatrical discipline, Godfather authority and obsessive film-language detail.
4. **THE FERAL HUMAN** — instinct beneath civilization; sensuality, severity, danger and nature without literal animal costume.
5. **THE MACHINE** — engineered closures, mechanical beauty, functional detail, technology vs humanity.
6. **MORTALITY** — time, ruins, memory, destiny, death, inheritance and artifacts.

## Design test
A new EDRIS object must be:
1. Beautiful at first glance.
2. Slightly strange at second glance.
3. Meaningful at third glance.
4. Recognizable without the logo.
5. Iranian in soul, never costume.
6. Architectural and materially convincing.
7. Durable enough to become an artifact.
8. Useful or emotionally necessary in the client's life.

---

# 3. 2026 TREND TRANSLATION — NOT TREND CHASING

## Current signals adopted

### A. Slimmer, more controlled menswear
FW26 reporting points toward slimmer tops, fitted waists and more controlled outerwear after years of oversized silhouettes. citeturn0search1

**EDRIS translation:** sculpted rather than skinny; strong shoulder + controlled waist + clean vertical line. Keep 001's long Hollywood body and 12 cm straight lapel. Expand fitted house blocks for shirts, knitwear and selected coats.

### B. Formality is becoming fluid
2026 menswear increasingly mixes tuxedo/formal codes with denim, loosened ties and casual elements. citeturn0search0

**EDRIS translation:** build a wardrobe where the same house language moves from tuxedo → tailored suit → knit → denim → technical outerwear without losing ceremony.

### C. Neck accessories, brooches and tie hardware
Neckties, collars, brooches and tie pins are rising as expressive menswear details. citeturn0search1turn0search6

**EDRIS translation:** make the hardware family important: aged-silver tie bars, collar bars, lapel pins and a signature EDRIS brooch derived from the Masked Rabbit geometry.

### D. Rugged luxury / sophisticated utility
2026 forecasts highlight refined utility, durable materials, field jackets, earth tones and outdoor-performance references. citeturn0search1

**EDRIS translation:** add an EDRIS field/utility capsule in forest, tobacco-black, mineral brown and oxidized olive, using tailoring-level construction rather than gorpcore or military cosplay.

### E. Craftsmanship and investment value
Luxury's recalibration is moving attention back toward creativity, quality, craftsmanship and meaningful product value. Jewelry is a particularly strong category, while resale/provenance is increasingly important. citeturn0search2

**EDRIS translation:** jewelry, hardware, repairability, provenance, archive and aftercare become central house systems, not extras.

### F. Smart eyewear + AI discovery
Smart frames are expected to become a major wearable format, while AI increasingly mediates fashion search and product discovery. citeturn0search2turn0search4

**EDRIS translation:** build EDRIS eyewear with an eventual smart-frame architecture, but preserve the visual identity. Make the website/product catalogue semantically rich and machine-readable so AI systems can understand every product, material, size, story and availability.

### G. Watch challenge
Traditional watch usage is under pressure from smartwatches, especially among younger consumers. citeturn0news9

**EDRIS translation:** do not make a generic luxury watch. Make an exceptionally legible tactical-dress mechanical watch that earns wrist time through utility, Bond versatility, craftsmanship and provenance.

---

# 4. HOUSE VISUAL SYSTEM

## Core contrasts
- BLACK × WHITE
- POLISHED × OXIDIZED
- SOFT WOOL × HARD METAL
- ANIMAL × MASK
- ANCIENT × FUTURISTIC
- IRAN × HOLLYWOOD
- BEAUTY × UNEASE
- LIFE × DEATH

## Palette
**Primary:** midnight black, black, forest black, ink.
**Secondary:** deep bottle green, midnight navy, deep burgundy.
**Expansion:** mineral brown, tobacco-black, oxidized olive, stone, wet concrete, dark umber.
**Light:** ivory, formal white.
**Metal:** aged silver, polished silver, gunmetal; antique gold only when justified.

## Material language
Fine wool, velvet, silk, cashmere, cotton, linen where appropriate, cupro, horsehair canvas, natural tailoring materials, premium leather, sterling silver, darkened metals and selected stones.

---

# 5. THE MASKED RABBIT — MASTER CREST

Front-facing, symmetrical, vertically elongated, severe, intelligent and slightly unnatural.

- Very long narrow ears.
- Straight architectural ear edges.
- Stepped inner-ear geometry.
- Tall forehead.
- Narrow head.
- Angular elongated muzzle.
- Deep empty eye openings.
- No pupils or cartoon expression.
- Ceremonial mask construction.
- Subtle Iranian geometry.
- Art Deco architectural portal rather than heraldic shield.
- Lower convergence suggesting obelisk/tomb/mortality.
- Blackened/aged sterling silver master treatment.

Official marks:
1. FULL CREST — rabbit + architectural portal.
2. MASKED RABBIT — rabbit only.
3. RABBIT SILHOUETTE — simplified mark.

No text inside the crest.

---

# 6. FORMAL CLOTHING

## 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO — LOCKED

- Midnight/forest-black luxury wool, target 280–320 gsm.
- Single-breasted, one-button.
- Approx. 79–80 cm finished length subject to fitting.
- Sculpted Hollywood shoulder.
- High armhole.
- Full canvas.
- Horsehair chest piece.
- Structured shoulder and sleeve head.
- Double-jetted pockets.
- Double vents.
- **12 cm STRAIGHT PEAK LAPEL.** The line must remain crisp and architectural, never visibly curved.
- Deep black satin facing.
- White fine formal cotton shirt.
- Formal collar.
- Concealed/clean front.
- French cuffs.
- Aged-silver studs.
- Large black architectural butterfly bow.
- High-rise flat-front trousers.
- Side adjusters; no belt loops.
- Black satin side stripe.
- White boutonniere and pocket square.
- Aged-silver buttons/cufflinks/studs.
- Hidden Iranian geometry in lining/internal construction.

**Meaning:** Old Hollywood body + Art Deco geometry + Iranian hidden construction + Gothic severity + engineered tailoring.

## 002 — THE CORLEONE
Deep forest-black wool, two-button single-breasted, 9–10 cm straight peak lapel, black silk facing, sculpted shoulder, double vents, high-rise side-adjuster trousers, white shirt, black silk tie, aged-silver cufflinks.

## 003 — THE NOIR
Midnight-black velvet dinner jacket, architectural shawl/peak geometry, black silk facing, slightly elongated body, sculpted waist, white shirt, black bow, high-rise trousers.

## 004 — THE GRAND HOTEL
Deep bottle green or deep burgundy jacket, black satin lapels, black trousers, ivory formal shirt, black bow, aged-silver hardware.

## THE EDRIS SUIT
Permanent house suit: midnight navy, forest black, charcoal, deep brown-black; two-button or restrained three-roll-two; straight architectural peak lapel; slightly extended jacket; high-rise trousers; side adjusters; natural but sculpted shoulder.

## NEW — THE AFTER-HOURS SUIT
A modern 2026 bridge between tailoring and eveningwear: slim controlled jacket, high-rise wide-to-straight trouser, black silk neck detail, polished leather shoe, optional brooch/tie bar. No streetwear styling.

## NEW — THE HOUSE TROUSER
High-rise, clean front, long leg, controlled fullness through upper leg, strong crease, slight taper. Versions: wool, velvet, cotton, technical wool and dark denim.

Heavy premium cotton, structured shoulder, clean neck, slightly elongated body, black/ivory/forest-black/washed charcoal, no visible branding or micro rabbit.

Black or ivory premium cotton, relaxed but controlled body, architectural collar, hidden placket, strong cuffs, aged-silver buttons, subtle rear pleat.

- High collar.
- Hidden aged-silver hardware.
- Optional removable liner.

Fine mercerized cotton/silk knit, close controlled fit, severe collar, three concealed buttons, small aged-silver internal button, no visible logo.

Black silk/wool scarf with hidden Iranian geometry and a narrow aged-silver/rabbit hardware point. Designed for tailoring, knitwear and outerwear.

Black leather Chelsea-inspired boot, slightly elongated toe, sculpted heel, strong side geometry, minimal elastic, small silver detail.

## RUIN BOOT
Heavy black leather, tall silhouette, architectural sole, gunmetal hardware, rugged but refined.

## EDRIS SNEAKER 001
Low-profile black leather, black sole, subtle silver hardware, architectural panel geometry, rabbit hidden on tongue/interior.

## NEW — THE DECO LOAFER
Black leather evening loafer with an architectural metal bar derived from the house hardware family. Designed for tuxedo, suit and dark denim.

## NEW — THE FIELD SHOE
Luxury black/brown service-inspired shoe with a refined welted construction and architectural sole. Durable, not military.

A quieter ring: rectangular black stone or blackened silver face, tiny hidden rabbit on inner surface.

Buttons, cufflinks, studs, tie bars, collar bars, lapel pins, brooches, buckles, bag hardware and eyewear hardware share one geometry and finish map.

# 13. HOROLOGY — THE TACTICAL DRESS WATCH PROGRAM

## DESIGN BRIEF FROM FOUNDER TASTE
The founder's reference set is **Seiko SKX007 + Rolex Submariner + Vacheron Constantin + James Bond**.

Translation:
- SKX007 = honest instrument, legibility, durability, tool-watch character.
- Submariner = iconic dive-watch discipline, black dial/bezel, robust proportions.
- Vacheron Constantin = finishing, refinement, heritage and mechanical beauty.
- Bond = tactical elegance; a watch that can live with a suit and with a field jacket.

EDRIS must not copy any reference. It should synthesize the principles into an original house object.

## EDRIS 001 — THE OPERATIVE
**Core watch.**
- 40–41 mm case.
- Slim enough for a tuxedo cuff.
- Automatic mechanical movement.
- Strong water resistance.
- Screw-down crown.
- Rotating dive-style bezel.
- Matte black dial.
- Restrained aged-silver/gunmetal indices.
- Sword/architectural hands.
- Strong lume.
- Date only if it improves utility.
- EDRIS at 12; AUTOMATIC or equivalent restrained technical text at 6.
- Brushed bracelet with polished architectural bevels and clean taper.
- Black leather, refined rubber and black NATO options.
- Caseback: small Masked Rabbit maker seal.
- Movement: excellent finishing, appropriate to price tier; decorated rotor with restrained EDRIS geometry.

**Positioning:** a tactical instrument dressed for civilization.

## EDRIS 002 — THE HOUSE
- 39–40 mm.
- Thin refined case.
- No dive bezel.
- Black or ivory dial.
- Precious-metal option.
- Hand-finished or high-grade decorated movement.
- Architectural indices.
- Slim black leather strap.
- Tuxedo/black-tie use.

## EDRIS 003 — THE FIELD
Future rugged watch: slightly tougher, black/forest dial, textile or bracelet options, field-inspired but unmistakably luxury.

## DIGITAL WATCH PASSPORT
Every EDRIS watch should have a digital identity tied to serial number, movement, service history, authenticity and ownership/provenance with privacy controls.

---

# 14. FRAGRANCE

## 001 — AFTER THE RAIN
### THE BLACK TIE SHIPYARD

Scene: midnight tuxedo, tropical/dark rainforest after heavy rain, derelict shipyard, black ocean, wet concrete, rusting machinery, vegetation reclaiming civilization, campfire, smoke, cognac, cacao, honey and old wood.

Signature accord: **CARDAMOM × VETIVER × MINERAL RAIN**.

Opening: bergamot FCF, cardamom, black pepper, cold mineral rain, dark marine/mineral accord.
Heart: clary sage, violet leaf, iris, wet green foliage/fern, oakmoss, Haitian vetiver, vetiver acetate.
Smoke: guaiac wood, frankincense, labdanum, trace birch tar/cade, patchouli, cashmeran.
Base: Haitian vetiver, cedarwood, sandalwood, Iso E Super, Ambroxan, musk, oakmoss, labdanum.
Warmth: cognac accord, cacao/dark chocolate, honey, benzoin, vanilla, tonka.

Target: dark aquatic woody amber/extrait.

Bottle: tall, heavy, architectural, black/dark translucent glass, substantial aged-silver rabbit-mask cap.

Box: near-black/forest-black exterior, hidden Iranian geometry, chamber-like opening.

## 002 — THE ARCHIVE
Dry woods, paper, iris, incense, leather, mineral facets, tobacco nuance, old-room atmosphere.

## 003 — NOCTURNE
Black pepper, cardamom, amber, dark rose, sandalwood, cacao, vanilla, leather.

## FUTURE 004 — RAIN ON STONE
Mineral rain, wet stone, petrichor impression, cypress, iris, smoke, cold woods. A more architectural, less sweet sibling to 001.

- Craft

- Objects

- Atelier notes
- Archive

Every product needs structured, semantically rich fields: category, silhouette, materials, color, dimensions, function, occasion, fit, compatibility, care, price tier, availability, story, collection, SKU, edition, origin and related products. This supports search engines and AI-mediated shopping, which is becoming increasingly important. citeturn0search2turn0search4

Never write generic keyword spam. Write real product knowledge with clear entity names, material facts, construction facts, use cases and house story.

# 20. WEBSITE VISUAL / UX SYSTEM

## Typography
- Primary: high-contrast editorial serif for major house/editorial moments.
- Secondary: restrained grotesk/sans for navigation, product data and technical information.
- Numerals should feel architectural.

## Layout
- generous negative space
- full-bleed cinematic imagery
- sharp vertical/horizontal geometry
- minimal rounded UI
- subtle transitions
- black/ivory foundation
- aged-silver used sparingly

## Interaction
- no excessive animation
- no autoplay audio
- fast performance
- tactile hover/scroll behavior
- product imagery with macro material views
- 360/AR only when useful

## Commerce
- guest checkout
- account checkout
- private client account
- appointments
- international shipping framework
- tax/duty messaging
- repair/aftercare requests
- digital passport
- waitlists
- limited-edition reservation

- versioned patterns
- timestamped measurements
- design revision history

# 29. QUALITY SYSTEM

Every product requires:
- technical specification
- approved material
- approved hardware
- prototype/golden sample
- defined tolerances
- QC checklist
- packaging specification
- care specification
- archive record

Reject shortcuts that are invisible in a spec sheet but visible in the hand.

---

# 30. REGULATORY GATE

Before commercial release, qualified specialists must validate applicable requirements for:
- textile labeling/care/flammability
- jewelry alloy, hallmarking and restricted substances
- eyewear/optical/sunglass safety
- fragrance/cosmetic safety, IFRA and labeling
- leather chemical restrictions
- packaging/environmental requirements
- market-specific consumer/product rules
- privacy/data protection
- smart-device/recording functionality if applicable

This catalogue is a design and strategy master, not a substitute for legal/regulatory approval.

# 33. HOUSE NON-NEGOTIABLES

- EDRIS is the public house.
- TUX GEN is the internal bespoke engine.
- The Masked Rabbit is the house crest.
- BUILD AGAINST TIME. is the house line.
- Iran is the soul, not costume.
- 001 uses a 12 cm straight peak lapel.
- 001 uses a white formal shirt.
- Aged-silver hardware is the principal formal hardware language.
- Beauty comes first; darkness is atmosphere, not a gimmick.
- Craftsmanship must be real.
- Products must be worthy of becoming artifacts.
- Trends are translated, never copied.
- Technology must serve the object and the client, never dominate the house.
- Privacy is a luxury and must be treated as one.

---

# 34. MASTER HOUSE STATEMENT

EDRIS explores the tension between civilization and the feral human through clothing, objects, fragrance, jewelry, eyewear, horology and architecture. Iranian soul is hidden within an international language of Art Deco geometry, Gothic monumentality, cinematic glamour, engineered craft and controlled darkness.

We are born feral.
We build civilization around ourselves.
We make beauty, machines, architecture and ritual to give meaning to existence.
But civilization decays.
The body returns to nature.
The object becomes an artifact.
The memory becomes a story.
The story becomes legacy.

**We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.**

---

# EDRIS — HOUSE CATALOGUE v2.0
## A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN
### BUILD AGAINST TIME.

**Status:** Canonical working house master / product + brand + digital system
**Version:** 2.0 — October 2026
**House:** EDRIS
**Founder / Creative Director:** EDRIS NAZPARVAR
**Public house:** EDRIS
**Internal bespoke engine:** TUX GEN
**House crest:** THE MASKED RABBIT

---

# 0. WHAT CHANGED IN v2.0

This edition expands EDRIS from a product catalogue into a complete house operating system: product universe, current-trend strategy, digital identity, website architecture, app architecture, commerce, clienteling, content, AI discoverability, provenance, atelier operations, launch hierarchy and future categories.

The 2026 market signal is clear: luxury is being forced back toward craftsmanship and creativity; jewelry is unusually strong; smart eyewear is emerging; menswear is mixing formal and casual codes; slimmer silhouettes are returning; rugged luxury and utility are becoming more sophisticated; neck accessories and brooch/tie details are rising; and AI is increasingly mediating product discovery. EDRIS adopts these signals selectively rather than chasing them. The house remains timeless first, current second. citeturn0search2turn0search1turn0search0turn0search4

**EDRIS rule:** trends may change the expression, never the identity.

---

# 1. HOUSE IDENTITY

## Name
**EDRIS**

## Descriptor
**A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN**

## House line
**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**

## Core philosophy
> We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.

## Master idea
**CIVILIZATION AGAINST NATURE, CREATED IN THE FACE OF MORTALITY.**

EDRIS is an Iranian luxury house using international visual language to explore civilization, ferality, time, decay, memory, desire and legacy.

Iran is the soul, not costume.

---

# 2. PERMANENT HOUSE DNA

1. **ANCIENT SOUL — IRAN** — architecture, geometry, gardens, poetry, metalwork, textiles, landscapes, rain, mountains, deserts, cities and history translated into hidden structure.
2. **DARK ARCHITECTURE** — Art Deco as structural grammar; Gothic/ruin as mood, monumentality and mortality.
3. **CINEMATIC GLAMOUR** — Old Hollywood, Bond precision, Grand Hotel theatrical discipline, Godfather authority and obsessive film-language detail.
4. **THE FERAL HUMAN** — instinct beneath civilization; sensuality, severity, danger and nature without literal animal costume.
5. **THE MACHINE** — engineered closures, mechanical beauty, functional detail, technology vs humanity.
6. **MORTALITY** — time, ruins, memory, destiny, death, inheritance and artifacts.

## Design test
A new EDRIS object must be:
1. Beautiful at first glance.
2. Slightly strange at second glance.
3. Meaningful at third glance.
4. Recognizable without the logo.
5. Iranian in soul, never costume.
6. Architectural and materially convincing.
7. Durable enough to become an artifact.
8. Useful or emotionally necessary in the client's life.

---

# 3. 2026 TREND TRANSLATION — NOT TREND CHASING

## Current signals adopted

### A. Slimmer, more controlled menswear
FW26 reporting points toward slimmer tops, fitted waists and more controlled outerwear after years of oversized silhouettes. citeturn0search1

**EDRIS translation:** sculpted rather than skinny; strong shoulder + controlled waist + clean vertical line. Keep 001's long Hollywood body and 12 cm straight lapel. Expand fitted house blocks for shirts, knitwear and selected coats.

### B. Formality is becoming fluid
2026 menswear increasingly mixes tuxedo/formal codes with denim, loosened ties and casual elements. citeturn0search0

**EDRIS translation:** build a wardrobe where the same house language moves from tuxedo → tailored suit → knit → denim → technical outerwear without losing ceremony.

### C. Neck accessories, brooches and tie hardware
Neckties, collars, brooches and tie pins are rising as expressive menswear details. citeturn0search1turn0search6

**EDRIS translation:** make the hardware family important: aged-silver tie bars, collar bars, lapel pins and a signature EDRIS brooch derived from the Masked Rabbit geometry.

### D. Rugged luxury / sophisticated utility
2026 forecasts highlight refined utility, durable materials, field jackets, earth tones and outdoor-performance references. citeturn0search1

**EDRIS translation:** add an EDRIS field/utility capsule in forest, tobacco-black, mineral brown and oxidized olive, using tailoring-level construction rather than gorpcore or military cosplay.

### E. Craftsmanship and investment value
Luxury's recalibration is moving attention back toward creativity, quality, craftsmanship and meaningful product value. Jewelry is a particularly strong category, while resale/provenance is increasingly important. citeturn0search2

**EDRIS translation:** jewelry, hardware, repairability, provenance, archive and aftercare become central house systems, not extras.

### F. Smart eyewear + AI discovery
Smart frames are expected to become a major wearable format, while AI increasingly mediates fashion search and product discovery. citeturn0search2turn0search4

**EDRIS translation:** build EDRIS eyewear with an eventual smart-frame architecture, but preserve the visual identity. Make the website/product catalogue semantically rich and machine-readable so AI systems can understand every product, material, size, story and availability.

### G. Watch challenge
Traditional watch usage is under pressure from smartwatches, especially among younger consumers. citeturn0news9

**EDRIS translation:** do not make a generic luxury watch. Make an exceptionally legible tactical-dress mechanical watch that earns wrist time through utility, Bond versatility, craftsmanship and provenance.

---

# 4. HOUSE VISUAL SYSTEM

## Core contrasts
- BLACK × WHITE
- POLISHED × OXIDIZED
- SOFT WOOL × HARD METAL
- ANIMAL × MASK
- ANCIENT × FUTURISTIC
- IRAN × HOLLYWOOD
- BEAUTY × UNEASE
- LIFE × DEATH

## Palette
**Primary:** midnight black, black, forest black, ink.
**Secondary:** deep bottle green, midnight navy, deep burgundy.
**Expansion:** mineral brown, tobacco-black, oxidized olive, stone, wet concrete, dark umber.
**Light:** ivory, formal white.
**Metal:** aged silver, polished silver, gunmetal; antique gold only when justified.

## Material language
Fine wool, velvet, silk, cashmere, cotton, linen where appropriate, cupro, horsehair canvas, natural tailoring materials, premium leather, sterling silver, darkened metals and selected stones.

---

# 5. THE MASKED RABBIT — MASTER CREST

Front-facing, symmetrical, vertically elongated, severe, intelligent and slightly unnatural.

- Very long narrow ears.
- Straight architectural ear edges.
- Stepped inner-ear geometry.
- Tall forehead.
- Narrow head.
- Angular elongated muzzle.
- Deep empty eye openings.
- No pupils or cartoon expression.
- Ceremonial mask construction.
- Subtle Iranian geometry.
- Art Deco architectural portal rather than heraldic shield.
- Lower convergence suggesting obelisk/tomb/mortality.
- Blackened/aged sterling silver master treatment.

Official marks:
1. FULL CREST — rabbit + architectural portal.
2. MASKED RABBIT — rabbit only.
3. RABBIT SILHOUETTE — simplified mark.

No text inside the crest.

---

# 6. FORMAL CLOTHING

## 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO — LOCKED

- Midnight/forest-black luxury wool, target 280–320 gsm.
- Single-breasted, one-button.
- Approx. 79–80 cm finished length subject to fitting.
- Sculpted Hollywood shoulder.
- High armhole.
- Full canvas.
- Horsehair chest piece.
- Structured shoulder and sleeve head.
- Double-jetted pockets.
- Double vents.
- **12 cm STRAIGHT PEAK LAPEL.** The line must remain crisp and architectural, never visibly curved.
- Deep black satin facing.
- White fine formal cotton shirt.
- Formal collar.
- Concealed/clean front.
- French cuffs.
- Aged-silver studs.
- Large black architectural butterfly bow.
- High-rise flat-front trousers.
- Side adjusters; no belt loops.
- Black satin side stripe.
- White boutonniere and pocket square.
- Aged-silver buttons/cufflinks/studs.
- Hidden Iranian geometry in lining/internal construction.

**Meaning:** Old Hollywood body + Art Deco geometry + Iranian hidden construction + Gothic severity + engineered tailoring.

## 002 — THE PATRON
Deep forest-black wool, two-button single-breasted, 9–10 cm straight peak lapel, black silk facing, sculpted shoulder, double vents, high-rise side-adjuster trousers, white shirt, black silk tie, aged-silver cufflinks.

## 003 — THE NOCTURNE
Midnight-black velvet dinner jacket, architectural shawl/peak geometry, black silk facing, slightly elongated body, sculpted waist, white shirt, black bow, high-rise trousers.

## 004 — THE SALON
Deep bottle green or deep burgundy jacket, black satin lapels, black trousers, ivory formal shirt, black bow, aged-silver hardware.

## THE STANDARD
Permanent house suit: midnight navy, forest black, charcoal, deep brown-black; two-button or restrained three-roll-two; straight architectural peak lapel; slightly extended jacket; high-rise trousers; side adjusters; natural but sculpted shoulder.

## NEW — THE VELVET HOUR
A modern 2026 bridge between tailoring and eveningwear: slim controlled jacket, high-rise wide-to-straight trouser, black silk neck detail, polished leather shoe, optional brooch/tie bar. No streetwear styling.

## NEW — THE COLUMN TROUSER
High-rise, clean front, long leg, controlled fullness through upper leg, strong crease, slight taper. Versions: wool, velvet, cotton, technical wool and dark denim.

A quieter ring: rectangular black stone or blackened silver face, tiny hidden rabbit on inner surface.

Buttons, cufflinks, studs, tie bars, collar bars, lapel pins, brooches, buckles, bag hardware and eyewear hardware share one geometry and finish map.

# 13. HOROLOGY — THE TACTICAL DRESS WATCH PROGRAM

## DESIGN BRIEF FROM FOUNDER TASTE
The founder's reference set is **Seiko SKX007 + Rolex Submariner + Vacheron Constantin + James Bond**.

Translation:
- SKX007 = honest instrument, legibility, durability, tool-watch character.
- Submariner = iconic dive-watch discipline, black dial/bezel, robust proportions.
- Vacheron Constantin = finishing, refinement, heritage and mechanical beauty.
- Bond = tactical elegance; a watch that can live with a suit and with a field jacket.

EDRIS must not copy any reference. It should synthesize the principles into an original house object.

## EDRIS 001 — THE OPERATIVE
**Core watch.**
- 40–41 mm case.
- Slim enough for a tuxedo cuff.
- Automatic mechanical movement.
- Strong water resistance.
- Screw-down crown.
- Rotating dive-style bezel.
- Matte black dial.
- Restrained aged-silver/gunmetal indices.
- Sword/architectural hands.
- Strong lume.
- Date only if it improves utility.
- EDRIS at 12; AUTOMATIC or equivalent restrained technical text at 6.
- Brushed bracelet with polished architectural bevels and clean taper.
- Black leather, refined rubber and black NATO options.
- Caseback: small Masked Rabbit maker seal.
- Movement: excellent finishing, appropriate to price tier; decorated rotor with restrained EDRIS geometry.

**Positioning:** a tactical instrument dressed for civilization.

## EDRIS 002 — THE HOUSE
- 39–40 mm.
- Thin refined case.
- No dive bezel.
- Black or ivory dial.
- Precious-metal option.
- Hand-finished or high-grade decorated movement.
- Architectural indices.
- Slim black leather strap.
- Tuxedo/black-tie use.

## EDRIS 003 — THE FIELD
Future rugged watch: slightly tougher, black/forest dial, textile or bracelet options, field-inspired but unmistakably luxury.

## DIGITAL WATCH PASSPORT
Every EDRIS watch should have a digital identity tied to serial number, movement, service history, authenticity and ownership/provenance with privacy controls.

---

# 14. FRAGRANCE

## 001 — AFTER THE RAIN
### THE BLACK TIE SHIPYARD

Scene: midnight tuxedo, tropical/dark rainforest after heavy rain, derelict shipyard, black ocean, wet concrete, rusting machinery, vegetation reclaiming civilization, campfire, smoke, cognac, cacao, honey and old wood.

Signature accord: **CARDAMOM × VETIVER × MINERAL RAIN**.

Opening: bergamot FCF, cardamom, black pepper, cold mineral rain, dark marine/mineral accord.
Heart: clary sage, violet leaf, iris, wet green foliage/fern, oakmoss, Haitian vetiver, vetiver acetate.
Smoke: guaiac wood, frankincense, labdanum, trace birch tar/cade, patchouli, cashmeran.
Base: Haitian vetiver, cedarwood, sandalwood, Iso E Super, Ambroxan, musk, oakmoss, labdanum.
Warmth: cognac accord, cacao/dark chocolate, honey, benzoin, vanilla, tonka.

Target: dark aquatic woody amber/extrait.

Bottle: tall, heavy, architectural, black/dark translucent glass, substantial aged-silver rabbit-mask cap.

Box: near-black/forest-black exterior, hidden Iranian geometry, chamber-like opening.

## 002 — THE ARCHIVE
Dry woods, paper, iris, incense, leather, mineral facets, tobacco nuance, old-room atmosphere.

## 003 — NOCTURNE
Black pepper, cardamom, amber, dark rose, sandalwood, cacao, vanilla, leather.

## FUTURE 004 — RAIN ON STONE
Mineral rain, wet stone, petrichor impression, cypress, iris, smoke, cold woods. A more architectural, less sweet sibling to 001.

# 18. PACKAGING SYSTEM

Universal language:
- near-black exterior
- restrained aged-silver details
- generous typography
- blind/debossed rabbit
- architectural reveal
- hidden Iranian geometry inside
- category-specific protection
- provenance/care information without clutter

Packaging should feel like opening an artifact chamber.

## Unboxing sequence
1. House mark.
2. Material/texture.
3. Mechanical or architectural reveal.
4. Object.
5. Provenance card.
6. Care/aftercare.
7. Digital passport activation.

# 19. WEBSITE — EDRIS.COM EXPERIENCE BLUEPRINT

## Principle
The website is not a normal luxury webshop. It is the digital entrance to the EDRIS world.

## Primary navigation
**HOUSE | COLLECTIONS | BESPOKE | OBJECTS | FRAGRANCE | JEWELRY | WATCHES | EYEWEAR | JOURNAL | ATELIER | ARCHIVE**

## Homepage
Hero: cinematic EDRIS image/video with minimal type.

Primary message:
**EDRIS**
*A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN*
**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**

Then:
- current collection
- 001 story
- Masked Rabbit
- atelier/bespoke invitation
- fragrance world
- jewelry/object spotlight
- journal/film
- archive/provenance

## Product page structure
1. Product name.
2. Hero visual.
3. Short emotional description.
4. Technical specifications.
5. Materials.
6. Construction/craft.
7. Fit and sizing.
8. Styling system.
9. Story/meaning.
10. Availability/edition number.
11. Digital passport/provenance.
12. Care/aftercare.
13. Related EDRIS objects.
14. Appointment / purchase.

## Bespoke flow
**Discover → Book → Measure → Design → Material → Fitting → Finalize**

No public client data. TUX GEN is accessed through authenticated client/atelier workflows.

## Journal
Sections:
- House
- Craft
- Iran
- Objects
- Film
- Fragrance
- Horology
- Atelier notes
- Archive

## AI-ready commerce
Every product needs structured, semantically rich fields: category, silhouette, materials, color, dimensions, function, occasion, fit, compatibility, care, price tier, availability, story, collection, SKU, edition, origin and related products. This supports search engines and AI-mediated shopping, which is becoming increasingly important. citeturn0search2turn0search4

## SEO/content rules
Never write generic keyword spam. Write real product knowledge with clear entity names, material facts, construction facts, use cases and house story.

# 20. WEBSITE VISUAL / UX SYSTEM

## Typography
- Primary: high-contrast editorial serif for major house/editorial moments.
- Secondary: restrained grotesk/sans for navigation, product data and technical information.
- Numerals should feel architectural.

## Layout
- generous negative space
- full-bleed cinematic imagery
- sharp vertical/horizontal geometry
- minimal rounded UI
- subtle transitions
- black/ivory foundation
- aged-silver used sparingly

## Interaction
- no excessive animation
- no autoplay audio
- fast performance
- tactile hover/scroll behavior
- product imagery with macro material views
- 360/AR only when useful

## Commerce
- guest checkout
- account checkout
- private client account
- appointments
- international shipping framework
- tax/duty messaging
- repair/aftercare requests
- digital passport
- waitlists
- limited-edition reservation

# 21. EDRIS APP — PRODUCT BLUEPRINT

Working name: **EDRIS — THE HOUSE**

## Bottom navigation
**HOUSE | SHOP | PASSPORT | ATELIER | ARCHIVE**

## HOUSE
Editorial home: current campaign, collection, films, fragrance, house stories.

## SHOP
Personalized but controlled product discovery. Filters: category, material, color, fit, occasion, collection.

## PASSPORT
User's authenticated EDRIS objects:
- garments
- watches
- jewelry
- fragrance
- leather goods
- provenance
- care
- service
- alterations
- repairs
- certificates

## ATELIER
- Book consultation.
- Measurement appointment.
- Bespoke project status.
- Fitting schedule.
- Design approvals.
- Messages with atelier.
- Care/repair request.

## ARCHIVE
Personal and public archive layers. Owners can see their pieces; public archive shows approved historical objects/campaigns.

## App personalization
Use first-party preference data, not surveillance. Client controls personalization. Privacy should feel like part of luxury, especially as 2026 brands face increasing tension between personalization and data collection. citeturn0search5

## Notifications
Only meaningful events:
- fitting ready
- alteration ready
- repair completed
- limited piece available
- appointment reminder
- passport update
- collection launch

No spam or artificial urgency.

# 22. TUX GEN — INTERNAL BESPOKE + ATELIER PLATFORM

Modules:
- client profile
- measurement/body scan data
- design concepts
- garment configuration
- material library
- hardware library
- pattern records
- fitting records
- alterations
- production workflow
- QC
- digital passport
- provenance
- archive

## Data principles
- unique garment ID
- versioned patterns
- timestamped measurements
- design revision history
- access control
- minimum necessary personal data
- production data separated from unnecessary personal data
- immutable historical records with documented corrections/versioning

## Bespoke workflow
Consultation → measurement → design brief → material selection → configuration → pattern → toile/prototype → fitting → correction → final construction → QC → delivery → passport → archive → aftercare.

# 23. DIGITAL PASSPORT / PROVENANCE

Each significant object can record:
- piece ID
- category
- collection
- creation date
- materials
- hardware
- construction notes
- pattern/version
- craftsperson/atelier where appropriate
- fitting/alteration history
- authenticity
- provenance where consented
- care instructions
- photography
- design notes
- service history

For watches: serial, movement, service, parts, authentication.
For jewelry: metal/alloy, stone information, weight where appropriate, workshop/finish.
For tailoring: original pattern, cloth, fittings and alterations.

Privacy is mandatory.

# 24. CLIENT EXPERIENCE / CRM

## Client tiers should be relationship-based, not status theatre
- House client
- Bespoke client
- Collector
- Archive/one-of-one client

## Client record
Preferences, sizes, prior purchases, fitting history, style preferences and explicit communications consent.

## Clienteling
A stylist/atelier member can prepare a private edit before appointment: garments, watch, jewelry, fragrance and accessories that naturally work together.

## No manipulative luxury tactics
No fake scarcity, fake countdowns, fabricated waitlists or artificial exclusivity.

4. **THE ARCHIVE** — history, references, prototypes, failures.
5. **THE WORLD** — rain, architecture, ruins, Tehran/Iranian landscapes, cinema-like environments.
6. **THE CLIENT** — finished looks and private-client stories with consent.

- tactile close-ups
- architecture
- negative space
- black retains texture
- no generic influencer grid
- no logo repetition
- no trend memes as core identity

Silence → object → human → movement → detail → reveal → house line.

**BESPOKE** — individualized through TUX GEN and atelier craft.

**READY-TO-WEAR** — scalable house blocks.
**LIMITED EDITION** — fixed quantity, individually numbered.
**CORE** — permanent house products.

---

# 28. RETAIL / ATELIER

Physical environment:
- black stone
- dark wood
- blackened metal
- selective greenery
- warm controlled light
- Iranian symmetry interpreted through Art Deco
- monumental scale without theme-park décor

Client journey:
**arrival → consultation → material discovery → design → fitting → reveal → packaging → aftercare → archive**

The atelier is the physical heart of EDRIS.

---

# 29. QUALITY SYSTEM

Every product requires:
- technical specification
- approved material
- approved hardware
- prototype/golden sample
- defined tolerances
- QC checklist
- packaging specification
- care specification
- archive record

Reject shortcuts that are invisible in a spec sheet but visible in the hand.

---

# 30. REGULATORY GATE

Before commercial release, qualified specialists must validate applicable requirements for:
- textile labeling/care/flammability
- jewelry alloy, hallmarking and restricted substances
- eyewear/optical/sunglass safety
- fragrance/cosmetic safety, IFRA and labeling
- leather chemical restrictions
- packaging/environmental requirements
- market-specific consumer/product rules
- privacy/data protection
- smart-device/recording functionality if applicable

This catalogue is a design and strategy master, not a substitute for legal/regulatory approval.

---

# 31. FORBIDDEN DIRECTIONS

EDRIS must never become:
- generic Persian luxury
- Persian costume
- costume Gothic
- medieval fantasy
- literal steampunk
- cyberpunk cosplay
- military cosplay
- generic streetwear
- logo-covered luxury
- fake aristocracy
- generic quiet luxury
- trend-chasing
- movie/game merchandise
- excessive skulls/gears/claws
- cheap dark aesthetic
- superficial AI gimmicks
- surveillance-heavy personalization

---

# 32. PRODUCT NAMING SYSTEM

EDRIS product names are permanent design identities, not generic SKU descriptions. Names draw from architecture, ritual, civilization, geography, time, objects and atmosphere.

Naming rules:
- Avoid generic category names as the primary identity.
- Avoid direct film, character or competitor references.
- Prefer one strong noun or a concise two-word construction.
- Numbers identify lineage; the name carries the soul.
- Names must work internationally and remain compatible with the Iranian foundation.
- Names must sound natural when spoken, engraved, photographed or archived.

The names below are the canonical EDRIS product names.

---

001 RAIN AFTER DARK; 002 THE ARCHIVE; 003 THE NOCTURNE; future 004 RAIN ON STONE.

### HOROLOGY
001 THE SENTINEL; 002 THE ATELIER; 003 THE OUTPOST.

THE OBSERVATORY LAMP; THE ALTAR TRAY; THE RELIQUARY; CANDLE 001 THE WET STONE; future incense/glass/bar/desk objects.

future body, hair, beard and aftershave ritual.

# 33. HOUSE NON-NEGOTIABLES

- EDRIS is the public house.
- TUX GEN is the internal bespoke engine.
- The Masked Rabbit is the house crest.
- BUILD AGAINST TIME. is the house line.
- Iran is the soul, not costume.
- 001 uses a 12 cm straight peak lapel.
- 001 uses a white formal shirt.
- Aged-silver hardware is the principal formal hardware language.
- Beauty comes first; darkness is atmosphere, not a gimmick.
- Craftsmanship must be real.
- Products must be worthy of becoming artifacts.
- Trends are translated, never copied.
- Technology must serve the object and the client, never dominate the house.
- Privacy is a luxury and must be treated as one.

---

# 34. MASTER HOUSE STATEMENT

EDRIS explores the tension between civilization and the feral human through clothing, objects, fragrance, jewelry, eyewear, horology and architecture. Iranian soul is hidden within an international language of Art Deco geometry, Gothic monumentality, cinematic glamour, engineered craft and controlled darkness.

We are born feral.
We build civilization around ourselves.
We make beauty, machines, architecture and ritual to give meaning to existence.
But civilization decays.
The body returns to nature.
The object becomes an artifact.
The memory becomes a story.
The story becomes legacy.

**We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.**

---

**Version:** 3.0 — October 2026

# 0. WHAT CHANGED IN v2.0

This edition expands EDRIS from a product catalogue into a complete house operating system: product universe, current-trend strategy, digital identity, website architecture, app architecture, commerce, clienteling, content, AI discoverability, provenance, atelier operations, launch hierarchy and future categories.

The 2026 market signal is clear: luxury is being forced back toward craftsmanship and creativity; jewelry is unusually strong; smart eyewear is emerging; menswear is mixing formal and casual codes; slimmer silhouettes are returning; rugged luxury and utility are becoming more sophisticated; neck accessories and brooch/tie details are rising; and AI is increasingly mediating product discovery. EDRIS adopts these signals selectively rather than chasing them. The house remains timeless first, current second. citeturn0search2turn0search1turn0search0turn0search4

**EDRIS rule:** trends may change the expression, never the identity.

## CANONICALIZATION NOTE — v3.0
This edition consolidates the prior EDRIS / Uncle Ed’s work into one house master. Earlier working names that conflicted with locked founder decisions have been normalized here. In particular: **001 is THE ONE OF ONE TUXEDO**; the 001 watch is **THE OPERATIVE**; the 001 fragrance is **AFTER THE RAIN**. **EDRIS** is the public house and **TUX GEN** is the internal bespoke/atelier system, not a public sub-brand. The 12 cm straight peak lapel, white formal shirt and aged-silver formal hardware remain locked for 001.

This master supersedes earlier catalogue drafts for house-level naming and product-universe decisions. Technical drawings, patterns, BOMs, supplier records, formulas, tolerances, regulatory files and final production approvals remain downstream controlled records.
