A tuxedo leads to a shirt.  
A shirt leads to a suit.  
A suit leads to a watch.  
A watch leads to a leather object.  
A leather object leads to travel.  
Travel leads to fragrance.  
Fragrance leads to memory.  
Memory becomes archive.  
Archive becomes legacy.

**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**


---

# SOURCE FILE: 013_EDRIS_House_Execution_Master_v5.0.md

# EDRIS HOUSE — EXECUTION MASTER v5.0
## From Designed House to Executable House
**House:** EDRIS  
**Internal atelier engine:** TUX GEN  
**Crest:** THE MASKED RABBIT  
**Motto:** BUILD AGAINST TIME.  
**Status:** Canonical execution layer following Ecosystem Master v4.2

---

# 1. EXECUTIVE DECISION

The EDRIS ecosystem is conceptually complete. v5.0 converts that completed concept into an executable operating system.

The house is governed by five gates:

**DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE**

No product moves to commercial release without passing all five.

The house principle remains:

> EDRIS sells a world, not a pile of products.

---

# 2. FINAL ECOSYSTEM AUDIT

## 2.1 Result

**HOUSE STATUS: COMPLETE.**

No new major category is required. The remaining work is specification, sampling, costing, supplier development, legal/regulatory preparation, digital implementation, launch sequencing and archival discipline.

## 2.2 Deliberate exclusions / holds

| Product | Decision | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| THE VELVET HOUR | Deleted | Ideas absorbed into NOCTURNE and SALON |
| COLLAR BAR | Merged | Becomes AXIS system |
| THE OUTPOST | Hold | Must earn a role distinct from RELIC |
| THE ORACLE | Hold | Smart eyewear only if design remains EDRIS without electronics |
| THE FIELD | Hold | Future rugged watch; must not duplicate OPERATIVE |
| RAIN ON STONE | Conditional | Only if materially colder/austerer than AFTER THE RAIN |
| GROOMING | Hold | Launch after fragrance world is established |
| THE BAR | Future | Useful hospitality object, not filler |
| THE DESK | Future | Useful writing/work object, not filler |

## 2.3 Overlap rules

1. One product = one primary job.
2. A new SKU must add a new function, silhouette or emotional world.
3. If a new product merely changes color/material, it is a variant—not a new house product.
4. If two products solve the same problem, keep the stronger one.
5. LE products may be more expressive, but may not violate house DNA.
6. Future products remain outside launch until they pass the same audit.

---

# 3. MASTER PRODUCT RECORD

Every EDRIS product receives one canonical Product Record.

### Required fields
- Product ID
- Product name
- Category
- Family
- Tier: O1 / B / MTM / RTW / LE / CORE
- Primary customer job
- Emotional proposition
- Silhouette / form
- Materials
- Hardware
- Colorways
- Size range
- Fit block
- Construction method
- Key measurements
- Tolerances
- Supplier / maker
- Prototype status
- Golden sample status
- QC checklist
- Packaging
- Care
- Repairability
- Provenance fields
- Photography requirements
- Product story
- Recommended products
- Next Object
- Launch movement
- Cost target
- Wholesale target if applicable
- Retail target
- Margin target
- Regulatory / labeling requirements
- Archive status

### Product release statuses
**CONCEPT → DESIGNED → ENGINEERED → PROTOTYPE → FIT APPROVED → GOLDEN SAMPLE → COSTED → QC APPROVED → CONTENT READY → RELEASED → ARCHIVED**

---

# 4. TECHNICAL PRODUCT FAMILY STANDARD

## 4.1 Tailoring

Every jacket record must specify:
- body length
- chest
- waist
- seat
- shoulder width
- back width
- armhole depth
- sleeve length
- biceps
- elbow
- cuff
- lapel width
- lapel break position
- gorge height
- button position
- pocket position
- vent length
- canvas specification
- chest piece
- shoulder construction
- sleeve head
- lining
- seam allowances
- pressing map
- alteration limits

Every trouser record must specify:
- waist
- front rise
- back rise
- seat
- thigh
- knee
- hem
- inseam
- crease position
- waistband construction
- closure
- side adjuster position
- pocket construction
- satin stripe where applicable

## 4.2 Shirts
Specify collar geometry, collar stand, chest, waist, back pleat, cuff, placket, button/stud system, sleeve pitch, hem and shrinkage tolerance.

## 4.3 Outerwear
Specify shell, membrane or weather resistance, seam sealing, insulation/liner, closure system, pocket architecture, hood, cuff, hem, hardware and repair strategy.

## 4.4 Footwear
Specify last, toe shape, heel, sole, welt, upper, lining, insole, shank, hardware, construction method, flex point, outsole compound and resoling plan.

## 4.5 Leather
Specify leather species/type, tannage, thickness, reinforcement, edge paint/burnish, stitching, hardware, lining, compartments, closure cycles and repairability.

## 4.6 Jewelry / hardware
Specify metal alloy, dimensions, weight, finish, oxidation treatment, polish zones, engraving/geometry, stone policy if any, clasp/hinge cycles and surface tolerance.

## 4.7 Watches
Specify case dimensions, lug geometry, crown, water resistance target, crystal, dial, hands, movement, bracelet/strap, clasp, service interval and originality review.

## 4.8 Fragrance
Specify formula brief, concentration, stability testing, bottle dimensions, closure, pump, compatibility, packaging, batch code, safety and labeling requirements.

---

# 5. EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO: TECHNICAL MASTER

## Locked identity
- Midnight / forest-black luxury wool
- Single-breasted
- One button
- Long Hollywood body
- Sculpted Hollywood shoulder
- High armhole
- Full canvas
- Horsehair chest
- Structured shoulder and sleeve head
- Double-jetted pockets
- Double vents
- **12 cm straight peak lapel — maximum**
- Deep black satin facing
- White fine formal cotton shirt
- Formal collar
- French cuffs
- Concealed / clean front
- Aged-silver shirt studs
- Large architectural black butterfly bow
- High-rise flat-front trouser
- Side adjusters
- No belt loops
- Black satin side stripe
- White boutonniere
- White pocket square
- Aged-silver jacket button and cufflinks
- Hidden Iranian geometry

## User-provided body measurements — preserved
1. 49 cm  
2. 54 cm  
3. 30 cm  
4. 56 cm  
5. 105 cm  
6. 73 cm  
7. 35 cm  
8. 18 cm  
9. 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist

**Important:** these raw measurements must be reconciled against the tailor's measurement chart before cutting. They are not automatically finished-garment measurements.

## Lapel engineering
The lapel must read as an architectural line, not a soft curve.

Rules:
- 12 cm finished width at the widest controlled point.
- Straight outer edge from break toward peak.
- Straight/controlled inner edge.
- Peak tip sharp and symmetrical.
- No decorative belly.
- No exaggerated roll that visually curves the line.
- Gorge and break must be aligned to preserve the long Hollywood proportion.
- Satin facing follows the architectural geometry cleanly.

## Jacket construction order
1. Verify paper pattern.
2. Cut shell, canvas, chest piece and lining.
3. Mark grain, balance lines and pocket placements.
4. Build front canvas and chest.
5. Shape shoulder and chest by hand pressing.
6. Form lapel roll without creating a visibly curved outer edge.
7. Construct collar and gorge.
8. Set sleeves at high armhole with controlled pitch.
9. Construct double-jetted pockets.
10. Construct double vents.
11. Close body and lining.
12. Hand finish buttonhole and button.
13. Apply aged-silver hardware.
14. Final press.
15. Fit on body.
16. Record alterations.
17. Produce golden sample record.

## 001 QC gate
- Lapels: 12 cm ± approved production tolerance.
- Left/right peak symmetry.
- Break height symmetry.
- Shoulder balance.
- Collar clean against neck.
- Sleeve pitch natural at rest.
- No pulling across chest.
- No collapse at armhole.
- Pocket jets parallel.
- Vents equal.
- Trouser crease centered.
- Satin stripe uninterrupted.
- Shirt studs aligned.
- Silver hardware finish consistent.
- White shirt remains deliberately crisp against black tuxedo.

---

# 6. RECOMMENDATION ENGINE — MASTER LOGIC

Every product page contains exactly four recommendation roles:

### WEAR WITH
The garments that make the primary look work.

### COMPLETE THE OBJECT
Accessories, jewelry, footwear or supporting objects that complete the purchase.

### THE EDRIS WORLD
Conceptually related products, not necessarily commercially adjacent.

### NEXT OBJECT
Exactly one strongest next purchase.

## Ranking logic
1. Functional necessity
2. Visual compatibility
3. Same house language
4. Same use occasion
5. Family continuity
6. Customer stage
7. Purchase diversity
8. No forced upsell

## Recommendation exclusions
Do not recommend:
- unrelated categories merely to increase basket size
- multiple near-identical alternatives
- products unavailable in the client's size/region
- products that contradict the outfit
- future/held products as if they are purchasable

## Family graph

**001 CEREMONY:** 001 → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → STUDS → CUFFLINKS → MONUMENT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN → VAULT

**COLUMN HOUSE SUIT:** COLUMN → STRUCTURE SHIRT → NEEDLE → SEAL → MONUMENT → DOSSIER → OPERATIVE

**PATRON AUTHORITY:** PATRON → STRUCTURE SHIRT → CREST → DECO → DOSSIER → HOUSE → ARCHIVE

**NOCTURNE NIGHT:** NOCTURNE → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → SEAL → DECO → MIDNIGHT → NOCTURNE FRAGRANCE

**RUIN WINTER:** RUIN → HEIRLOOM KNIT → COLUMN TROUSER → SPIRE → VAULT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN

**RAIN WEATHER:** RAIN → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → VOYAGE → AFTER THE RAIN

**WILD FERAL:** WILD → FOUNDATION TEE → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → LINK → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN

**FRONTIER TRAVEL:** FRONTIER → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RAIN → RELIC → VOYAGE → FIELD

**ARTIFACT:** RABBIT → SEAL → VAULT → RELIQUARY → ARCHIVE → OPERATIVE

---

# 7. VISUAL DESIGN SYSTEM

## 7.1 Wordmark
EDRIS wordmark must be restrained, architectural and legible. No decorative Persian imitation. No permanent dependency on the crest.

## 7.2 Masked Rabbit
Use as a house signature, not wallpaper.

Primary forms:
- blind/debossed
- cast metal
- engraved
- embroidered only when materially justified
- architectural negative space

Never use as a cartoon mascot.

## 7.3 Typography
System should use:
- one high-contrast editorial serif or equivalent for ceremonial/editorial expression
- one neutral architectural sans for navigation, product data and utility
- one optional monospaced/technical face for provenance and atelier records

Typography must feel cinematic and engineered rather than futuristic.

## 7.4 Photography
Six recurring visual worlds:
- ceremony
- architecture
- weather
- wilderness
- machine/workshop
- archive/memory

Rules:
- human presence over sterile product-only imagery
- controlled shadow
- tactile material detail
- no excessive black crush
- no generic Persian props
- no fake aristocratic mansion clichés
- no AI-generated people presented as real campaigns

## 7.5 Color architecture
Core: black, forest-black, ivory/white, mineral grey, aged silver.
Secondary: bottle green, black-burgundy, mineral brown.
No rainbow seasonal identity.

## 7.6 Hardware
Master geometry:
**narrow vertical incision crossing a framed/stepped architectural form.**

The geometry should be recognizable across a button, clasp, ring, watch crown and fragrance cap without literally repeating the same object.

---

# 8. PACKAGING EXECUTION STANDARD

Universal sequence:
**OUTER SHELL → REVEAL → OBJECT CHAMBER → PROVENANCE → CARE → DIGITAL PASSPORT**

Materials:
- near-black board
- black textile
- aged/blackened metal
- blind/debossed marks
- concealed geometry

Packaging QC:
- closure cycle test
- abrasion test
- transit protection
- humidity considerations
- hardware tarnish control
- correct provenance record
- correct care insert
- correct product identity

Packaging must feel like opening an EDRIS object, not opening advertising.

---

# 9. TUX GEN — OPERATING SYSTEM

## Client record
- client ID
- consent / privacy status
- body measurements
- posture observations
- fit preferences
- style preferences
- previous garments
- alterations
- material history
- hardware history
- fitting notes
- final pattern version
- production record
- repair history
- re-order history

## Bespoke workflow
**CONSULT → MEASURE → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FIRST FIT → SECOND FIT → GOLDEN FIT → MAKE → QC → REVEAL → CARE → ARCHIVE**

## Pattern versioning
Every pattern receives:
**PRODUCT / CLIENT / BLOCK / VERSION / DATE / MAKER**

Never overwrite a proven pattern. Create a new version.

## Privacy rule
Collect only information required for service, fit, provenance or legally necessary operations. No surveillance-based personalization.

---

# 10. PROVENANCE / ARCHIVE

Every significant object receives:

**OBJECT ID → PRODUCT FAMILY → MATERIALS → HARDWARE → CRAFT RECORD → EDITION → CLIENT RECORD WHERE APPROPRIATE → CARE → REPAIR → ALTERATION → AUTHENTICITY → ARCHIVE**

Physical archive remains authoritative. Digital passport is supporting evidence.

## Repair principle
A repaired EDRIS object should become more historically valuable, not disposable.

Repairs must be logged with:
- date
- reason
- component replaced
- material used
- maker
- photographs where appropriate
- post-repair condition

---

# 11. ATELIER OPERATING STANDARD

## Physical sequence
**ARRIVAL → CONSULTATION → MATERIAL DISCOVERY → DESIGN → FITTING → REVEAL → PACKAGING → AFTERCARE → ARCHIVE**

## Environment
- black stone
- dark wood
- blackened metal
- controlled warm light
- selective greenery
- Iranian symmetry translated through Art Deco
- no theme-park Persian décor

## Service behavior
EDRIS should feel calm, exact, private and human.

No aggressive sales choreography.

---

# 12. DIGITAL HOUSE — INFORMATION ARCHITECTURE

Primary worlds:
**SHOP / TAILORING / OBJECTS / JEWELRY / WATCHES / FRAGRANCE / TRAVEL / HOUSE / ARCHIVE / ATELIER**

Primary discovery paths:
- Product
- Outfit
- Occasion
- Family
- Material
- Weather
- Journey
- Object
- Story

Every product page:
1. Hero object
2. Function
3. Material
4. Construction
5. House story
6. Wear With
7. Complete the Object
8. The EDRIS World
9. Next Object
10. Care
11. Provenance

---

# 13. CONTENT ENGINE

Six editorial worlds:

**THE HOUSE** — architecture, identity, craft  
**THE GARMENT** — construction, tailoring, material  
**THE OBJECT** — watches, jewelry, leather, eyewear  
**THE WORLD** — Iran, rain, ruins, nature, cities, night  
**THE HUMAN** — desire, ceremony, solitude, movement  
**THE ARCHIVE** — provenance, repair, aging, memory, legacy

Content rule:
**Show the world; let the product emerge from it.**

---

# 14. PRODUCTION ROADMAP

## Phase 0 — House lock
- Freeze v4.2 ecosystem.
- Approve v5.0 execution rules.
- Assign product IDs.
- Create master product records.

## Phase 1 — Foundation prototypes
Prioritize:
001 TUXEDO, COLUMN, STRUCTURE SHIRT, FOUNDATION TEE, DISTRICT DENIM, RUIN, MONUMENT, DOSSIER, RABBIT, SEAL, HOUSE CUFFLINKS, HOUSE STUDS, OPERATIVE, AFTER THE RAIN, 12 CM BOW, VAULT.

## Phase 2 — Fit and engineering
- develop blocks
- prototype
- fit
- revise
- golden sample
- tolerance sheet

## Phase 3 — Supply chain
- identify material suppliers
- identify hardware maker
- identify footwear maker
- identify leather maker
- identify watch development partner
- identify fragrance development/manufacturing partner
- document minimum order quantities and lead times

## Phase 4 — House systems
- packaging prototypes
- provenance system
- TUX GEN records
- website architecture
- recommendation graph
- content library

## Phase 5 — Launch preparation
- costing
- pricing
- labeling
- safety/regulatory review
- photography
- copy
- inventory
- fulfillment
- customer care

## Phase 6 — Release
Foundation → Second Movement → Third Movement.

## Phase 7 — Archive and iteration
Every released object enters the archive. Changes become new versions, not silent substitutions.

---

# 15. COSTING / PRICING WORKFLOW

For every product:

**MATERIAL COST + COMPONENT COST + LABOR + DEVELOPMENT AMORTIZATION + PACKAGING + LOGISTICS + QC + OVERHEAD ALLOCATION = LANDED COST**

Then establish target gross margin and channel economics.

Pricing must reflect:
- craft
- scarcity
- material quality
- development complexity
- aftercare
- provenance
- brand position

Never price solely from competitor imitation.

---

# 16. QC MASTER GATE

A product fails release if any of the following is unresolved:

- unsafe construction
- unacceptable material performance
- dimensional instability
- inconsistent hardware finish
- poor fit
- weak closure/fastener performance
- packaging damage risk
- missing care instructions
- incomplete provenance
- incorrect labeling
- unresolved supplier quality
- unresolved regulatory requirement
- unacceptable visual deviation from golden sample

Golden sample is the visual and construction reference.

---

# 17. HOUSE-LEVEL FINAL CHECK

Before any collection launch ask:

### Identity
Does it feel unmistakably EDRIS without the logo?

### Function
Does it solve a real problem?

### Beauty
Is it beautiful before the story is explained?

### Strangeness
Is there a second-glance detail?

### Meaning
Does the object carry a deeper house idea?

### Iranian soul
Is Iran present in the thinking rather than costume?

### Longevity
Would it still make sense in twenty years?

### System
Does it connect naturally to another EDRIS object?

### Craft
Can it be made properly?

### Archive
Will it become more interesting with age?

If the answer is no, redesign or delete.

---

# 18. MASTER LAUNCH PRIORITY

### Tier A — Build first
001 TUXEDO; STRUCTURE SHIRT; COLUMN; MONUMENT; DOSSIER; RABBIT; SEAL; HOUSE CUFFLINKS; HOUSE STUDS; OPERATIVE; AFTER THE RAIN; VAULT; 12 CM BOW.

### Tier B — Complete the wardrobe
FOUNDATION TEE; DISTRICT DENIM; RUIN; HEIRLOOM KNIT; AFTERHOURS; SPIRE; DECO; TWENTY-FOUR; NOIR; VOYAGE; AXIS; CREST; MIDNIGHT; ARCHIVE.

### Tier C — Expand the world
PATRON; MONSOON; PORTAL; WILD; OBSIDIAN; FRONTIER; RAIN; RELIC; VECTOR; NOCTURNE; SALON; HOUSE; LINK; BASTION; MASK; RELIQUARY; OBSERVATORY; ALTAR; WET STONE.

### Conditional / later
OUTPOST; ORACLE; FIELD; RAIN ON STONE; GROOMING; BAR; DESK.

---

# 19. WHAT IS NOW FINISHED

The following are considered conceptually finished:

- House identity
- House philosophy
- Product universe
- Product hierarchy
- Category coverage
- Product-family system
- Recommendation philosophy
- Client journey
- TUX GEN role
- Provenance philosophy
- Packaging architecture
- Atelier identity
- Digital information architecture
- Content architecture
- Launch hierarchy
- Quality gate
- Future-product discipline
- 001 design direction

# 20. WHAT IS NOW THE WORK

The remaining work is physical and operational:

1. Draw production patterns.
2. Produce prototypes.
3. Fit and revise.
4. Approve golden samples.
5. Engineer materials and hardware.
6. Establish suppliers.
7. Cost and price.
8. Complete regulatory/labeling review.
9. Build packaging.
10. Build TUX GEN records.
11. Build website/app data model.
12. Photograph and write the products.
13. Launch.
14. Repair, archive and iterate.

This is no longer a question of inventing more EDRIS categories.

It is the transition from **house design to house construction**.

---

# FINAL HOUSE STATEMENT

**EDRIS is a complete house.**

It does not need endless products to feel complete.
It needs excellent products, connected intelligently, made correctly, documented permanently and allowed to age.

A tuxedo leads to a shirt.
A shirt leads to a suit.
A suit leads to a watch.
A watch leads to a leather object.
A leather object leads to travel.
Travel leads to fragrance.
Fragrance leads to memory.
Memory becomes archive.
Archive becomes legacy.

**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**


---

## 001 QC gate
- Lapels: 12 cm ± approved production tolerance.
- Left/right peak symmetry.
- Break height symmetry.
- Shoulder balance.
- Collar clean against neck.
- Sleeve pitch natural at rest.
- No pulling across chest.
- No collapse at armhole.
- Pocket jets parallel.
- Vents equal.
- Trouser crease centered.
- Satin stripe uninterrupted.
- Shirt studs aligned.
- Silver hardware finish consistent.
- White shirt remains deliberately crisp against black tuxedo.

---

# 6. RECOMMENDATION ENGINE — MASTER LOGIC

Every product page contains exactly four recommendation roles:

### WEAR WITH
The garments that make the primary look work.

### COMPLETE THE OBJECT
Accessories, jewelry, footwear or supporting objects that complete the purchase.

### THE EDRIS WORLD
Conceptually related products, not necessarily commercially adjacent.

### NEXT OBJECT
Exactly one strongest next purchase.

## Ranking logic
1. Functional necessity
2. Visual compatibility
3. Same house language
4. Same use occasion
5. Family continuity
6. Customer stage
7. Purchase diversity
8. No forced upsell

## Recommendation exclusions
Do not recommend:
- unrelated categories merely to increase basket size
- multiple near-identical alternatives
- products unavailable in the client's size/region
- products that contradict the outfit
- future/held products as if they are purchasable

## Family graph

**001 CEREMONY:** 001 → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → STUDS → CUFFLINKS → MONUMENT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN → VAULT

**COLUMN HOUSE SUIT:** COLUMN → STRUCTURE SHIRT → NEEDLE → SEAL → MONUMENT → DOSSIER → OPERATIVE

**PATRON AUTHORITY:** PATRON → STRUCTURE SHIRT → CREST → DECO → DOSSIER → HOUSE → ARCHIVE

**NOCTURNE NIGHT:** NOCTURNE → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → SEAL → DECO → MIDNIGHT → NOCTURNE FRAGRANCE

**RUIN WINTER:** RUIN → HEIRLOOM KNIT → COLUMN TROUSER → SPIRE → VAULT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN

**RAIN WEATHER:** RAIN → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → VOYAGE → AFTER THE RAIN

**WILD FERAL:** WILD → FOUNDATION TEE → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → LINK → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN

**FRONTIER TRAVEL:** FRONTIER → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RAIN → RELIC → VOYAGE → FIELD

**ARTIFACT:** RABBIT → SEAL → VAULT → RELIQUARY → ARCHIVE → OPERATIVE

---

# 7. VISUAL DESIGN SYSTEM

## 7.1 Wordmark
EDRIS wordmark must be restrained, architectural and legible. No decorative Persian imitation. No permanent dependency on the crest.

## 7.2 Masked Rabbit
Use as a house signature, not wallpaper.

Primary forms:
- blind/debossed
- cast metal
- engraved
- embroidered only when materially justified
- architectural negative space

Never use as a cartoon mascot.

## 7.3 Typography
System should use:
- one high-contrast editorial serif or equivalent for ceremonial/editorial expression
- one neutral architectural sans for navigation, product data and utility
- one optional monospaced/technical face for provenance and atelier records

Typography must feel cinematic and engineered rather than futuristic.

## 7.4 Photography
Six recurring visual worlds:
- ceremony
- architecture
- weather
- wilderness
- machine/workshop
- archive/memory

Rules:
- human presence over sterile product-only imagery
- controlled shadow
- tactile material detail
- no excessive black crush
- no generic Persian props
- no fake aristocratic mansion clichés
- no AI-generated people presented as real campaigns

## 7.5 Color architecture
Core: black, forest-black, ivory/white, mineral grey, aged silver.
Secondary: bottle green, black-burgundy, mineral brown.
No rainbow seasonal identity.

## 7.6 Hardware
Master geometry:
**narrow vertical incision crossing a framed/stepped architectural form.**

The geometry should be recognizable across a button, clasp, ring, watch crown and fragrance cap without literally repeating the same object.

---

# 8. PACKAGING EXECUTION STANDARD

Universal sequence:
**OUTER SHELL → REVEAL → OBJECT CHAMBER → PROVENANCE → CARE → DIGITAL PASSPORT**

Materials:
- near-black board
- black textile
- aged/blackened metal
- blind/debossed marks
- concealed geometry

Packaging QC:
- closure cycle test
- abrasion test
- transit protection
- humidity considerations
- hardware tarnish control
- correct provenance record
- correct care insert
- correct product identity

Packaging must feel like opening an EDRIS object, not opening advertising.

---

# 9. TUX GEN — OPERATING SYSTEM

## Client record
- client ID
- consent / privacy status
- body measurements
- posture observations
- fit preferences
- style preferences
- previous garments
- alterations
- material history
- hardware history
- fitting notes
- final pattern version
- production record
- repair history
- re-order history

## Bespoke workflow
**CONSULT → MEASURE → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FIRST FIT → SECOND FIT → GOLDEN FIT → MAKE → QC → REVEAL → CARE → ARCHIVE**

## Pattern versioning
Every pattern receives:
**PRODUCT / CLIENT / BLOCK / VERSION / DATE / MAKER**

Never overwrite a proven pattern. Create a new version.

## Privacy rule
Collect only information required for service, fit, provenance or legally necessary operations. No surveillance-based personalization.

---

# 10. PROVENANCE / ARCHIVE

Every significant object receives:

**OBJECT ID → PRODUCT FAMILY → MATERIALS → HARDWARE → CRAFT RECORD → EDITION → CLIENT RECORD WHERE APPROPRIATE → CARE → REPAIR → ALTERATION → AUTHENTICITY → ARCHIVE**

Physical archive remains authoritative. Digital passport is supporting evidence.

## Repair principle
A repaired EDRIS object should become more historically valuable, not disposable.

Repairs must be logged with:
- date
- reason
- component replaced
- material used
- maker
- photographs where appropriate
- post-repair condition

---

# 11. ATELIER OPERATING STANDARD

## Physical sequence
**ARRIVAL → CONSULTATION → MATERIAL DISCOVERY → DESIGN → FITTING → REVEAL → PACKAGING → AFTERCARE → ARCHIVE**

## Environment
- black stone
- dark wood
- blackened metal
- controlled warm light
- selective greenery
- Iranian symmetry translated through Art Deco
- no theme-park Persian décor

## Service behavior
EDRIS should feel calm, exact, private and human.

No aggressive sales choreography.

---

# 12. DIGITAL HOUSE — INFORMATION ARCHITECTURE

Primary worlds:
**SHOP / TAILORING / OBJECTS / JEWELRY / WATCHES / FRAGRANCE / TRAVEL / HOUSE / ARCHIVE / ATELIER**

Primary discovery paths:
- Product
- Outfit
- Occasion
- Family
- Material
- Weather
- Journey
- Object
- Story

Every product page:
1. Hero object
2. Function
3. Material
4. Construction
5. House story
6. Wear With
7. Complete the Object
8. The EDRIS World
9. Next Object
10. Care
11. Provenance

---

# 13. CONTENT ENGINE

Six editorial worlds:

**THE HOUSE** — architecture, identity, craft  
**THE GARMENT** — construction, tailoring, material  
**THE OBJECT** — watches, jewelry, leather, eyewear  
**THE WORLD** — Iran, rain, ruins, nature, cities, night  
**THE HUMAN** — desire, ceremony, solitude, movement  
**THE ARCHIVE** — provenance, repair, aging, memory, legacy

Content rule:
**Show the world; let the product emerge from it.**

---

# 14. PRODUCTION ROADMAP

## Phase 0 — House lock
- Freeze v4.2 ecosystem.
- Approve v5.0 execution rules.
- Assign product IDs.
- Create master product records.

## Phase 1 — Foundation prototypes
Prioritize:
001 TUXEDO, COLUMN, STRUCTURE SHIRT, FOUNDATION TEE, DISTRICT DENIM, RUIN, MONUMENT, DOSSIER, RABBIT, SEAL, HOUSE CUFFLINKS, HOUSE STUDS, OPERATIVE, AFTER THE RAIN, 12 CM BOW, VAULT.

## Phase 2 — Fit and engineering
- develop blocks
- prototype
- fit
- revise
- golden sample
- tolerance sheet

## Phase 3 — Supply chain
- identify material suppliers
- identify hardware maker
- identify footwear maker
- identify leather maker
- identify watch development partner
- identify fragrance development/manufacturing partner
- document minimum order quantities and lead times

## Phase 4 — House systems
- packaging prototypes
- provenance system
- TUX GEN records
- website architecture
- recommendation graph
- content library

## Phase 5 — Launch preparation
- costing
- pricing
- labeling
- safety/regulatory review
- photography
- copy
- inventory
- fulfillment
- customer care

## Phase 6 — Release
Foundation → Second Movement → Third Movement.

## Phase 7 — Archive and iteration
Every released object enters the archive. Changes become new versions, not silent substitutions.

---

# 15. COSTING / PRICING WORKFLOW

For every product:

**MATERIAL COST + COMPONENT COST + LABOR + DEVELOPMENT AMORTIZATION + PACKAGING + LOGISTICS + QC + OVERHEAD ALLOCATION = LANDED COST**

Then establish target gross margin and channel economics.

Pricing must reflect:
- craft
- scarcity
- material quality
- development complexity
- aftercare
- provenance
- brand position

Never price solely from competitor imitation.

---

# 16. QC MASTER GATE

A product fails release if any of the following is unresolved:

- unsafe construction
- unacceptable material performance
- dimensional instability
- inconsistent hardware finish
- poor fit
- weak closure/fastener performance
- packaging damage risk
- missing care instructions
- incomplete provenance
- incorrect labeling
- unresolved supplier quality
- unresolved regulatory requirement
- unacceptable visual deviation from golden sample

Golden sample is the visual and construction reference.

---

# 17. HOUSE-LEVEL FINAL CHECK

Before any collection launch ask:

### Identity
Does it feel unmistakably EDRIS without the logo?

### Function
Does it solve a real problem?

### Beauty
Is it beautiful before the story is explained?

### Strangeness
Is there a second-glance detail?

### Meaning
Does the object carry a deeper house idea?

### Iranian soul
Is Iran present in the thinking rather than costume?

### Longevity
Would it still make sense in twenty years?

### System
Does it connect naturally to another EDRIS object?

### Craft
Can it be made properly?

### Archive
Will it become more interesting with age?

If the answer is no, redesign or delete.

---

# 18. MASTER LAUNCH PRIORITY

### Tier A — Build first
001 TUXEDO; STRUCTURE SHIRT; COLUMN; MONUMENT; DOSSIER; RABBIT; SEAL; HOUSE CUFFLINKS; HOUSE STUDS; OPERATIVE; AFTER THE RAIN; VAULT; 12 CM BOW.

### Tier B — Complete the wardrobe
FOUNDATION TEE; DISTRICT DENIM; RUIN; HEIRLOOM KNIT; AFTERHOURS; SPIRE; DECO; TWENTY-FOUR; NOIR; VOYAGE; AXIS; CREST; MIDNIGHT; ARCHIVE.

### Tier C — Expand the world
PATRON; MONSOON; PORTAL; WILD; OBSIDIAN; FRONTIER; RAIN; RELIC; VECTOR; NOCTURNE; SALON; HOUSE; LINK; BASTION; MASK; RELIQUARY; OBSERVATORY; ALTAR; WET STONE.

### Conditional / later
OUTPOST; ORACLE; FIELD; RAIN ON STONE; GROOMING; BAR; DESK.

---

# 19. WHAT IS NOW FINISHED

The following are considered conceptually finished:

- House identity
- House philosophy
- Product universe
- Product hierarchy
- Category coverage
- Product-family system
- Recommendation philosophy
- Client journey
- TUX GEN role
- Provenance philosophy
- Packaging architecture
- Atelier identity
- Digital information architecture
- Content architecture
- Launch hierarchy
- Quality gate
- Future-product discipline
- 001 design direction

# 20. WHAT IS NOW THE WORK

The remaining work is physical and operational:

1. Draw production patterns.
2. Produce prototypes.
3. Fit and revise.
4. Approve golden samples.
5. Engineer materials and hardware.
6. Establish suppliers.
7. Cost and price.
8. Complete regulatory/labeling review.
9. Build packaging.
10. Build TUX GEN records.
11. Build website/app data model.
12. Photograph and write the products.
13. Launch.
14. Repair, archive and iterate.

This is no longer a question of inventing more EDRIS categories.

It is the transition from **house design to house construction**.

---

# FINAL HOUSE STATEMENT

**EDRIS is a complete house.**

It does not need endless products to feel complete.
It needs excellent products, connected intelligently, made correctly, documented permanently and allowed to age.

A tuxedo leads to a shirt.
A shirt leads to a suit.
A suit leads to a watch.
A watch leads to a leather object.
A leather object leads to travel.
Travel leads to fragrance.
Fragrance leads to memory.
Memory becomes archive.
Archive becomes legacy.

**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/002_EDRIS_001_Printable_Pattern_Pack_v1.0/EDRIS_001_PATTERN_INSTRUCTIONS_FA_EN.txt

HOUSE OF EDRIS — 001 ONE OF ONE
1:1 PATTERN DRAFT v1.0

ثبت اندازه‌ها:
شانه 46 / سینه 105 / کمر 101 / باسن 112 / حلقه آستین 56 سانتی‌متر
آرنج 35 / شانه تا آرنج 40 / مبنای آستین 70 سانتی‌متر
هدف کت: سینه 113 / کمر 107 / باسن 117 / قد 79
شلوار: فاق جلو 38 / فاق پشت 40 / دور ساق 43 / دور مچ 26 / قد بیرونی 100
کمر شلوار: 4.5 سانتی‌متر نهایی، ساید اَجاستر، بدون حلقه کمربند

قفل‌های طراحی:
یک دکمه؛ یقه پیک 12 سانتی‌متری کاملاً صاف و هندسی؛ دو چاک بلند؛ شانه ساختاریافته؛
پیراهن سفید رسمی با سرآستین فرانسوی و استد نقره‌ای کهنه؛ پاپیون مشکی بزرگ.

چاپ:
100% / Actual Size. گزینه Fit to Page خاموش.
مربع کالیبراسیون باید دقیقاً 100×100 میلی‌متر باشد.
قبل از پارچه اصلی، توآل دوخته و پرو شود.
این فایل «draft/toile master» است و پس از پرو باید اصلاح نهایی الگو انجام شود.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/COMMERCE_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/015_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v1.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---

:root{--b:#080808;--w:#eeeae2;--m:#aaa69e;--l:#2b2b2b}*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;background:var(--b);color:var(--w);font-family:Arial,sans-serif}header{height:78px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--l);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:0 4vw;position:sticky;top:0;background:rgba(8,8,8,.94);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);z-index:5}.mark{font:700 24px Georgia,serif;letter-spacing:.28em}nav{display:flex;gap:28px;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em}button{background:none;border:0;color:var(--w);letter-spacing:.16em}#menu{display:none}.hero{min-height:calc(100vh - 78px);padding:12vh 8vw;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;border-bottom:1px solid var(--l)}.hero p:first-child,.label{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.24em;color:var(--m)}h1{font:500 clamp(70px,13vw,190px)/.78 Georgia,serif;letter-spacing:-.06em;margin:40px 0}.sub{max-width:560px;color:#bbb;line-height:1.7}.cta{display:inline-block;margin-top:35px;border:1px solid #777;padding:15px 22px;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.18em;width:max-content}.section,.split,.archive,.atelier{padding:9vw 8vw;border-bottom:1px solid var(--l)}h2{font:500 clamp(44px,7vw,105px)/.95 Georgia,serif;margin:20px 0 50px;letter-spacing:-.04em}.grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:1px;background:var(--l)}article{background:var(--b);padding:55px;min-height:300px}article span{font-size:9px;color:var(--m);letter-spacing:.2em}h3{font:500 34px Georgia,serif}article p,.split p,.archive p,.atelier p{color:#aaa;line-height:1.7;max-width:600px}.split{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8vw;align-items:end}.archive{min-height:65vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center}.atelier{min-height:70vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center}footer{padding:35px 4vw;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;color:#888;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.18em}@media(max-width:700px){nav{display:none}#menu{display:block}.hero{padding:16vh 7vw}h1{font-size:20vw}.grid,.split{grid-template-columns:1fr}article{padding:35px}.split{gap:30px}.section,.split,.archive,.atelier{padding:18vw 7vw}}

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/COMMERCE_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/017_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v2.0/EDRIS_BUILD/website/app.js

const products = [
  {id:'001',name:'ONE OF ONE TUXEDO',category:'FORMAL',tier:'O1',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Midnight / forest-black wool. One button. 12 cm straight peak. Full canvas.'},
  {id:'COLUMN',name:'THE COLUMN',category:'TAILORING',tier:'CORE / MTM',world:'TAILORING',desc:'Long controlled body, high armhole, straight architectural peak.'},
  {id:'STRUCTURE',name:'THE STRUCTURE SHIRT',category:'SHIRTS',tier:'CORE / SIGNATURE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Concealed placket, architectural collar, controlled body.'},
  {id:'FOUNDATION',name:'THE FOUNDATION TEE',category:'CASUAL',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Heavy compact cotton, strong shoulder, elongated straight body.'},
  {id:'DENIM',name:'THE DISTRICT DENIM',category:'DENIM',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Near-black heavy denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered.'},
  {id:'RUIN',name:'THE RUIN',category:'OUTERWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'WEATHER',desc:'Below-knee black wool, controlled shoulder, high collar.'},
  {id:'MONUMENT',name:'THE MONUMENT',category:'FOOTWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Formal wholecut built as the natural partner to 001.'},
  {id:'DOSSIER',name:'THE DOSSIER',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'TRAVEL',desc:'Rigid black architectural briefcase with mechanical lock.'},
  {id:'RABBIT',name:'THE RABBIT',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'ICON',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Substantial aged sterling silver house icon.'},
  {id:'SEAL',name:'THE SEAL',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Quiet architectural brooch for tailoring.'},
  {id:'OPERATIVE',name:'THE OPERATIVE',category:'HOROLOGY',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'TIME',desc:'40–41 mm automatic instrument watch refined for formal wear.'},
  {id:'RAIN',name:'001 AFTER THE RAIN',category:'FRAGRANCE',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'FRAGRANCE',desc:'Cardamom × vetiver × mineral rain. Civilization reclaimed by nature.'},
  {id:'BOW12',name:'THE 12 CM BOW',category:'ACCESSORIES',tier:'ICON',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Oversized architectural butterfly bow in black silk.'},
  {id:'VAULT',name:'THE VAULT',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Personal-object case for watch, jewelry, fragrance, passport and keys.'}
];
const recommendations={001:['STRUCTURE','BOW12','SEAL','MONUMENT','OPERATIVE','RAIN','VAULT'],COLUMN:['STRUCTURE','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','OPERATIVE'],STRUCTURE:['COLUMN','001','DENIM','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','RAIN'],RUIN:['STRUCTURE','FOUNDATION','OPERATIVE','VAULT'],RAIN:['001','RUIN','MONSOON','VAULT']};
let cart=[];
function render(list=products){document.querySelector('#catalogue').innerHTML=list.map(p=>`<article class="product" data-id="${p.id}"><div class="product-code">${p.tier}</div><h3>${p.name}</h3><p>${p.desc}</p><small>${p.category} · ${p.world}</small><button onclick="openProduct('${p.id}')">ENTER OBJECT</button></article>`).join('')}
function openProduct(id){const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);const ids=recommendations[id]||[];const rec=ids.map(x=>products.find(y=>y.id===x)).filter(Boolean);document.querySelector('#modal').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-inner"><button class="close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button><div class="label">${p.tier} / ${p.world}</div><h2>${p.name}</h2><p>${p.desc}</p><p class="rule">DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE</p><button class="cta" onclick="addToBuild('${p.id}')">ADD TO BUILD</button><h4>THE EDRIS WORLD</h4><div class="rec-grid">${rec.map(r=>`<button onclick="openProduct('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.querySelector('#modal').classList.add('show')}
function closeModal(){document.querySelector('#modal').classList.remove('show')}
function addToBuild(id){if(!cart.includes(id))cart.push(id);document.querySelector('#buildCount').textContent=cart.length;openBuild()}
function openBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.add('show');document.querySelector('#buildItems').innerHTML=cart.map(id=>{const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<div><span>${p.name}</span><button onclick="cart=cart.filter(x=>x!=='${id}');openBuild()">REMOVE</button></div>`}).join('')||'<p>Your build is empty.</p>'}
function closeBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.remove('show')}
document.querySelector('#search').addEventListener('input',e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render(products.filter(p=>Object.values(p).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)))});
document.querySelector('#filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{const q=e.target.value;render(q==='ALL'?products:products.filter(p=>p.category===q||p.world===q))});
document.querySelector('#buildButton').onclick=openBuild;
render();

.tools{display:flex;gap:12px;margin:28px 0;flex-wrap:wrap}.tools input,.tools select,.tools button{background:#0b0b0b;color:#eee;border:1px solid #333;padding:14px 16px;font:inherit}.tools input{min-width:280px;flex:1}.product{border-top:1px solid #333;padding:24px 0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px}.product h3{margin:0;font-size:24px}.product p{max-width:480px;color:#aaa}.product small{color:#777}.product button,.rec-grid button{align-self:flex-start;background:none;color:#eee;border:1px solid #555;padding:10px 14px;cursor:pointer}.overlay{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.88);z-index:20;padding:6vw;overflow:auto}.overlay.show{display:block}.modal-inner{max-width:820px;margin:auto;background:#0b0b0b;border:1px solid #333;padding:40px;position:relative}.close{position:absolute;right:18px;top:14px;background:none;border:0;color:#fff;font-size:30px}.rec-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(180px,1fr));gap:10px}.rec-grid button{width:100%;text-align:left}.rule{letter-spacing:.12em;font-size:11px;color:#888;margin:28px 0}.modal-inner h2{font-size:clamp(38px,7vw,90px);line-height:.9;margin:20px 0}.modal-inner .cta{display:inline-block;border:1px solid #aaa;padding:14px 20px;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;margin-bottom:25px}

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TAILOR_CUTTING_SHEET_FA.md

# EDRIS 001 — برگه فنی برش و خیاطی

## مشخصات قفل‌شده
- کت: تک‌دکمه، یقه آرشال نوک‌تیز، عرض ۱۲ سانتی‌متر، خط یقه کاملاً مستقیم و معماری‌وار.
- پارچه: پشم لوکس مشکی-نیمه‌شب / forest-black.
- ساختار: فول‌کَنواس، horsehair chest، سرشانه ساختاریافته، armhole بالا.
- جیب: دو جیب فیلتابی دوتایی.
- پشت: دو چاک.
- پیراهن: سفید، پنبه رسمی ظریف، یقه رسمی، French cuff، جلوی تمیز/پنهان، studs نقره‌ای کهنه.
- شلوار: فاق بلند، جلوی صاف، side adjuster، بدون belt loop، نوار ساتن مشکی، خط اتوی قوی.

## اندازه‌های بدن ثبت‌شده
1. 49 cm
2. 54 cm
3. 30 cm
4. 56 cm
5. 105 cm
6. 73 cm
7. 35 cm
8. 18 cm
9. 70 cm — سرشانه تا مچ

## ترتیب ساخت
1. اندازه‌گیری بدن و کنترل نقاط مبنا
2. ترسیم بلوک پایه
3. افزودن ease طراحی
4. ترسیم یقه و rever با خط مستقیم
5. toile اول
6. پرو اول و ثبت اصلاحات
7. toile دوم
8. ساخت نمونه canvas
9. پرو نهایی
10. golden sample
11. QC
12. تولید

## قانون یقه
خط بیرونی peak lapel نباید قوس‌دار به نظر برسد. شکست یقه، roll و نقطه peak باید با خطوط کنترل‌شده و هندسی ساخته شوند. ۱۲ سانتی‌متر سقف عرض است و بیشتر از آن مجاز نیست.

## سخت‌گیری خیاطی
هیچ تغییر سلیقه‌ای در عرض یقه، جای دکمه، فرم شانه، طول بدن یا rise بدون ثبت نسخه جدید مجاز نیست.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/DATA_MODEL.md

# TUX GEN — DATA MODEL v1

## Client
client_id, identity, contact, consent_status, created_at

## Body Profile
profile_id, client_id, measurement_set_version, measurements, posture_notes, date

## Pattern
pattern_id, product_id, client_id, block_version, alteration_version, status

## Fitting
fitting_id, client_id, garment_id, stage, date, notes, corrections, approval

## Garment
garment_id, product_id, serial, material_lot, hardware_set, maker, QC_status

## Provenance
object_id, product_family, materials, craft_record, edition, care, repair, alteration, authenticity, archive

## Rule
Every change creates a version. Never overwrite an approved measurement, pattern, fitting or provenance record.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/001_SUPPLIER_PACK.md

# EDRIS 001 — SUPPLIER / MAKER PACK

## Required inputs
- Approved technical master
- Approved body measurement sheet
- Approved toile corrections
- Fabric specification and approved swatch
- Black satin facing specification
- Aged-silver hardware specification
- Thread / lining / canvas specification

## Sample gates
A: block/toile — fit only
B: corrected toile — fit + proportion
C: canvas sample — construction + fit
D: golden sample — production reference

## Supplier must return
- sample photos
- construction notes
- deviations
- material substitutions
- labor estimate
- lead time
- unit cost

No substitution becomes approved by silence. Every deviation requires written approval and version update.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/CHECKOUT_SPEC.md

# EDRIS COMMERCE — LIVE CHECKOUT SPEC

## Customer
account, guest checkout, consent, addresses, saved preferences

## Order
order_id, customer_id, line_items, variant, quantity, price_snapshot, tax, shipping, status

## Inventory
SKU, location, available, reserved, allocated, sold, return_hold

## Payments
Use a PCI-compliant external payment provider. Store provider reference, not raw card data.

## Fulfillment
payment confirmed → inventory reserved → pick/pack → provenance attached → shipment created → tracking → delivered

## Returns
return request → eligibility → authorization → inspection → refund/exchange → inventory disposition


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/COMMERCE_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.


---

## Digital
[ ] Responsive QA
[ ] Accessibility pass
[ ] Product data complete

[ ] Build/recommendation QA
[ ] Account QA
[ ] Checkout sandbox QA
[ ] Analytics QA
[ ] Error pages
[ ] Security review

## Physical
[ ] Golden sample approved
[ ] Materials secured
[ ] QC standard signed
[ ] Packaging approved
[ ] Care labels/documentation
[ ] Provenance record
[ ] Inventory received
[ ] Photography complete

## Commercial
[ ] Pricing approved
[ ] Tax setup
[ ] Shipping zones/rates
[ ] Returns policy
[ ] Privacy/consent
[ ] Terms
[ ] Customer support

## Launch sequence
PRIVATE CLIENTS → CONTROLLED ORDER WINDOW → FEEDBACK → CORRECTION → PUBLIC RELEASE

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/018_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/website/app.js

const products = [
  {id:'001',name:'ONE OF ONE TUXEDO',category:'FORMAL',tier:'O1',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Midnight / forest-black wool. One button. 12 cm straight peak. Full canvas.'},
  {id:'COLUMN',name:'THE COLUMN',category:'TAILORING',tier:'CORE / MTM',world:'TAILORING',desc:'Long controlled body, high armhole, straight architectural peak.'},
  {id:'STRUCTURE',name:'THE STRUCTURE SHIRT',category:'SHIRTS',tier:'CORE / SIGNATURE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Concealed placket, architectural collar, controlled body.'},
  {id:'FOUNDATION',name:'THE FOUNDATION TEE',category:'CASUAL',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Heavy compact cotton, strong shoulder, elongated straight body.'},
  {id:'DENIM',name:'THE DISTRICT DENIM',category:'DENIM',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Near-black heavy denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered.'},
  {id:'RUIN',name:'THE RUIN',category:'OUTERWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'WEATHER',desc:'Below-knee black wool, controlled shoulder, high collar.'},
  {id:'MONUMENT',name:'THE MONUMENT',category:'FOOTWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Formal wholecut built as the natural partner to 001.'},
  {id:'DOSSIER',name:'THE DOSSIER',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'TRAVEL',desc:'Rigid black architectural briefcase with mechanical lock.'},
  {id:'RABBIT',name:'THE RABBIT',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'ICON',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Substantial aged sterling silver house icon.'},
  {id:'SEAL',name:'THE SEAL',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Quiet architectural brooch for tailoring.'},
  {id:'OPERATIVE',name:'THE OPERATIVE',category:'HOROLOGY',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'TIME',desc:'40–41 mm automatic instrument watch refined for formal wear.'},
  {id:'RAIN',name:'001 AFTER THE RAIN',category:'FRAGRANCE',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'FRAGRANCE',desc:'Cardamom × vetiver × mineral rain. Civilization reclaimed by nature.'},
  {id:'BOW12',name:'THE 12 CM BOW',category:'ACCESSORIES',tier:'ICON',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Oversized architectural butterfly bow in black silk.'},
  {id:'VAULT',name:'THE VAULT',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Personal-object case for watch, jewelry, fragrance, passport and keys.'}
];
const recommendations={001:['STRUCTURE','BOW12','SEAL','MONUMENT','OPERATIVE','RAIN','VAULT'],COLUMN:['STRUCTURE','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','OPERATIVE'],STRUCTURE:['COLUMN','001','DENIM','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','RAIN'],RUIN:['STRUCTURE','FOUNDATION','OPERATIVE','VAULT'],RAIN:['001','RUIN','MONSOON','VAULT']};
let cart=[];
function render(list=products){document.querySelector('#catalogue').innerHTML=list.map(p=>`<article class="product" data-id="${p.id}"><div class="product-code">${p.tier}</div><h3>${p.name}</h3><p>${p.desc}</p><small>${p.category} · ${p.world}</small><button onclick="openProduct('${p.id}')">ENTER OBJECT</button></article>`).join('')}
function openProduct(id){const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);const ids=recommendations[id]||[];const rec=ids.map(x=>products.find(y=>y.id===x)).filter(Boolean);document.querySelector('#modal').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-inner"><button class="close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button><div class="label">${p.tier} / ${p.world}</div><h2>${p.name}</h2><p>${p.desc}</p><p class="rule">DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE</p><button class="cta" onclick="addToBuild('${p.id}')">ADD TO BUILD</button><h4>THE EDRIS WORLD</h4><div class="rec-grid">${rec.map(r=>`<button onclick="openProduct('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.querySelector('#modal').classList.add('show')}
function closeModal(){document.querySelector('#modal').classList.remove('show')}
function addToBuild(id){if(!cart.includes(id))cart.push(id);document.querySelector('#buildCount').textContent=cart.length;openBuild()}
function openBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.add('show');document.querySelector('#buildItems').innerHTML=cart.map(id=>{const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<div><span>${p.name}</span><button onclick="cart=cart.filter(x=>x!=='${id}');openBuild()">REMOVE</button></div>`}).join('')||'<p>Your build is empty.</p>'}
function closeBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.remove('show')}
document.querySelector('#search').addEventListener('input',e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render(products.filter(p=>Object.values(p).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)))});
document.querySelector('#filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{const q=e.target.value;render(q==='ALL'?products:products.filter(p=>p.category===q||p.world===q))});
document.querySelector('#buildButton').onclick=openBuild;
render();

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TAILOR_CUTTING_SHEET_FA.md

# EDRIS 001 — برگه فنی برش و خیاطی

## مشخصات قفل‌شده
- کت: تک‌دکمه، یقه آرشال نوک‌تیز، عرض ۱۲ سانتی‌متر، خط یقه کاملاً مستقیم و معماری‌وار.
- پارچه: پشم لوکس مشکی-نیمه‌شب / forest-black.
- ساختار: فول‌کَنواس، horsehair chest، سرشانه ساختاریافته، armhole بالا.
- جیب: دو جیب فیلتابی دوتایی.
- پشت: دو چاک.
- پیراهن: سفید، پنبه رسمی ظریف، یقه رسمی، French cuff، جلوی تمیز/پنهان، studs نقره‌ای کهنه.
- شلوار: فاق بلند، جلوی صاف، side adjuster، بدون belt loop، نوار ساتن مشکی، خط اتوی قوی.

## اندازه‌های بدن ثبت‌شده
1. 49 cm
2. 54 cm
3. 30 cm
4. 56 cm
5. 105 cm
6. 73 cm
7. 35 cm
8. 18 cm
9. 70 cm — سرشانه تا مچ

## ترتیب ساخت
1. اندازه‌گیری بدن و کنترل نقاط مبنا
2. ترسیم بلوک پایه
3. افزودن ease طراحی
4. ترسیم یقه و rever با خط مستقیم
5. toile اول
6. پرو اول و ثبت اصلاحات
7. toile دوم
8. ساخت نمونه canvas
9. پرو نهایی
10. golden sample
11. QC
12. تولید

## قانون یقه
خط بیرونی peak lapel نباید قوس‌دار به نظر برسد. شکست یقه، roll و نقطه peak باید با خطوط کنترل‌شده و هندسی ساخته شوند. ۱۲ سانتی‌متر سقف عرض است و بیشتر از آن مجاز نیست.

## سخت‌گیری خیاطی
هیچ تغییر سلیقه‌ای در عرض یقه، جای دکمه، فرم شانه، طول بدن یا rise بدون ثبت نسخه جدید مجاز نیست.
