# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/DATA_MODEL.md

# TUX GEN — DATA MODEL v1

## Client
client_id, identity, contact, consent_status, created_at

## Body Profile
profile_id, client_id, measurement_set_version, measurements, posture_notes, date

## Pattern
pattern_id, product_id, client_id, block_version, alteration_version, status

## Fitting
fitting_id, client_id, garment_id, stage, date, notes, corrections, approval

## Garment
garment_id, product_id, serial, material_lot, hardware_set, maker, QC_status

## Provenance
object_id, product_family, materials, craft_record, edition, care, repair, alteration, authenticity, archive

## Rule
Every change creates a version. Never overwrite an approved measurement, pattern, fitting or provenance record.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/001_SUPPLIER_PACK.md

# EDRIS 001 — SUPPLIER / MAKER PACK

## Required inputs
- Approved technical master
- Approved body measurement sheet
- Approved toile corrections
- Fabric specification and approved swatch
- Black satin facing specification
- Aged-silver hardware specification
- Thread / lining / canvas specification

## Sample gates
A: block/toile — fit only
B: corrected toile — fit + proportion
C: canvas sample — construction + fit
D: golden sample — production reference

## Supplier must return
- sample photos
- construction notes
- deviations
- material substitutions
- labor estimate
- lead time
- unit cost

No substitution becomes approved by silence. Every deviation requires written approval and version update.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/CHECKOUT_SPEC.md

# EDRIS COMMERCE — LIVE CHECKOUT SPEC

## Customer
account, guest checkout, consent, addresses, saved preferences

## Order
order_id, customer_id, line_items, variant, quantity, price_snapshot, tax, shipping, status

## Inventory
SKU, location, available, reserved, allocated, sold, return_hold

## Payments
Use a PCI-compliant external payment provider. Store provider reference, not raw card data.

## Fulfillment
payment confirmed → inventory reserved → pick/pack → provenance attached → shipment created → tracking → delivered

## Returns
return request → eligibility → authorization → inspection → refund/exchange → inventory disposition


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/COMMERCE_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.

## Digital

[ ] Analytics QA
[ ] Error pages

[ ] Materials secured
[ ] QC standard signed
[ ] Packaging approved
[ ] Care labels/documentation

[ ] Photography complete

## Commercial

[ ] Tax setup
[ ] Shipping zones/rates
[ ] Returns policy
[ ] Privacy/consent
[ ] Terms
[ ] Customer support

## Launch sequence
PRIVATE CLIENTS → CONTROLLED ORDER WINDOW → FEEDBACK → CORRECTION → PUBLIC RELEASE

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/019_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v3.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---

let cart=[];
function render(list=products){document.querySelector('#catalogue').innerHTML=list.map(p=>`<article class="product" data-id="${p.id}"><div class="product-code">${p.tier}</div><h3>${p.name}</h3><p>${p.desc}</p><small>${p.category} · ${p.world}</small><button onclick="openProduct('${p.id}')">ENTER OBJECT</button></article>`).join('')}
function openProduct(id){const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);const ids=recommendations[id]||[];const rec=ids.map(x=>products.find(y=>y.id===x)).filter(Boolean);document.querySelector('#modal').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-inner"><button class="close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button><div class="label">${p.tier} / ${p.world}</div><h2>${p.name}</h2><p>${p.desc}</p><p class="rule">DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE</p><button class="cta" onclick="addToBuild('${p.id}')">ADD TO BUILD</button><h4>THE EDRIS WORLD</h4><div class="rec-grid">${rec.map(r=>`<button onclick="openProduct('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.querySelector('#modal').classList.add('show')}
function closeModal(){document.querySelector('#modal').classList.remove('show')}
function addToBuild(id){if(!cart.includes(id))cart.push(id);document.querySelector('#buildCount').textContent=cart.length;openBuild()}
function openBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.add('show');document.querySelector('#buildItems').innerHTML=cart.map(id=>{const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<div><span>${p.name}</span><button onclick="cart=cart.filter(x=>x!=='${id}');openBuild()">REMOVE</button></div>`}).join('')||'<p>Your build is empty.</p>'}
function closeBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.remove('show')}
document.querySelector('#search').addEventListener('input',e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render(products.filter(p=>Object.values(p).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)))});
document.querySelector('#filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{const q=e.target.value;render(q==='ALL'?products:products.filter(p=>p.category===q||p.world===q))});
document.querySelector('#buildButton').onclick=openBuild;
render();


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.

## Sneaker discovery
THE VECTOR is intentionally presented as a full EDRIS casual sneaker, not a minimalist luxury trainer. Product discovery should communicate its retro-technical, chunky, layered character while preserving EDRIS's dark architectural identity.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TAILOR_CUTTING_SHEET_FA.md

# EDRIS 001 — برگه فنی برش و خیاطی

## مشخصات قفل‌شده
- کت: تک‌دکمه، یقه آرشال نوک‌تیز، عرض ۱۲ سانتی‌متر، خط یقه کاملاً مستقیم و معماری‌وار.
- پارچه: پشم لوکس مشکی-نیمه‌شب / forest-black.
- ساختار: فول‌کَنواس، horsehair chest، سرشانه ساختاریافته، armhole بالا.
- جیب: دو جیب فیلتابی دوتایی.
- پشت: دو چاک.
- پیراهن: سفید، پنبه رسمی ظریف، یقه رسمی، French cuff، جلوی تمیز/پنهان، studs نقره‌ای کهنه.
- شلوار: فاق بلند، جلوی صاف، side adjuster، بدون belt loop، نوار ساتن مشکی، خط اتوی قوی.

## اندازه‌های بدن ثبت‌شده
1. 49 cm
2. 54 cm
3. 30 cm
4. 56 cm
5. 105 cm
6. 73 cm
7. 35 cm
8. 18 cm
9. 70 cm — سرشانه تا مچ

## ترتیب ساخت
1. اندازه‌گیری بدن و کنترل نقاط مبنا
2. ترسیم بلوک پایه
3. افزودن ease طراحی
4. ترسیم یقه و rever با خط مستقیم
5. toile اول
6. پرو اول و ثبت اصلاحات
7. toile دوم
8. ساخت نمونه canvas
9. پرو نهایی
10. golden sample
11. QC
12. تولید

## قانون یقه
خط بیرونی peak lapel نباید قوس‌دار به نظر برسد. شکست یقه، roll و نقطه peak باید با خطوط کنترل‌شده و هندسی ساخته شوند. ۱۲ سانتی‌متر سقف عرض است و بیشتر از آن مجاز نیست.

## سخت‌گیری خیاطی
هیچ تغییر سلیقه‌ای در عرض یقه، جای دکمه، فرم شانه، طول بدن یا rise بدون ثبت نسخه جدید مجاز نیست.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/FOOTWEAR_SNEAKER_DIRECTION.md

# EDRIS Footwear — Retro Technical Sneaker Direction

## New permanent preference
EDRIS casual footwear should include a genuine retro-technical sneaker language inspired by the user's love of adidas Originals-era silhouettes such as the Adi2000 and Niteball II, and the sculptural technical language associated with the Yeezy 700 MNVN.

These are **references for energy and proportion, not templates to copy**.

## THE VECTOR — REVISED CORE SNEAKER

**Role:** EDRIS's signature everyday sneaker.

**Design character:** retro-futurist, chunky, architectural, slightly strange, luxurious but not precious.

### Silhouette
- Full, substantial retro sneaker volume rather than a minimal low-profile trainer.
- Layered upper with controlled panel geometry.
- Strong heel structure.
- Generous padded tongue and collar.
- Sculptural midsole with visible architectural mass.
- Moderately wide forefoot and stable stance.
- Athletic proportions, refined enough to sit beside tailoring.

### EDRIS translation
- Near-black / black / washed charcoal as the primary palette.
- Secondary forest-black, mineral grey and controlled ivory editions.
- Matte technical mesh or woven textile combined with smooth leather/suede overlays.
- One aged-silver or blackened-metal hardware detail only where functionally justified.
- House vertical-incision geometry hidden or subtly integrated into a panel, lace keeper or heel detail.
- Masked Rabbit remains hidden; never make the sneaker look like merchandise.
- No oversized external EDRIS logo.
- Identity must work from silhouette, panel architecture, material contrast and sole geometry.

### Sole
- Thick but disciplined.
- Layered/segmented construction with an Art Deco-like stepped rhythm.
- Black rubber outsole.
- No fake futuristic effects, transparent gimmicks or excessive mechanical detailing.

### Styling
THE VECTOR should work with:
- FOUNDATION TEE
- STRUCTURE SHIRT
- DISTRICT DENIM
- OBSIDIAN
- MONSOON
- AFTERHOURS
- RUIN

It should deliberately create a bridge between EDRIS tailoring and casual clothing.

## Design guardrails
- Do not copy adidas three-stripe placement, proprietary panel layouts, recognizable Yeezy cage/upper geometry, or other signature trade dress.
- Do not turn EDRIS into a sportswear brand.
- Do not make the sneaker excessively clean or minimal.
- Do not use random straps, fake technical vents, unnecessary metal or cyberpunk details.
- The goal is **retro athletic energy civilized through EDRIS architecture**.

## Future sneaker family
If a second sneaker is eventually justified, it should occupy a clearly different role from THE VECTOR rather than becoming a colorway exercise. Possible direction: a more refined court/terrace-derived silhouette, still architectural and dark, but only after THE VECTOR is proven.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/DATA_MODEL.md

# TUX GEN — DATA MODEL v1

## Client
client_id, identity, contact, consent_status, created_at

## Body Profile
profile_id, client_id, measurement_set_version, measurements, posture_notes, date

## Pattern
pattern_id, product_id, client_id, block_version, alteration_version, status

## Fitting
fitting_id, client_id, garment_id, stage, date, notes, corrections, approval

## Garment
garment_id, product_id, serial, material_lot, hardware_set, maker, QC_status

## Provenance
object_id, product_family, materials, craft_record, edition, care, repair, alteration, authenticity, archive

## Rule
Every change creates a version. Never overwrite an approved measurement, pattern, fitting or provenance record.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/001_SUPPLIER_PACK.md

# EDRIS 001 — SUPPLIER / MAKER PACK

## Required inputs
- Approved technical master
- Approved body measurement sheet
- Approved toile corrections
- Fabric specification and approved swatch
- Black satin facing specification
- Aged-silver hardware specification
- Thread / lining / canvas specification

## Sample gates
A: block/toile — fit only
B: corrected toile — fit + proportion
C: canvas sample — construction + fit
D: golden sample — production reference

## Supplier must return
- sample photos
- construction notes
- deviations
- material substitutions
- labor estimate
- lead time
- unit cost

No substitution becomes approved by silence. Every deviation requires written approval and version update.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/CHECKOUT_SPEC.md

# EDRIS COMMERCE — LIVE CHECKOUT SPEC

## Customer
account, guest checkout, consent, addresses, saved preferences

## Order
order_id, customer_id, line_items, variant, quantity, price_snapshot, tax, shipping, status

## Inventory
SKU, location, available, reserved, allocated, sold, return_hold

## Payments
Use a PCI-compliant external payment provider. Store provider reference, not raw card data.

## Fulfillment
payment confirmed → inventory reserved → pick/pack → provenance attached → shipment created → tracking → delivered

## Returns
return request → eligibility → authorization → inspection → refund/exchange → inventory disposition


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/COMMERCE_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.

## Update — Retro sneaker direction
THE VECTOR has been revised as the EDRIS signature casual sneaker. Permanent preference: retro-technical/chunky athletic proportions inspired by the energy of adidas Originals Adi2000/Niteball II and Yeezy 700 MNVN, translated into original EDRIS architecture. No copying of signature trade dress.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/020_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---

const products = [
  {id:'001',name:'ONE OF ONE TUXEDO',category:'FORMAL',tier:'O1',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Midnight / forest-black wool. One button. 12 cm straight peak. Full canvas.'},
  {id:'COLUMN',name:'THE COLUMN',category:'TAILORING',tier:'CORE / MTM',world:'TAILORING',desc:'Long controlled body, high armhole, straight architectural peak.'},
  {id:'STRUCTURE',name:'THE STRUCTURE SHIRT',category:'SHIRTS',tier:'CORE / SIGNATURE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Concealed placket, architectural collar, controlled body.'},
  {id:'FOUNDATION',name:'THE FOUNDATION TEE',category:'CASUAL',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Heavy compact cotton, strong shoulder, elongated straight body.'},
  {id:'DENIM',name:'THE DISTRICT DENIM',category:'DENIM',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Near-black heavy denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered.'},
  {id:'RUIN',name:'THE RUIN',category:'OUTERWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'WEATHER',desc:'Below-knee black wool, controlled shoulder, high collar.'},
  {id:'MONUMENT',name:'THE MONUMENT',category:'FOOTWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Formal wholecut built as the natural partner to 001.'},
  {id:'DOSSIER',name:'THE DOSSIER',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'TRAVEL',desc:'Rigid black architectural briefcase with mechanical lock.'},
  {id:'RABBIT',name:'THE RABBIT',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'ICON',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Substantial aged sterling silver house icon.'},
  {id:'SEAL',name:'THE SEAL',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Quiet architectural brooch for tailoring.'},
  {id:'OPERATIVE',name:'THE OPERATIVE',category:'HOROLOGY',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'TIME',desc:'40–41 mm automatic instrument watch refined for formal wear.'},
  {id:'RAIN',name:'001 AFTER THE RAIN',category:'FRAGRANCE',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'FRAGRANCE',desc:'Cardamom × vetiver × mineral rain. Civilization reclaimed by nature.'},
  {id:'BOW12',name:'THE 12 CM BOW',category:'ACCESSORIES',tier:'ICON',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Oversized architectural butterfly bow in black silk.'},
  {id:'VAULT',name:'THE VAULT',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Personal-object case for watch, jewelry, fragrance, passport and keys.'}
];
const recommendations={001:['STRUCTURE','BOW12','SEAL','MONUMENT','OPERATIVE','RAIN','VAULT'],COLUMN:['STRUCTURE','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','OPERATIVE'],STRUCTURE:['COLUMN','001','DENIM','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','RAIN'],RUIN:['STRUCTURE','FOUNDATION','OPERATIVE','VAULT'],RAIN:['001','RUIN','MONSOON','VAULT']};
let cart=[];
function render(list=products){document.querySelector('#catalogue').innerHTML=list.map(p=>`<article class="product" data-id="${p.id}"><div class="product-code">${p.tier}</div><h3>${p.name}</h3><p>${p.desc}</p><small>${p.category} · ${p.world}</small><button onclick="openProduct('${p.id}')">ENTER OBJECT</button></article>`).join('')}
function openProduct(id){const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);const ids=recommendations[id]||[];const rec=ids.map(x=>products.find(y=>y.id===x)).filter(Boolean);document.querySelector('#modal').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-inner"><button class="close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button><div class="label">${p.tier} / ${p.world}</div><h2>${p.name}</h2><p>${p.desc}</p><p class="rule">DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE</p><button class="cta" onclick="addToBuild('${p.id}')">ADD TO BUILD</button><h4>THE EDRIS WORLD</h4><div class="rec-grid">${rec.map(r=>`<button onclick="openProduct('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.querySelector('#modal').classList.add('show')}
function closeModal(){document.querySelector('#modal').classList.remove('show')}
function addToBuild(id){if(!cart.includes(id))cart.push(id);document.querySelector('#buildCount').textContent=cart.length;openBuild()}
function openBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.add('show');document.querySelector('#buildItems').innerHTML=cart.map(id=>{const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<div><span>${p.name}</span><button onclick="cart=cart.filter(x=>x!=='${id}');openBuild()">REMOVE</button></div>`}).join('')||'<p>Your build is empty.</p>'}
function closeBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.remove('show')}
document.querySelector('#search').addEventListener('input',e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render(products.filter(p=>Object.values(p).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)))});
document.querySelector('#filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{const q=e.target.value;render(q==='ALL'?products:products.filter(p=>p.category===q||p.world===q))});
document.querySelector('#buildButton').onclick=openBuild;
render();

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.

## Sneaker discovery
THE VECTOR is intentionally presented as a full EDRIS casual sneaker, not a minimalist luxury trainer. Product discovery should communicate its retro-technical, chunky, layered character while preserving EDRIS's dark architectural identity.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TAILOR_CUTTING_SHEET_FA.md

# EDRIS 001 — برگه فنی برش و خیاطی

## مشخصات قفل‌شده
- کت: تک‌دکمه، یقه آرشال نوک‌تیز، عرض ۱۲ سانتی‌متر، خط یقه کاملاً مستقیم و معماری‌وار.
- پارچه: پشم لوکس مشکی-نیمه‌شب / forest-black.
- ساختار: فول‌کَنواس، horsehair chest، سرشانه ساختاریافته، armhole بالا.
- جیب: دو جیب فیلتابی دوتایی.
- پشت: دو چاک.
- پیراهن: سفید، پنبه رسمی ظریف، یقه رسمی، French cuff، جلوی تمیز/پنهان، studs نقره‌ای کهنه.
- شلوار: فاق بلند، جلوی صاف، side adjuster، بدون belt loop، نوار ساتن مشکی، خط اتوی قوی.

## اندازه‌های بدن ثبت‌شده
1. 49 cm
2. 54 cm
3. 30 cm
4. 56 cm
5. 105 cm
6. 73 cm
7. 35 cm
8. 18 cm
9. 70 cm — سرشانه تا مچ

## ترتیب ساخت
1. اندازه‌گیری بدن و کنترل نقاط مبنا
2. ترسیم بلوک پایه
3. افزودن ease طراحی
4. ترسیم یقه و rever با خط مستقیم
5. toile اول
6. پرو اول و ثبت اصلاحات
7. toile دوم
8. ساخت نمونه canvas
9. پرو نهایی
10. golden sample
11. QC
12. تولید

## قانون یقه
خط بیرونی peak lapel نباید قوس‌دار به نظر برسد. شکست یقه، roll و نقطه peak باید با خطوط کنترل‌شده و هندسی ساخته شوند. ۱۲ سانتی‌متر سقف عرض است و بیشتر از آن مجاز نیست.

## سخت‌گیری خیاطی
هیچ تغییر سلیقه‌ای در عرض یقه، جای دکمه، فرم شانه، طول بدن یا rise بدون ثبت نسخه جدید مجاز نیست.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/FOOTWEAR_SNEAKER_DIRECTION.md

# EDRIS Footwear — Retro Technical Sneaker Direction

## New permanent preference
EDRIS casual footwear should include a genuine retro-technical sneaker language inspired by the user's love of adidas Originals-era silhouettes such as the Adi2000 and Niteball II, and the sculptural technical language associated with the Yeezy 700 MNVN.

These are **references for energy and proportion, not templates to copy**.

## THE VECTOR — REVISED CORE SNEAKER

**Role:** EDRIS's signature everyday sneaker.

**Design character:** retro-futurist, chunky, architectural, slightly strange, luxurious but not precious.

### Silhouette
- Full, substantial retro sneaker volume rather than a minimal low-profile trainer.
- Layered upper with controlled panel geometry.
- Strong heel structure.
- Generous padded tongue and collar.
- Sculptural midsole with visible architectural mass.
- Moderately wide forefoot and stable stance.
- Athletic proportions, refined enough to sit beside tailoring.

### EDRIS translation
- Near-black / black / washed charcoal as the primary palette.
- Secondary forest-black, mineral grey and controlled ivory editions.
- Matte technical mesh or woven textile combined with smooth leather/suede overlays.
- One aged-silver or blackened-metal hardware detail only where functionally justified.
- House vertical-incision geometry hidden or subtly integrated into a panel, lace keeper or heel detail.
- Masked Rabbit remains hidden; never make the sneaker look like merchandise.
- No oversized external EDRIS logo.
- Identity must work from silhouette, panel architecture, material contrast and sole geometry.

### Sole
- Thick but disciplined.
- Layered/segmented construction with an Art Deco-like stepped rhythm.
- Black rubber outsole.
- No fake futuristic effects, transparent gimmicks or excessive mechanical detailing.

### Styling
THE VECTOR should work with:
- FOUNDATION TEE
- STRUCTURE SHIRT
- DISTRICT DENIM
- OBSIDIAN
- MONSOON
- AFTERHOURS
- RUIN

It should deliberately create a bridge between EDRIS tailoring and casual clothing.

## Design guardrails
- Do not copy adidas three-stripe placement, proprietary panel layouts, recognizable Yeezy cage/upper geometry, or other signature trade dress.
- Do not turn EDRIS into a sportswear brand.
- Do not make the sneaker excessively clean or minimal.
- Do not use random straps, fake technical vents, unnecessary metal or cyberpunk details.
- The goal is **retro athletic energy civilized through EDRIS architecture**.

## Future sneaker family
If a second sneaker is eventually justified, it should occupy a clearly different role from THE VECTOR rather than becoming a colorway exercise. Possible direction: a more refined court/terrace-derived silhouette, still architectural and dark, but only after THE VECTOR is proven.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/DATA_MODEL.md

# TUX GEN — DATA MODEL v1

## Client
client_id, identity, contact, consent_status, created_at

## Body Profile
profile_id, client_id, measurement_set_version, measurements, posture_notes, date

## Pattern
pattern_id, product_id, client_id, block_version, alteration_version, status

## Fitting
fitting_id, client_id, garment_id, stage, date, notes, corrections, approval

## Garment
garment_id, product_id, serial, material_lot, hardware_set, maker, QC_status

## Provenance
object_id, product_family, materials, craft_record, edition, care, repair, alteration, authenticity, archive

## Rule
Every change creates a version. Never overwrite an approved measurement, pattern, fitting or provenance record.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/001_SUPPLIER_PACK.md

# EDRIS 001 — SUPPLIER / MAKER PACK

## Required inputs
- Approved technical master
- Approved body measurement sheet
- Approved toile corrections
- Fabric specification and approved swatch
- Black satin facing specification
- Aged-silver hardware specification
- Thread / lining / canvas specification

## Sample gates
A: block/toile — fit only
B: corrected toile — fit + proportion
C: canvas sample — construction + fit
D: golden sample — production reference

## Supplier must return
- sample photos
- construction notes
- deviations
- material substitutions
- labor estimate
- lead time
- unit cost

No substitution becomes approved by silence. Every deviation requires written approval and version update.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/CHECKOUT_SPEC.md

# EDRIS COMMERCE — LIVE CHECKOUT SPEC

## Customer
account, guest checkout, consent, addresses, saved preferences

## Order
order_id, customer_id, line_items, variant, quantity, price_snapshot, tax, shipping, status

## Inventory
SKU, location, available, reserved, allocated, sold, return_hold

## Payments
Use a PCI-compliant external payment provider. Store provider reference, not raw card data.

## Fulfillment
payment confirmed → inventory reserved → pick/pack → provenance attached → shipment created → tracking → delivered

## Returns
return request → eligibility → authorization → inspection → refund/exchange → inventory disposition


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/COMMERCE_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/GO_LIVE_CHECKLIST.md

# EDRIS GO-LIVE CHECKLIST

## Digital
[ ] Responsive QA
[ ] Accessibility pass
[ ] Product data complete
[ ] Search/filter QA
[ ] Build/recommendation QA
[ ] Account QA
[ ] Checkout sandbox QA
[ ] Analytics QA
[ ] Error pages
[ ] Security review

## Physical
[ ] Golden sample approved
[ ] Materials secured
[ ] QC standard signed
[ ] Packaging approved
[ ] Care labels/documentation
[ ] Provenance record
[ ] Inventory received
[ ] Photography complete

## Commercial
[ ] Pricing approved
[ ] Tax setup
[ ] Shipping zones/rates
[ ] Returns policy
[ ] Privacy/consent
[ ] Terms
[ ] Customer support

## Launch sequence
PRIVATE CLIENTS → CONTROLLED ORDER WINDOW → FEEDBACK → CORRECTION → PUBLIC RELEASE


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.

## Update — Retro sneaker direction
THE VECTOR has been revised as the EDRIS signature casual sneaker. Permanent preference: retro-technical/chunky athletic proportions inspired by the energy of adidas Originals Adi2000/Niteball II and Yeezy 700 MNVN, translated into original EDRIS architecture. No copying of signature trade dress.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/021_EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/website/app.js

const products = [
  {id:'001',name:'ONE OF ONE TUXEDO',category:'FORMAL',tier:'O1',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Midnight / forest-black wool. One button. 12 cm straight peak. Full canvas.'},
  {id:'COLUMN',name:'THE COLUMN',category:'TAILORING',tier:'CORE / MTM',world:'TAILORING',desc:'Long controlled body, high armhole, straight architectural peak.'},
  {id:'STRUCTURE',name:'THE STRUCTURE SHIRT',category:'SHIRTS',tier:'CORE / SIGNATURE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Concealed placket, architectural collar, controlled body.'},
  {id:'FOUNDATION',name:'THE FOUNDATION TEE',category:'CASUAL',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Heavy compact cotton, strong shoulder, elongated straight body.'},
  {id:'DENIM',name:'THE DISTRICT DENIM',category:'DENIM',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Near-black heavy denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered.'},
  {id:'RUIN',name:'THE RUIN',category:'OUTERWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'WEATHER',desc:'Below-knee black wool, controlled shoulder, high collar.'},
  {id:'MONUMENT',name:'THE MONUMENT',category:'FOOTWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Formal wholecut built as the natural partner to 001.'},
  {id:'DOSSIER',name:'THE DOSSIER',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'TRAVEL',desc:'Rigid black architectural briefcase with mechanical lock.'},
  {id:'RABBIT',name:'THE RABBIT',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'ICON',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Substantial aged sterling silver house icon.'},
  {id:'SEAL',name:'THE SEAL',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Quiet architectural brooch for tailoring.'},
  {id:'OPERATIVE',name:'THE OPERATIVE',category:'HOROLOGY',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'TIME',desc:'40–41 mm automatic instrument watch refined for formal wear.'},
  {id:'RAIN',name:'001 AFTER THE RAIN',category:'FRAGRANCE',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'FRAGRANCE',desc:'Cardamom × vetiver × mineral rain. Civilization reclaimed by nature.'},
  {id:'BOW12',name:'THE 12 CM BOW',category:'ACCESSORIES',tier:'ICON',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Oversized architectural butterfly bow in black silk.'},
  {id:'VAULT',name:'THE VAULT',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Personal-object case for watch, jewelry, fragrance, passport and keys.'}
];
const recommendations={001:['STRUCTURE','BOW12','SEAL','MONUMENT','OPERATIVE','RAIN','VAULT'],COLUMN:['STRUCTURE','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','OPERATIVE'],STRUCTURE:['COLUMN','001','DENIM','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','RAIN'],RUIN:['STRUCTURE','FOUNDATION','OPERATIVE','VAULT'],RAIN:['001','RUIN','MONSOON','VAULT']};
let cart=[];
function render(list=products){document.querySelector('#catalogue').innerHTML=list.map(p=>`<article class="product" data-id="${p.id}"><div class="product-code">${p.tier}</div><h3>${p.name}</h3><p>${p.desc}</p><small>${p.category} · ${p.world}</small><button onclick="openProduct('${p.id}')">ENTER OBJECT</button></article>`).join('')}
function openProduct(id){const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);const ids=recommendations[id]||[];const rec=ids.map(x=>products.find(y=>y.id===x)).filter(Boolean);document.querySelector('#modal').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-inner"><button class="close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button><div class="label">${p.tier} / ${p.world}</div><h2>${p.name}</h2><p>${p.desc}</p><p class="rule">DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE</p><button class="cta" onclick="addToBuild('${p.id}')">ADD TO BUILD</button><h4>THE EDRIS WORLD</h4><div class="rec-grid">${rec.map(r=>`<button onclick="openProduct('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.querySelector('#modal').classList.add('show')}
function closeModal(){document.querySelector('#modal').classList.remove('show')}
function addToBuild(id){if(!cart.includes(id))cart.push(id);document.querySelector('#buildCount').textContent=cart.length;openBuild()}
function openBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.add('show');document.querySelector('#buildItems').innerHTML=cart.map(id=>{const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<div><span>${p.name}</span><button onclick="cart=cart.filter(x=>x!=='${id}');openBuild()">REMOVE</button></div>`}).join('')||'<p>Your build is empty.</p>'}
function closeBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.remove('show')}
document.querySelector('#search').addEventListener('input',e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render(products.filter(p=>Object.values(p).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)))});
document.querySelector('#filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{const q=e.target.value;render(q==='ALL'?products:products.filter(p=>p.category===q||p.world===q))});
document.querySelector('#buildButton').onclick=openBuild;
render();

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/031_TUX-GEN-uncle-eds-prototype/TUX-GEN/README.md

# TUX GEN — Uncle Ed's

Portable prototype of the Uncle Ed's Digital Tailoring System.

## Included
- Body profile with confidence-aware measurements
- Guided front/side/back scan workflow
- Scan pipeline: framing → landmarks → segmentation → scale → anthropometrics → posture/asymmetry → confidence → verification
- Scan simulation mode
- 001 Design Studio
- Proportion / finished-measurement engine
- Textile Lab
- Tailor Mode + print specification
- JSON export
- Responsive UI
- Honest production-scan boundary

## Run
Node.js 18+:

    npm install
    npm run dev

## Body scan reality
The scan workflow is implemented, but no claim is made that browser 2D pose alone is tailor-grade. Production requires camera capture, segmentation, calibration, multi-view 3D reconstruction and a validated anthropometric solver. This project provides the integration contract and UI around those components.

## Locked 001 rules
- 001 = highest designation / One of One
- 12 cm maximum lapel
- 4.5 cm waistband target / 5 cm absolute maximum
- Structured Boxy Hollywood fit
- Crossover single-button architecture
- White formal shirt
- Large black satin butterfly bow tie
- Aged silver hardware
- Deep black 280–320 g/m² wool
- Black/deep forest cupro with subtle oxblood pathways

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/032_TUX-GEN-v0.2-complete/TUX-GEN/README.md

# TUX GEN — Uncle Ed's v0.2

This is the expanded end-to-end product prototype for **UNCLE ED'S — By Edris Nazparvar** and the TUX GEN Digital Tailoring System.

## Implemented UI/UX

Home / house console; onboarding; Quick/Guided/Bespoke mode choice; body profile; editable measurement database; guided scan workflow; scan quality; front/side/back stages; confidence model; body model; posture/asymmetry visualization; Design Studio; parametric garment preview; fit/proportion engine; Textile Lab; cost planning; Pattern Lab; tailor mode; fitting/version workflow; QC checklist; wardrobe; production stages; certificate; settings/privacy shell; JSON garment export.

## Locked 001 rules

- 001 = highest designation / ONE OF ONE, not first production model.
- 12 cm maximum lapel width.
- 4.5 cm trouser waistband target; 5 cm absolute maximum.
- Structured Boxy Hollywood fit.
- Crossover single-button architecture.
- Straight architectural peak-lapel edge.
- White formal shirt.
- Large black satin butterfly bow tie.
- Aged silver house hardware.
- Deep black 280–320 g/m² wool with subtle green depth.
- Black/deep forest cupro lining with hidden oxblood pathways.
- Nothing militaristic on the surface; purposeful engineering underneath.

## What is genuinely functional now

The UI is stateful during the session, controls update the garment model, measurements are editable, derived measurements update, scan workflow advances, simulated scan changes confidence/source, revisions increment, pattern manifest exports, garment JSON exports, and print views are available.

## The hard limits

The following are intentionally **not faked**:

1. Production-grade phone-only body scanning / 3D reconstruction.
2. Validated anthropometric solver from camera imagery.
3. True physical cloth simulation and production 3D garment drape.
4. A validated 1:1 pattern-drafting solver with real blocks and industrial grading.
5. DXF/AAMA/ASTM production pattern export.
6. Real supplier/fabric inventory, pricing and payment integrations.
7. Cloud authentication, encrypted body-data storage and production privacy infrastructure.
8. Real atelier/tailor network and order fulfillment.

The interfaces and data contracts for these capabilities are already represented so the engines can be plugged in rather than redesigning the product.

## Run

Node.js 18+ recommended:

```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

## Export

Use the Export button to download the current TUX GEN garment record as JSON. Pattern Lab can export a pattern manifest.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/033_TUX-GEN-v0.3-digital-atelier/README.md

# TUX GEN v0.3 — The Digital Atelier

A refined Uncle Ed's UI/UX layer for the TUX GEN digital tailoring system.

## Design direction
- Obsidian / deep forest / ivory / aged silver as the core palette
- Oxblood used as a discovery accent rather than a dominant UI color
- Editorial serif + technical sans/monospace hierarchy
- Atelier / Instrument / Archive visual language
- Restrained motion and drafting-line details
- Mobile responsive and reduced-motion aware

## Run
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

## What remains an external engineering boundary
- production-grade phone body scanning / multi-view 3D reconstruction
- physically accurate cloth/garment simulation
- validated production 1:1 pattern solver and DXF/AAMA/ASTM output
- cloud authentication, encrypted persistence, payments, vendors and tailor network

The interface is designed so those engines can be connected without redesigning the user journey.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/034_TUX-GEN-v0.4-psychological-atelier/TUX-GEN-v0.3/README.md

# TUX GEN v0.4 — The Psychological Atelier

UNCLE ED'S — By Edris Nazparvar

## 001 — ONE OF ONE

The Legacy Tuxedo now carries a psychological design layer: intelligence, composure, observation, cultivation, mystery, and controlled danger. The reference is psychological rather than literal: no costume imitation, no theatrical menace, and nothing militaristic on the surface.

### 001 design lock
- Structured Boxy Hollywood silhouette
- Crossover single-button architecture
- 12 cm straight peak lapel; maximum width
- High gorge
- Black satin facing
- Architectural shoulder, high armhole, full canvas
- Long double vents
- Deepest black wool with subtle green depth
- White formal shirt
- Large black butterfly bow tie
- Aged-silver house hardware / 001 button
- High-rise flat-front trousers
- 4.5 cm waistband target; 5 cm absolute maximum
- Controlled taper, minimal break
- Black / forest cupro lining with restrained oxblood pathways

### Psychological design system
The Design Studio now includes:
- Presence
- Visibility
- Detail philosophy
- Composure
- Danger language

The system translates these into a visual rule: **nothing looks accidental**. The exterior remains restrained; complexity is revealed at close range.

### Still genuine external engineering boundaries
- Production-grade phone body scanning / multi-view 3D reconstruction
- Physically accurate cloth simulation
- Validated production 1:1 pattern solver and DXF/AAMA/ASTM output
- Cloud authentication, encrypted persistence, payments, vendors and tailor network

This prototype intentionally does not fake those capabilities.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/035_TUX-GEN-v1.0-house-redesign/TUX-GEN-v1.0/README.md

# TUX GEN 1.0 — UNCLE ED'S HOUSE SYSTEM

A complete redesign of TUX GEN around the UNCLE ED'S house ideology rather than a collection of tailoring utilities.

## House thesis
**The body is nature. The mind is civilization.**

Nature → Life → Desire → Civilization → Creation → Decay → Death → Nature.

TUX GEN is the digital instrument of an old, extremely sophisticated tailoring house that happens to have been designed in 2026.

## Product architecture
- HOUSE — philosophy, culture, visual language, rules
- 001 — the canonical One of One garment
- ATELIER — garment architecture and design decisions
- PSYCHOLOGY — presence / composure / mystery / danger translated into tailoring
- FIT — body truth, confidence and finished measurements
- MATERIAL — textile, lining, hardware and light behavior
- TAILOR — production-facing specification
- ARCHIVE — memory, versions, fittings, repairs and provenance

## House rules
1. Nothing looks accidental.
2. The outside creates intrigue. The inside rewards curiosity.
3. Function must have a reason.
4. Luxury is proportion, material, craft and time—not decoration.
5. Influence is digested, never copied.
6. Nothing militaristic on the surface. Everything purposeful underneath.
7. Death is expressed through memory and impermanence, not gothic decoration.

## 001
UNCLE ED'S 001 — ONE OF ONE — 2026
The Legacy Tuxedo.

Structured boxy Hollywood fit; crossover single-button architecture; 12 cm straight peak lapels; aged-silver house hardware; black satin large butterfly bow; full canvas; high armhole; high-rise trousers; 4.5 cm finished waistband; deep black 280–320 g/m² wool; black/deep-forest lining with hidden oxblood pathways.

## Cultural synthesis
Qaemshahr → Tehran → Paris → Rome → Los Angeles → New York → wherever relevant.

Iranian/Turkic/Mazandarani roots are treated as source material for sensibility, not surface decoration: forest, rain, architecture, craftsmanship, cinema, hospitality, modernity, geometry and memory.

## Product family
The same house language eventually governs tailoring, fragrance, leather goods, hardware, shirts, formal accessories and digital tools. The aged-silver button is the physical logo and hardware family anchor.

## Development note
This is a product/UX prototype and house-system foundation, not production-grade computer-vision scanning or mathematical pattern drafting. Those require dedicated CV/3D and pattern-engineering implementations.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/01_DIGITAL_HOUSE/ARCHITECTURE.md

# Digital House Architecture

## Primary navigation
SHOP · TAILORING · OBJECTS · JEWELRY · WATCHES · FRAGRANCE · TRAVEL · HOUSE · ARCHIVE · ATELIER

## Product page modules
1. Object identity
2. Story
3. Materials / construction
4. WEAR WITH
5. COMPLETE THE OBJECT
6. THE EDRIS WORLD
7. NEXT OBJECT
8. Provenance
9. Care / repair
10. Bespoke or acquisition CTA

## Core entities
Product, ProductFamily, Material, Hardware, Client, MeasurementSet, PatternVersion, Order, Fitting, ProvenanceRecord, CareRecord, RepairRecord, ArchiveRecord.

## Technical stack recommendation
Frontend: Next.js + TypeScript. Styling: CSS variables/design tokens. Commerce: headless commerce layer. Data: PostgreSQL. Media: object storage/CDN. Auth: managed identity provider. Payments/shipping/tax: region-appropriate providers. Admin: role-based internal console.

## Non-negotiable UX
No logo wall. No forced cross-sell. Recommendation engine must feel editorial and useful. Mobile-first. Accessibility and performance are release gates.

## Sneaker discovery
THE VECTOR is intentionally presented as a full EDRIS casual sneaker, not a minimalist luxury trainer. Product discovery should communicate its retro-technical, chunky, layered character while preserving EDRIS's dark architectural identity.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TAILOR_CUTTING_SHEET_FA.md

# EDRIS 001 — برگه فنی برش و خیاطی

## مشخصات قفل‌شده
- کت: تک‌دکمه، یقه آرشال نوک‌تیز، عرض ۱۲ سانتی‌متر، خط یقه کاملاً مستقیم و معماری‌وار.
- پارچه: پشم لوکس مشکی-نیمه‌شب / forest-black.
- ساختار: فول‌کَنواس، horsehair chest، سرشانه ساختاریافته، armhole بالا.
- جیب: دو جیب فیلتابی دوتایی.
- پشت: دو چاک.
- پیراهن: سفید، پنبه رسمی ظریف، یقه رسمی، French cuff، جلوی تمیز/پنهان، studs نقره‌ای کهنه.
- شلوار: فاق بلند، جلوی صاف، side adjuster، بدون belt loop، نوار ساتن مشکی، خط اتوی قوی.

## اندازه‌های بدن ثبت‌شده
1. 49 cm
2. 54 cm
3. 30 cm
4. 56 cm
5. 105 cm
6. 73 cm
7. 35 cm
8. 18 cm
9. 70 cm — سرشانه تا مچ

## ترتیب ساخت
1. اندازه‌گیری بدن و کنترل نقاط مبنا
2. ترسیم بلوک پایه
3. افزودن ease طراحی
4. ترسیم یقه و rever با خط مستقیم
5. toile اول
6. پرو اول و ثبت اصلاحات
7. toile دوم
8. ساخت نمونه canvas
9. پرو نهایی
10. golden sample
11. QC
12. تولید

## قانون یقه
خط بیرونی peak lapel نباید قوس‌دار به نظر برسد. شکست یقه، roll و نقطه peak باید با خطوط کنترل‌شده و هندسی ساخته شوند. ۱۲ سانتی‌متر سقف عرض است و بیشتر از آن مجاز نیست.

## سخت‌گیری خیاطی
هیچ تغییر سلیقه‌ای در عرض یقه، جای دکمه، فرم شانه، طول بدن یا rise بدون ثبت نسخه جدید مجاز نیست.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/001/001_TECHNICAL_MASTER.md

# EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO
## Locked design
Midnight / forest-black luxury wool. Single-breasted, one button. Long Hollywood body. Sculpted Hollywood shoulder. High armhole. Full canvas. Horsehair chest. Structured shoulder and sleeve head. Double-jetted pockets. Double vents. 12 cm STRAIGHT peak lapel. Deep black satin facing.

## Shirt
White fine formal cotton. Formal collar. French cuffs. Concealed/clean front. Aged-silver studs.

## Accessories
Large architectural black butterfly bow. White boutonniere. White pocket square. Aged-silver buttons, cufflinks and studs.

## Trouser
High-rise flat front. Side adjusters. No belt loops. Black satin side stripe. Strong crease. Controlled taper.

## Body measurements supplied
49 cm; 54 cm; 30 cm; 56 cm; 105 cm; 73 cm; 35 cm; 18 cm; 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist.

## Critical engineering rule
Lapel line must read STRAIGHT and architectural, not curved. 12 cm is the maximum approved width.

## Prototype sequence
Draft block → toile → first fitting → correction → second fitting → canvas sample → golden sample → QC → production.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/FOOTWEAR_SNEAKER_DIRECTION.md

# EDRIS Footwear — Retro Technical Sneaker Direction

## New permanent preference
EDRIS casual footwear should include a genuine retro-technical sneaker language inspired by the user's love of adidas Originals-era silhouettes such as the Adi2000 and Niteball II, and the sculptural technical language associated with the Yeezy 700 MNVN.

These are **references for energy and proportion, not templates to copy**.

## THE VECTOR — REVISED CORE SNEAKER

**Role:** EDRIS's signature everyday sneaker.

**Design character:** retro-futurist, chunky, architectural, slightly strange, luxurious but not precious.

### Silhouette
- Full, substantial retro sneaker volume rather than a minimal low-profile trainer.
- Layered upper with controlled panel geometry.
- Strong heel structure.
- Generous padded tongue and collar.
- Sculptural midsole with visible architectural mass.
- Moderately wide forefoot and stable stance.
- Athletic proportions, refined enough to sit beside tailoring.

### EDRIS translation
- Near-black / black / washed charcoal as the primary palette.
- Secondary forest-black, mineral grey and controlled ivory editions.
- Matte technical mesh or woven textile combined with smooth leather/suede overlays.
- One aged-silver or blackened-metal hardware detail only where functionally justified.
- House vertical-incision geometry hidden or subtly integrated into a panel, lace keeper or heel detail.
- Masked Rabbit remains hidden; never make the sneaker look like merchandise.
- No oversized external EDRIS logo.
- Identity must work from silhouette, panel architecture, material contrast and sole geometry.

### Sole
- Thick but disciplined.
- Layered/segmented construction with an Art Deco-like stepped rhythm.
- Black rubber outsole.
- No fake futuristic effects, transparent gimmicks or excessive mechanical detailing.

### Styling
THE VECTOR should work with:
- FOUNDATION TEE
- STRUCTURE SHIRT
- DISTRICT DENIM
- OBSIDIAN
- MONSOON
- AFTERHOURS
- RUIN

It should deliberately create a bridge between EDRIS tailoring and casual clothing.

## Design guardrails
- Do not copy adidas three-stripe placement, proprietary panel layouts, recognizable Yeezy cage/upper geometry, or other signature trade dress.
- Do not turn EDRIS into a sportswear brand.
- Do not make the sneaker excessively clean or minimal.
- Do not use random straps, fake technical vents, unnecessary metal or cyberpunk details.
- The goal is **retro athletic energy civilized through EDRIS architecture**.

## Future sneaker family
If a second sneaker is eventually justified, it should occupy a clearly different role from THE VECTOR rather than becoming a colorway exercise. Possible direction: a more refined court/terrace-derived silhouette, still architectural and dark, but only after THE VECTOR is proven.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/02_PRODUCT_ENGINEERING/PRODUCT_RECORD_STANDARD.md

# EDRIS Product Record Standard v1.0

Every SKU gets: Product ID; family; tier; world; silhouette; intended use; approved materials; hardware; colorways; construction; measurements/spec; tolerances; BOM; supplier/maker; prototype version; golden sample; QC; packaging; care; provenance; photography; copy; pricing; inventory; release status.

Gate: DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/DATA_MODEL.md

# TUX GEN — DATA MODEL v1

## Client
client_id, identity, contact, consent_status, created_at

## Body Profile
profile_id, client_id, measurement_set_version, measurements, posture_notes, date

## Pattern
pattern_id, product_id, client_id, block_version, alteration_version, status

## Fitting
fitting_id, client_id, garment_id, stage, date, notes, corrections, approval

## Garment
garment_id, product_id, serial, material_lot, hardware_set, maker, QC_status

## Provenance
object_id, product_family, materials, craft_record, edition, care, repair, alteration, authenticity, archive

## Rule
Every change creates a version. Never overwrite an approved measurement, pattern, fitting or provenance record.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/03_TUX_GEN/OPERATING_SYSTEM.md

# TUX GEN — Internal Bespoke Operating System

## Client lifecycle
CONSULTATION → MEASUREMENT → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FITTING → CONSTRUCTION → REVEAL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → REORDER

## Core records
Client; MeasurementSet(versioned); PatternBlock(versioned); DesignSpec; MaterialSelection; HardwareSelection; FittingSession; Alteration; ConstructionRecord; GoldenSample; Order; Provenance; Care; Repair; Archive.

## Measurement rule
Never overwrite a historical measurement set. Every change creates a new dated version with source, fitter and notes.

## Privacy
Collect only what is required for the service. Restrict staff access by role. Client records must have retention and deletion policies appropriate to applicable law.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/001_SUPPLIER_PACK.md

# EDRIS 001 — SUPPLIER / MAKER PACK

## Required inputs
- Approved technical master
- Approved body measurement sheet
- Approved toile corrections
- Fabric specification and approved swatch
- Black satin facing specification
- Aged-silver hardware specification
- Thread / lining / canvas specification

## Sample gates
A: block/toile — fit only
B: corrected toile — fit + proportion
C: canvas sample — construction + fit
D: golden sample — production reference

## Supplier must return
- sample photos
- construction notes
- deviations
- material substitutions
- labor estimate
- lead time
- unit cost

No substitution becomes approved by silence. Every deviation requires written approval and version update.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/04_PRODUCTION/PRODUCTION_SYSTEM.md

# EDRIS Production System

## Supplier packet
Material specification · color standard · weight/GSM where applicable · construction sheet · hardware drawing · tolerance sheet · packaging sheet · QC checklist · sample approval form.

## Production gates
1 Design approval
2 Material approval
3 Prototype approval
4 Fit/golden sample approval
5 Cost approval
6 Supplier approval
7 Pilot production
8 QC
9 Packaging
10 Release

## QC minimum
Dimensions; seam quality; symmetry; pressing; hardware security; stains/marks; lining; labels; packaging; final visual against golden sample.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/05_COMMERCE/CHECKOUT_SPEC.md

# EDRIS COMMERCE — LIVE CHECKOUT SPEC

## Customer
account, guest checkout, consent, addresses, saved preferences

## Order
order_id, customer_id, line_items, variant, quantity, price_snapshot, tax, shipping, status

## Inventory
SKU, location, available, reserved, allocated, sold, return_hold

## Payments
Use a PCI-compliant external payment provider. Store provider reference, not raw card data.

## Fulfillment
payment confirmed → inventory reserved → pick/pack → provenance attached → shipment created → tracking → delivered

## Returns
return request → eligibility → authorization → inspection → refund/exchange → inventory disposition


---

# EDRIS Commerce System

## Product page recommendation engine
WEAR WITH · COMPLETE THE OBJECT · THE EDRIS WORLD · NEXT OBJECT.

Recommendations are relationship-based, not generic “customers also bought” filler.

## Commerce objects
Catalog; variants; price; inventory; cart; order; payment; shipping; tax; returns; care; repair; provenance.

## Release content gate
Photography + product copy + materials + sizing + care + provenance + pricing + inventory + legal/labeling review before publication.

# EDRIS GO-LIVE CHECKLIST

## Digital
[ ] Responsive QA
[ ] Accessibility pass
[ ] Product data complete
[ ] Search/filter QA
[ ] Build/recommendation QA
[ ] Account QA
[ ] Checkout sandbox QA
[ ] Analytics QA
[ ] Error pages
[ ] Security review

## Physical

[ ] Materials secured

[ ] Packaging approved
[ ] Care labels/documentation
[ ] Provenance record
[ ] Inventory received
[ ] Photography complete

## Commercial

[ ] Tax setup
[ ] Shipping zones/rates
[ ] Returns policy
[ ] Privacy/consent
[ ] Terms
[ ] Customer support

## Launch sequence
PRIVATE CLIENTS → CONTROLLED ORDER WINDOW → FEEDBACK → CORRECTION → PUBLIC RELEASE

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/06_LAUNCH/LAUNCH_GATE.md

# EDRIS Launch Gate

## Private launch
Founder/atelier clients → 001 + Foundation system → controlled orders → fitting feedback → production correction → service rehearsal.

## Public launch
Website QA → mobile QA → checkout test → inventory test → transactional email test → shipping test → returns test → privacy/consent review → product photography → final catalogue → analytics → customer support → release.

## First release principle
Launch a small number of exceptional products correctly rather than exposing the entire designed universe before engineering is complete.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/BUILD_STATUS.md

# EDRIS Build Status — v1.0

| Phase | Build output | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 01 Digital House | Website prototype, IA, design tokens, catalogue data | BUILT — prototype |
| 02 Product Engineering | Product standard + 001 technical master | BUILT — engineering ready |
| 03 TUX GEN | Bespoke OS/data model | BUILT — system specification |
| 04 Production | Supplier packet, gates, QC | BUILT — operating specification |
| 05 Commerce | Recommendation + commerce architecture | BUILT — implementation specification |
| 06 Launch | Private/public launch gates | BUILT — execution checklist |

## Remaining external execution
Live hosting/deployment; payment/tax/shipping accounts; supplier contracts; physical samples; manufacturing; photography; legal review; real inventory; public release.

## Update — Retro sneaker direction
THE VECTOR has been revised as the EDRIS signature casual sneaker. Permanent preference: retro-technical/chunky athletic proportions inspired by the energy of adidas Originals Adi2000/Niteball II and Yeezy 700 MNVN, translated into original EDRIS architecture. No copying of signature trade dress.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/EDRIS_Six_Phase_Build_v4.0/EDRIS_BUILD/README.md

# EDRIS — SIX-PHASE BUILD v1.0

This package converts the EDRIS House ecosystem into an execution-ready build system.

## Phases
1. Digital House — working static website prototype + catalogue data + information architecture.
2. Product Engineering — 001 technical master, product record standard, Foundation engineering queue.
3. TUX GEN — data model and bespoke workflow.
4. Production — BOM/QC/costing/production gates.
5. Commerce — product content, recommendation logic, checkout/inventory architecture.
6. Launch — private-to-public launch gates and operating checklist.

The physical manufacture, supplier contracting, payment accounts, tax registrations and live deployment require real-world services and human/vendor execution.


---
