const products = [
  {id:'001',name:'ONE OF ONE TUXEDO',category:'FORMAL',tier:'O1',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Midnight / forest-black wool. One button. 12 cm straight peak. Full canvas.'},
  {id:'COLUMN',name:'THE COLUMN',category:'TAILORING',tier:'CORE / MTM',world:'TAILORING',desc:'Long controlled body, high armhole, straight architectural peak.'},
  {id:'STRUCTURE',name:'THE STRUCTURE SHIRT',category:'SHIRTS',tier:'CORE / SIGNATURE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Concealed placket, architectural collar, controlled body.'},
  {id:'FOUNDATION',name:'THE FOUNDATION TEE',category:'CASUAL',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Heavy compact cotton, strong shoulder, elongated straight body.'},
  {id:'DENIM',name:'THE DISTRICT DENIM',category:'DENIM',tier:'CORE',world:'EVERYDAY',desc:'Near-black heavy denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered.'},
  {id:'RUIN',name:'THE RUIN',category:'OUTERWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'WEATHER',desc:'Below-knee black wool, controlled shoulder, high collar.'},
  {id:'MONUMENT',name:'THE MONUMENT',category:'FOOTWEAR',tier:'CORE',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Formal wholecut built as the natural partner to 001.'},
  {id:'DOSSIER',name:'THE DOSSIER',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'TRAVEL',desc:'Rigid black architectural briefcase with mechanical lock.'},
  {id:'RABBIT',name:'THE RABBIT',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'ICON',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Substantial aged sterling silver house icon.'},
  {id:'SEAL',name:'THE SEAL',category:'JEWELRY',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Quiet architectural brooch for tailoring.'},
  {id:'OPERATIVE',name:'THE OPERATIVE',category:'HOROLOGY',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'TIME',desc:'40–41 mm automatic instrument watch refined for formal wear.'},
  {id:'RAIN',name:'001 AFTER THE RAIN',category:'FRAGRANCE',tier:'FLAGSHIP',world:'FRAGRANCE',desc:'Cardamom × vetiver × mineral rain. Civilization reclaimed by nature.'},
  {id:'BOW12',name:'THE 12 CM BOW',category:'ACCESSORIES',tier:'ICON',world:'CEREMONY',desc:'Oversized architectural butterfly bow in black silk.'},
  {id:'VAULT',name:'THE VAULT',category:'LEATHER',tier:'CORE',world:'OBJECTS',desc:'Personal-object case for watch, jewelry, fragrance, passport and keys.'}
];
const recommendations={001:['STRUCTURE','BOW12','SEAL','MONUMENT','OPERATIVE','RAIN','VAULT'],COLUMN:['STRUCTURE','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','OPERATIVE'],STRUCTURE:['COLUMN','001','DENIM','SEAL','MONUMENT','DOSSIER','RAIN'],RUIN:['STRUCTURE','FOUNDATION','OPERATIVE','VAULT'],RAIN:['001','RUIN','MONSOON','VAULT']};
let cart=[];
function render(list=products){document.querySelector('#catalogue').innerHTML=list.map(p=>`<article class="product" data-id="${p.id}"><div class="product-code">${p.tier}</div><h3>${p.name}</h3><p>${p.desc}</p><small>${p.category} · ${p.world}</small><button onclick="openProduct('${p.id}')">ENTER OBJECT</button></article>`).join('')}
function openProduct(id){const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);const ids=recommendations[id]||[];const rec=ids.map(x=>products.find(y=>y.id===x)).filter(Boolean);document.querySelector('#modal').innerHTML=`<div class="modal-inner"><button class="close" onclick="closeModal()">×</button><div class="label">${p.tier} / ${p.world}</div><h2>${p.name}</h2><p>${p.desc}</p><p class="rule">DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE</p><button class="cta" onclick="addToBuild('${p.id}')">ADD TO BUILD</button><h4>THE EDRIS WORLD</h4><div class="rec-grid">${rec.map(r=>`<button onclick="openProduct('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;document.querySelector('#modal').classList.add('show')}
function closeModal(){document.querySelector('#modal').classList.remove('show')}
function addToBuild(id){if(!cart.includes(id))cart.push(id);document.querySelector('#buildCount').textContent=cart.length;openBuild()}
function openBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.add('show');document.querySelector('#buildItems').innerHTML=cart.map(id=>{const p=products.find(x=>x.id===id);return `<div><span>${p.name}</span><button onclick="cart=cart.filter(x=>x!=='${id}');openBuild()">REMOVE</button></div>`}).join('')||'<p>Your build is empty.</p>'}
function closeBuild(){document.querySelector('#build').classList.remove('show')}
document.querySelector('#search').addEventListener('input',e=>{const q=e.target.value.toLowerCase();render(products.filter(p=>Object.values(p).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q)))});
document.querySelector('#filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{const q=e.target.value;render(q==='ALL'?products:products.filter(p=>p.category===q||p.world===q))});
document.querySelector('#buildButton').onclick=openBuild;
render();

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/TUX-GEN-v0.2-complete/TUX-GEN/README.md

# TUX GEN — Uncle Ed's v0.2

This is the expanded end-to-end product prototype for **UNCLE ED'S — By Edris Nazparvar** and the TUX GEN Digital Tailoring System.

## Implemented UI/UX

Home / house console; onboarding; Quick/Guided/Bespoke mode choice; body profile; editable measurement database; guided scan workflow; scan quality; front/side/back stages; confidence model; body model; posture/asymmetry visualization; Design Studio; parametric garment preview; fit/proportion engine; Textile Lab; cost planning; Pattern Lab; tailor mode; fitting/version workflow; QC checklist; wardrobe; production stages; certificate; settings/privacy shell; JSON garment export.

## Locked 001 rules

- 001 = highest designation / ONE OF ONE, not first production model.
- 12 cm maximum lapel width.
- 4.5 cm trouser waistband target; 5 cm absolute maximum.
- Structured Boxy Hollywood fit.
- Crossover single-button architecture.
- Straight architectural peak-lapel edge.
- White formal shirt.
- Large black satin butterfly bow tie.
- Aged silver house hardware.
- Deep black 280–320 g/m² wool with subtle green depth.
- Black/deep forest cupro lining with hidden oxblood pathways.
- Nothing militaristic on the surface; purposeful engineering underneath.

## What is genuinely functional now

The UI is stateful during the session, controls update the garment model, measurements are editable, derived measurements update, scan workflow advances, simulated scan changes confidence/source, revisions increment, pattern manifest exports, garment JSON exports, and print views are available.

## The hard limits

The following are intentionally **not faked**:

1. Production-grade phone-only body scanning / 3D reconstruction.
2. Validated anthropometric solver from camera imagery.
3. True physical cloth simulation and production 3D garment drape.
4. A validated 1:1 pattern-drafting solver with real blocks and industrial grading.
5. DXF/AAMA/ASTM production pattern export.
6. Real supplier/fabric inventory, pricing and payment integrations.
7. Cloud authentication, encrypted body-data storage and production privacy infrastructure.
8. Real atelier/tailor network and order fulfillment.

The interfaces and data contracts for these capabilities are already represented so the engines can be plugged in rather than redesigning the product.

## Run

Node.js 18+ recommended:

```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

## Export

Use the Export button to download the current TUX GEN garment record as JSON. Pattern Lab can export a pattern manifest.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/TUX-GEN-v0.3-digital-atelier/README.md

# TUX GEN v0.3 — The Digital Atelier

A refined Uncle Ed's UI/UX layer for the TUX GEN digital tailoring system.

## Design direction
- Obsidian / deep forest / ivory / aged silver as the core palette
- Oxblood used as a discovery accent rather than a dominant UI color
- Editorial serif + technical sans/monospace hierarchy
- Atelier / Instrument / Archive visual language
- Restrained motion and drafting-line details
- Mobile responsive and reduced-motion aware

## Run
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

## What remains an external engineering boundary
- production-grade phone body scanning / multi-view 3D reconstruction
- physically accurate cloth/garment simulation
- validated production 1:1 pattern solver and DXF/AAMA/ASTM output
- cloud authentication, encrypted persistence, payments, vendors and tailor network

The interface is designed so those engines can be connected without redesigning the user journey.


---

@media(max-width:680px){.app{display:block}aside{width:auto;min-height:auto;border-right:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}nav{display:flex;overflow:auto}nav button{white-space:nowrap}.foot{display:none}header{height:auto;padding:18px;display:block}.head-actions{margin-top:12px}.content{padding:18px}.grid3,.grid4,.grid2{grid-template-columns:1fr}.timeline{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}.hero{display:block;padding:30px 24px}.hero h2{font-size:39px}.bigseal{margin:30px auto 0}.tr{grid-template-columns:1.3fr 1fr}.tr>*:nth-child(3),.tr>*:nth-child(4){display:none}.piecegrid,.specgrid,.qcgrid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.camera{height:430px}.jacket{transform:scale(.8)}}

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/Builds_and_Visuals/TUX-GEN-v1.0-house-redesign/TUX-GEN-v1.0/README.md

# TUX GEN 1.0 — UNCLE ED'S HOUSE SYSTEM

A complete redesign of TUX GEN around the UNCLE ED'S house ideology rather than a collection of tailoring utilities.

## House thesis
**The body is nature. The mind is civilization.**

Nature → Life → Desire → Civilization → Creation → Decay → Death → Nature.

TUX GEN is the digital instrument of an old, extremely sophisticated tailoring house that happens to have been designed in 2026.

## Product architecture
- HOUSE — philosophy, culture, visual language, rules
- 001 — the canonical One of One garment
- ATELIER — garment architecture and design decisions
- PSYCHOLOGY — presence / composure / mystery / danger translated into tailoring
- FIT — body truth, confidence and finished measurements
- MATERIAL — textile, lining, hardware and light behavior
- TAILOR — production-facing specification
- ARCHIVE — memory, versions, fittings, repairs and provenance

## House rules
1. Nothing looks accidental.
2. The outside creates intrigue. The inside rewards curiosity.
3. Function must have a reason.
4. Luxury is proportion, material, craft and time—not decoration.
5. Influence is digested, never copied.
6. Nothing militaristic on the surface. Everything purposeful underneath.
7. Death is expressed through memory and impermanence, not gothic decoration.

## 001
UNCLE ED'S 001 — ONE OF ONE — 2026
The Legacy Tuxedo.

Structured boxy Hollywood fit; crossover single-button architecture; 12 cm straight peak lapels; aged-silver house hardware; black satin large butterfly bow; full canvas; high armhole; high-rise trousers; 4.5 cm finished waistband; deep black 280–320 g/m² wool; black/deep-forest lining with hidden oxblood pathways.

## Cultural synthesis
Qaemshahr → Tehran → Paris → Rome → Los Angeles → New York → wherever relevant.

Iranian/Turkic/Mazandarani roots are treated as source material for sensibility, not surface decoration: forest, rain, architecture, craftsmanship, cinema, hospitality, modernity, geometry and memory.

## Product family
The same house language eventually governs tailoring, fragrance, leather goods, hardware, shirts, formal accessories and digital tools. The aged-silver button is the physical logo and hardware family anchor.

## Development note
This is a product/UX prototype and house-system foundation, not production-grade computer-vision scanning or mathematical pattern drafting. Those require dedicated CV/3D and pattern-engineering implementations.

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/EDRIS/EDRIS_App_Prototype_v1.0.md

# EDRIS — THE HOUSE APP v1.0

## Product purpose
A private mobile layer for the EDRIS house: discovery, commerce, digital passport, atelier communication, appointments, aftercare and archive.

## Navigation
HOUSE / SHOP / PASSPORT / ATELIER / ARCHIVE

## HOUSE
- Campaign hero
- Current collection
- Film
- House story
- New object releases
- Fragrance world

## SHOP
- Categories: tailoring, casual, outerwear, leather, footwear, jewelry, eyewear, fragrance, watches, objects
- Filters: material, color, fit, occasion, collection, availability
- Product page mirrors website but adds saved items and private recommendations

## PASSPORT
- My EDRIS objects
- Authenticity
- Provenance
- Care
- Service
- Alterations
- Warranty/records where applicable
- Watch serial/movement/service history
- Bespoke garment pattern and fitting history

## ATELIER
- Book consultation
- Measurement appointment
- Fitting calendar
- Bespoke project status
- Approvals
- Secure atelier messaging
- Repair/alteration request

## ARCHIVE
- Personal collection timeline
- Public house archive
- Campaigns
- Product stories
- Approved historical pieces

## Personalization rules
Use explicit preferences, purchase history and fitting data only where necessary and consented. The app must make privacy visible and controllable. No behavioral surveillance aesthetic, no spam, no artificial scarcity.

## Signature feature: THE OBJECT PASSPORT
Scan/enter the object ID to open its authenticated EDRIS record. The owner sees a private timeline of the object from creation through service and ownership/provenance events that they choose to record.

## Signature feature: ATELIER MIRROR
A private client view of the current bespoke project: silhouette, cloth, hardware, pattern revision, fitting stage, approvals and final archive record.

## Signature feature: THE HOUSE EDIT
A restrained personal edit: 3–8 products that work together based on the client's actual wardrobe, preferences, climate and occasion. Never a feed of endless recommendations.

## Push notification policy
Only meaningful service and client events. No daily promotional spam.

## Visual design
Near-black background, ivory type, aged-silver accents, editorial serif for stories, restrained sans for controls, sharp geometry, no excessive rounded cards, no gamified behavior.


---

# SOURCE FILE: _EXPANDED_ARCHIVES/044_USER_DATA_DUMP_AND_PROJECT_FILES/EDRIS/EDRIS_House_Catalogue_v3.0.md

# EDRIS — HOUSE CATALOGUE v3.0
## A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN
### BUILD AGAINST TIME.

**Status:** CANONICAL HOUSE MASTER / PRODUCT + BRAND + DIGITAL SYSTEM
**Version:** 3.0 — October 2026
**House:** EDRIS
**Founder / Creative Director:** EDRIS NAZPARVAR
**Public house:** EDRIS
**Internal bespoke engine:** TUX GEN
**House crest:** THE MASKED RABBIT

---

# 0. WHAT CHANGED IN v2.0

This edition expands EDRIS from a product catalogue into a complete house operating system: product universe, current-trend strategy, digital identity, website architecture, app architecture, commerce, clienteling, content, AI discoverability, provenance, atelier operations, launch hierarchy and future categories.

The 2026 market signal is clear: luxury is being forced back toward craftsmanship and creativity; jewelry is unusually strong; smart eyewear is emerging; menswear is mixing formal and casual codes; slimmer silhouettes are returning; rugged luxury and utility are becoming more sophisticated; neck accessories and brooch/tie details are rising; and AI is increasingly mediating product discovery. EDRIS adopts these signals selectively rather than chasing them. The house remains timeless first, current second. citeturn0search2turn0search1turn0search0turn0search4

**EDRIS rule:** trends may change the expression, never the identity.

## CANONICALIZATION NOTE — v3.0
This edition consolidates the prior EDRIS / Uncle Ed’s work into one house master. Earlier working names that conflicted with locked founder decisions have been normalized here. In particular: **001 is THE ONE OF ONE TUXEDO**; the 001 watch is **THE OPERATIVE**; the 001 fragrance is **AFTER THE RAIN**. **EDRIS** is the public house and **TUX GEN** is the internal bespoke/atelier system, not a public sub-brand. The 12 cm straight peak lapel, white formal shirt and aged-silver formal hardware remain locked for 001.

This master supersedes earlier catalogue drafts for house-level naming and product-universe decisions. Technical drawings, patterns, BOMs, supplier records, formulas, tolerances, regulatory files and final production approvals remain downstream controlled records.

---

# 1. HOUSE IDENTITY

## Name
**EDRIS**

## Descriptor
**A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN**

## House line
**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**

## Core philosophy
> We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.

## Master idea
**CIVILIZATION AGAINST NATURE, CREATED IN THE FACE OF MORTALITY.**

EDRIS is an Iranian luxury house using international visual language to explore civilization, ferality, time, decay, memory, desire and legacy.

Iran is the soul, not costume.

---

# 2. PERMANENT HOUSE DNA

1. **ANCIENT SOUL — IRAN** — architecture, geometry, gardens, poetry, metalwork, textiles, landscapes, rain, mountains, deserts, cities and history translated into hidden structure.
2. **DARK ARCHITECTURE** — Art Deco as structural grammar; Gothic/ruin as mood, monumentality and mortality.
3. **CINEMATIC GLAMOUR** — Old Hollywood, Bond precision, Grand Hotel theatrical discipline, Godfather authority and obsessive film-language detail.
4. **THE FERAL HUMAN** — instinct beneath civilization; sensuality, severity, danger and nature without literal animal costume.
5. **THE MACHINE** — engineered closures, mechanical beauty, functional detail, technology vs humanity.
6. **MORTALITY** — time, ruins, memory, destiny, death, inheritance and artifacts.

## Design test
A new EDRIS object must be:
1. Beautiful at first glance.
2. Slightly strange at second glance.
3. Meaningful at third glance.
4. Recognizable without the logo.
5. Iranian in soul, never costume.
6. Architectural and materially convincing.
7. Durable enough to become an artifact.
8. Useful or emotionally necessary in the client's life.

---

# 3. 2026 TREND TRANSLATION — NOT TREND CHASING

## Current signals adopted

### A. Slimmer, more controlled menswear
FW26 reporting points toward slimmer tops, fitted waists and more controlled outerwear after years of oversized silhouettes. citeturn0search1

**EDRIS translation:** sculpted rather than skinny; strong shoulder + controlled waist + clean vertical line. Keep 001's long Hollywood body and 12 cm straight lapel. Expand fitted house blocks for shirts, knitwear and selected coats.

### B. Formality is becoming fluid
2026 menswear increasingly mixes tuxedo/formal codes with denim, loosened ties and casual elements. citeturn0search0

**EDRIS translation:** build a wardrobe where the same house language moves from tuxedo → tailored suit → knit → denim → technical outerwear without losing ceremony.

### C. Neck accessories, brooches and tie hardware
Neckties, collars, brooches and tie pins are rising as expressive menswear details. citeturn0search1turn0search6

**EDRIS translation:** make the hardware family important: aged-silver tie bars, collar bars, lapel pins and a signature EDRIS brooch derived from the Masked Rabbit geometry.

### D. Rugged luxury / sophisticated utility
2026 forecasts highlight refined utility, durable materials, field jackets, earth tones and outdoor-performance references. citeturn0search1

**EDRIS translation:** add an EDRIS field/utility capsule in forest, tobacco-black, mineral brown and oxidized olive, using tailoring-level construction rather than gorpcore or military cosplay.

### E. Craftsmanship and investment value
Luxury's recalibration is moving attention back toward creativity, quality, craftsmanship and meaningful product value. Jewelry is a particularly strong category, while resale/provenance is increasingly important. citeturn0search2

**EDRIS translation:** jewelry, hardware, repairability, provenance, archive and aftercare become central house systems, not extras.

### F. Smart eyewear + AI discovery
Smart frames are expected to become a major wearable format, while AI increasingly mediates fashion search and product discovery. citeturn0search2turn0search4

**EDRIS translation:** build EDRIS eyewear with an eventual smart-frame architecture, but preserve the visual identity. Make the website/product catalogue semantically rich and machine-readable so AI systems can understand every product, material, size, story and availability.

### G. Watch challenge
Traditional watch usage is under pressure from smartwatches, especially among younger consumers. citeturn0news9

**EDRIS translation:** do not make a generic luxury watch. Make an exceptionally legible tactical-dress mechanical watch that earns wrist time through utility, Bond versatility, craftsmanship and provenance.

---

# 4. HOUSE VISUAL SYSTEM

## Core contrasts
- BLACK × WHITE
- POLISHED × OXIDIZED
- SOFT WOOL × HARD METAL
- ANIMAL × MASK
- ANCIENT × FUTURISTIC
- IRAN × HOLLYWOOD
- BEAUTY × UNEASE
- LIFE × DEATH

## Palette
**Primary:** midnight black, black, forest black, ink.
**Secondary:** deep bottle green, midnight navy, deep burgundy.
**Expansion:** mineral brown, tobacco-black, oxidized olive, stone, wet concrete, dark umber.
**Light:** ivory, formal white.
**Metal:** aged silver, polished silver, gunmetal; antique gold only when justified.

## Material language
Fine wool, velvet, silk, cashmere, cotton, linen where appropriate, cupro, horsehair canvas, natural tailoring materials, premium leather, sterling silver, darkened metals and selected stones.

---

# 5. THE MASKED RABBIT — MASTER CREST

Front-facing, symmetrical, vertically elongated, severe, intelligent and slightly unnatural.

- Very long narrow ears.
- Straight architectural ear edges.
- Stepped inner-ear geometry.
- Tall forehead.
- Narrow head.
- Angular elongated muzzle.
- Deep empty eye openings.
- No pupils or cartoon expression.
- Ceremonial mask construction.
- Subtle Iranian geometry.
- Art Deco architectural portal rather than heraldic shield.
- Lower convergence suggesting obelisk/tomb/mortality.
- Blackened/aged sterling silver master treatment.

Official marks:
1. FULL CREST — rabbit + architectural portal.
2. MASKED RABBIT — rabbit only.
3. RABBIT SILHOUETTE — simplified mark.

No text inside the crest.

---

# 6. FORMAL CLOTHING

## 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO — LOCKED

- Midnight/forest-black luxury wool, target 280–320 gsm.
- Single-breasted, one-button.
- Approx. 79–80 cm finished length subject to fitting.
- Sculpted Hollywood shoulder.
- High armhole.
- Full canvas.
- Horsehair chest piece.
- Structured shoulder and sleeve head.
- Double-jetted pockets.
- Double vents.
- **12 cm STRAIGHT PEAK LAPEL.** The line must remain crisp and architectural, never visibly curved.
- Deep black satin facing.
- White fine formal cotton shirt.
- Formal collar.
- Concealed/clean front.
- French cuffs.
- Aged-silver studs.
- Large black architectural butterfly bow.
- High-rise flat-front trousers.
- Side adjusters; no belt loops.
- Black satin side stripe.
- White boutonniere and pocket square.
- Aged-silver buttons/cufflinks/studs.
- Hidden Iranian geometry in lining/internal construction.

**Meaning:** Old Hollywood body + Art Deco geometry + Iranian hidden construction + Gothic severity + engineered tailoring.

## 002 — THE PATRON
Deep forest-black wool, two-button single-breasted, 9–10 cm straight peak lapel, black silk facing, sculpted shoulder, double vents, high-rise side-adjuster trousers, white shirt, black silk tie, aged-silver cufflinks.

## 003 — THE NOCTURNE
Midnight-black velvet dinner jacket, architectural shawl/peak geometry, black silk facing, slightly elongated body, sculpted waist, white shirt, black bow, high-rise trousers.

## 004 — THE SALON
Deep bottle green or deep burgundy jacket, black satin lapels, black trousers, ivory formal shirt, black bow, aged-silver hardware.

## THE STANDARD
Permanent house suit: midnight navy, forest black, charcoal, deep brown-black; two-button or restrained three-roll-two; straight architectural peak lapel; slightly extended jacket; high-rise trousers; side adjusters; natural but sculpted shoulder.

## NEW — THE VELVET HOUR
A modern 2026 bridge between tailoring and eveningwear: slim controlled jacket, high-rise wide-to-straight trouser, black silk neck detail, polished leather shoe, optional brooch/tie bar. No streetwear styling.

## NEW — THE COLUMN TROUSER
High-rise, clean front, long leg, controlled fullness through upper leg, strong crease, slight taper. Versions: wool, velvet, cotton, technical wool and dark denim.

---

# 7. CASUAL + UTILITY

## THE FOUNDATION TEE
Heavy premium cotton, structured shoulder, clean neck, slightly elongated body, black/ivory/forest-black/washed charcoal, no visible branding or micro rabbit.

## THE STRUCTURE SHIRT
Black or ivory premium cotton, relaxed but controlled body, architectural collar, hidden placket, strong cuffs, aged-silver buttons, subtle rear pleat.

## THE MONSOON SHIRT
Deep forest technical cotton or wool/cotton, weather-resistant, high collar, concealed hardware, architectural pockets, dark metal zipper.

## THE HEIRLOOM KNIT
Heavy black cashmere/wool, slightly dropped shoulder, longer body, architectural aged-silver buttons, hidden rabbit inside cuff.

## EDRIS THE DISTRICT DENIM
Near-black premium denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered, heavy fabric, silver hardware, no artificial rips, natural aging.

## NEW — THE SENTINEL FIELD JACKET
Luxury utility jacket inspired by the 2026 rugged-luxury direction but translated through EDRIS.
- Forest-black or mineral brown wool/cotton.
- Four architectural pockets, not military patchwork.
- Sculpted waist.
- High collar.
- Hidden aged-silver hardware.
- Optional removable liner.
- Hidden passport/document pocket.
- No camouflage, badges or tactical cosplay.

## NEW — THE CLUB POLO
Fine mercerized cotton/silk knit, close controlled fit, severe collar, three concealed buttons, small aged-silver internal button, no visible logo.

## NEW — THE EDRIS THE VEIL SCARF
Black silk/wool scarf with hidden Iranian geometry and a narrow aged-silver/rabbit hardware point. Designed for tailoring, knitwear and outerwear.

---

# 8. OUTERWEAR

## THE RUIN COAT
Long black wool coat below knee, strong controlled shoulder, straight front, hidden closure, high collar, architectural lapel option, deep internal pockets, hidden aged-silver hardware.

## THE PORTAL TRENCH
Long black body, strong straight silhouette, gunmetal hardware, wide belt, high collar, storm flap, dark lining with Iranian geometry.

## THE WILD JACKET
Black horsehide/exceptional leather, sculpted shoulder, slightly cropped, minimal hardware, heavy aged-silver zipper, architectural cuffs.

## NEW — THE OBSIDIAN BOMBER
A tailored bomber, not streetwear: close body, sculpted shoulder, clean hem, black technical wool, concealed zip, aged-silver pull, silk/cupro interior.

## NEW — THE FRONTIER COAT
A hybrid of military utility and tailored overcoat without military insignia. Mineral brown/forest black, hidden pockets, severe collar, removable quilted lining.

---

# 9. FOOTWEAR

## EDRIS THE MONUMENT
Black premium leather, slightly elongated toe, extremely clean upper, formal silhouette.

## THE SPIRE BOOT
Black leather Chelsea-inspired boot, slightly elongated toe, sculpted heel, strong side geometry, minimal elastic, small silver detail.

## THE RELIC BOOT
Heavy black leather, tall silhouette, architectural sole, gunmetal hardware, rugged but refined.

## EDRIS THE VECTOR SNEAKER
Low-profile black leather, black sole, subtle silver hardware, architectural panel geometry, rabbit hidden on tongue/interior.

## NEW — THE DECO LOAFER
Black leather evening loafer with an architectural metal bar derived from the house hardware family. Designed for tuxedo, suit and dark denim.

## NEW — THE OUTPOST SHOE
Luxury black/brown service-inspired shoe with a refined welted construction and architectural sole. Durable, not military.

---

# 10. LEATHER GOODS

## THE EDRIS THE DOSSIER
Rigid architectural black leather case, Art Deco rectangular proportions, aged-silver lock, rabbit engraved into lock, engineered work compartments.

## THE MIDNIGHT BAG
Soft black leather compact evening bag, substantial silver hardware, minimal exterior branding, dark architectural lining.

## THE VOYAGE CASE
Black leather garment/travel case with suit, shoe, jewelry and fragrance compartments.

Permanent range: briefcase, weekend bag, crossbody, backpack, wallet, cardholder, passport holder, belt, gloves, garment bag.

## NEW — THE TWENTY-FOUR
A structured-soft daily bag bridging briefcase and weekend bag. Laptop/document protection, hidden passport pocket, removable internal archive pouch, black leather, aged-silver mechanical closure.

## NEW — THE VAULT POUCH
Small leather pouch for watch, jewelry, fragrance atomizer, keys and personal objects; designed to live inside larger EDRIS bags.

---

# 11. EYEWEAR

## 01 THE NOIR
Thick black acetate, angular rectangular lens, strong masculine proportions, subtle silver temple detail.

## 02 THE DECO
Thin gunmetal frame, vertically elongated lens, restrained Art Deco geometry.

## 03 THE MASK
Black acetate + metal, subtly elongated eye shape, geometry echoing the Masked Rabbit without literal rabbit imagery.

## FUTURE 04 — EDRIS SMART FRAME
A smart-eyewear platform is reserved for a later release. The frame must look like EDRIS eyewear first and technology second. No visible gadget aesthetic. Potential features: discreet audio, camera/AI functions where legally and ethically appropriate, object authentication, translation, navigation and atelier/client services.

The 2026 smart-frame trend makes this a strategic future category, but EDRIS should enter only when the hardware is mature and the privacy model is credible. citeturn0search2

---

# 12. JEWELRY — STRATEGIC GROWTH CATEGORY

Jewelry becomes one of EDRIS's most important commercial and cultural categories because it naturally carries the house's artifact philosophy and aligns with the current growth of investment/self-expression jewelry. citeturn0search2

## THE RABBIT RING
Substantial sterling silver; architectural rabbit-mask face; polished forehead/high points; brushed planes; blackened recesses; hidden Iranian geometry.

## THE PORTAL RING
Architectural rectangular opening, recessed dark center, no rabbit, discreet everyday signature.

## THE LINK CHAIN
Heavy but elegant darkened sterling silver, custom architectural joint links.

## THE RELIC PENDANT
Simplified Masked Rabbit, long vertical profile, thin blackened-silver body, designed over a white formal shirt.

## THE BASTION CUFF
Wide controlled blackened silver, architectural edges, central vertical incision, tiny rabbit hidden inside.

## NEW — THE EDRIS THE SEAL BROOCH
A small architectural silver object derived from the rabbit/portal geometry. Designed for lapel, scarf, coat and knitwear. This directly translates the 2026 rise of brooch/tie styling into EDRIS. citeturn0search1

## NEW — THE EDRIS THE NEEDLE TIE PIN
Long aged-silver bar with an almost invisible central incision. Optional miniature rabbit at the terminal.

## NEW — THE EDRIS THE AXIS COLLAR BAR
Slim aged-silver architectural bar; designed for formal and relaxed shirt styling.

## NEW — THE EDRIS THE CREST SIGNET
A quieter ring: rectangular black stone or blackened silver face, tiny hidden rabbit on inner surface.

## HOUSE HARDWARE FAMILY
Buttons, cufflinks, studs, tie bars, collar bars, lapel pins, brooches, buckles, bag hardware and eyewear hardware share one geometry and finish map.

---

# 13. HOROLOGY — THE TACTICAL DRESS WATCH PROGRAM

## DESIGN BRIEF FROM FOUNDER TASTE
The founder's reference set is **Seiko SKX007 + Rolex Submariner + Vacheron Constantin + James Bond**.

Translation:
- SKX007 = honest instrument, legibility, durability, tool-watch character.
- Submariner = iconic dive-watch discipline, black dial/bezel, robust proportions.
- Vacheron Constantin = finishing, refinement, heritage and mechanical beauty.
- Bond = tactical elegance; a watch that can live with a suit and with a field jacket.

EDRIS must not copy any reference. It should synthesize the principles into an original house object.

## EDRIS 001 — THE OPERATIVE
**Core watch.**
- 40–41 mm case.
- Slim enough for a tuxedo cuff.
- Automatic mechanical movement.
- Strong water resistance.
- Screw-down crown.
- Rotating dive-style bezel.
- Matte black dial.
- Restrained aged-silver/gunmetal indices.
- Sword/architectural hands.
- Strong lume.
- Date only if it improves utility.
- EDRIS at 12; AUTOMATIC or equivalent restrained technical text at 6.
- Brushed bracelet with polished architectural bevels and clean taper.
- Black leather, refined rubber and black NATO options.
- Caseback: small Masked Rabbit maker seal.
- Movement: excellent finishing, appropriate to price tier; decorated rotor with restrained EDRIS geometry.

**Positioning:** a tactical instrument dressed for civilization.

## EDRIS 002 — THE HOUSE
- 39–40 mm.
- Thin refined case.
- No dive bezel.
- Black or ivory dial.
- Precious-metal option.
- Hand-finished or high-grade decorated movement.
- Architectural indices.
- Slim black leather strap.
- Tuxedo/black-tie use.

## EDRIS 003 — THE FIELD
Future rugged watch: slightly tougher, black/forest dial, textile or bracelet options, field-inspired but unmistakably luxury.

## DIGITAL WATCH PASSPORT
Every EDRIS watch should have a digital identity tied to serial number, movement, service history, authenticity and ownership/provenance with privacy controls.

---

# 14. FRAGRANCE

## 001 — AFTER THE RAIN
### THE BLACK TIE SHIPYARD

Scene: midnight tuxedo, tropical/dark rainforest after heavy rain, derelict shipyard, black ocean, wet concrete, rusting machinery, vegetation reclaiming civilization, campfire, smoke, cognac, cacao, honey and old wood.

Signature accord: **CARDAMOM × VETIVER × MINERAL RAIN**.

Opening: bergamot FCF, cardamom, black pepper, cold mineral rain, dark marine/mineral accord.
Heart: clary sage, violet leaf, iris, wet green foliage/fern, oakmoss, Haitian vetiver, vetiver acetate.
Smoke: guaiac wood, frankincense, labdanum, trace birch tar/cade, patchouli, cashmeran.
Base: Haitian vetiver, cedarwood, sandalwood, Iso E Super, Ambroxan, musk, oakmoss, labdanum.
Warmth: cognac accord, cacao/dark chocolate, honey, benzoin, vanilla, tonka.

Target: dark aquatic woody amber/extrait.

Bottle: tall, heavy, architectural, black/dark translucent glass, substantial aged-silver rabbit-mask cap.

Box: near-black/forest-black exterior, hidden Iranian geometry, chamber-like opening.

## 002 — THE ARCHIVE
Dry woods, paper, iris, incense, leather, mineral facets, tobacco nuance, old-room atmosphere.

## 003 — NOCTURNE
Black pepper, cardamom, amber, dark rose, sandalwood, cacao, vanilla, leather.

## FUTURE 004 — RAIN ON STONE
Mineral rain, wet stone, petrichor impression, cypress, iris, smoke, cold woods. A more architectural, less sweet sibling to 001.

# 18. PACKAGING SYSTEM

Universal language:
- near-black exterior
- restrained aged-silver details
- generous typography
- blind/debossed rabbit
- architectural reveal
- hidden Iranian geometry inside
- category-specific protection
- provenance/care information without clutter

Packaging should feel like opening an artifact chamber.

1. House mark.
2. Material/texture.
3. Mechanical or architectural reveal.
4. Object.
5. Provenance card.
6. Care/aftercare.
7. Digital passport activation.

# 19. WEBSITE — EDRIS.COM EXPERIENCE BLUEPRINT

## Principle
The website is not a normal luxury webshop. It is the digital entrance to the EDRIS world.

## Primary navigation
**HOUSE | COLLECTIONS | BESPOKE | OBJECTS | FRAGRANCE | JEWELRY | WATCHES | EYEWEAR | JOURNAL | ATELIER | ARCHIVE**

## Homepage
Hero: cinematic EDRIS image/video with minimal type.

Primary message:
**EDRIS**
*A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN*
**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**

Then:
- current collection
- 001 story
- Masked Rabbit
- atelier/bespoke invitation
- fragrance world
- jewelry/object spotlight
- journal/film
- archive/provenance

## Product page structure
1. Product name.
2. Hero visual.
3. Short emotional description.
4. Technical specifications.
5. Materials.
6. Construction/craft.
7. Fit and sizing.
8. Styling system.
9. Story/meaning.
10. Availability/edition number.
11. Digital passport/provenance.
12. Care/aftercare.
13. Related EDRIS objects.
14. Appointment / purchase.

## Bespoke flow
**Discover → Book → Measure → Design → Material → Fitting → Finalize**

No public client data. TUX GEN is accessed through authenticated client/atelier workflows.

## Journal
Sections:
- House
- Craft
- Iran
- Objects
- Film
- Fragrance
- Horology
- Atelier notes
- Archive

## AI-ready commerce
Every product needs structured, semantically rich fields: category, silhouette, materials, color, dimensions, function, occasion, fit, compatibility, care, price tier, availability, story, collection, SKU, edition, origin and related products. This supports search engines and AI-mediated shopping, which is becoming increasingly important. citeturn0search2turn0search4

## SEO/content rules
Never write generic keyword spam. Write real product knowledge with clear entity names, material facts, construction facts, use cases and house story.

# 20. WEBSITE VISUAL / UX SYSTEM

## Typography
- Primary: high-contrast editorial serif for major house/editorial moments.
- Secondary: restrained grotesk/sans for navigation, product data and technical information.
- Numerals should feel architectural.

## Layout
- generous negative space
- full-bleed cinematic imagery
- sharp vertical/horizontal geometry
- minimal rounded UI
- subtle transitions
- black/ivory foundation
- aged-silver used sparingly

## Interaction
- no excessive animation
- no autoplay audio
- fast performance
- tactile hover/scroll behavior
- product imagery with macro material views
- 360/AR only when useful

## Commerce
- guest checkout
- account checkout
- private client account
- appointments
- international shipping framework
- tax/duty messaging
- repair/aftercare requests
- digital passport
- waitlists
- limited-edition reservation

---

# 21. EDRIS APP — PRODUCT BLUEPRINT

Working name: **EDRIS — THE HOUSE**

## Bottom navigation
**HOUSE | SHOP | PASSPORT | ATELIER | ARCHIVE**

## HOUSE
Editorial home: current campaign, collection, films, fragrance, house stories.

## SHOP
Personalized but controlled product discovery. Filters: category, material, color, fit, occasion, collection.

## PASSPORT
User's authenticated EDRIS objects:
- garments
- watches
- jewelry
- fragrance
- leather goods
- provenance
- care
- service
- alterations
- repairs
- certificates

## ATELIER
- Book consultation.
- Measurement appointment.
- Bespoke project status.
- Fitting schedule.
- Design approvals.
- Messages with atelier.
- Care/repair request.

## ARCHIVE
Personal and public archive layers. Owners can see their pieces; public archive shows approved historical objects/campaigns.

## App personalization
Use first-party preference data, not surveillance. Client controls personalization. Privacy should feel like part of luxury, especially as 2026 brands face increasing tension between personalization and data collection. citeturn0search5

## Notifications
Only meaningful events:
- fitting ready
- alteration ready
- repair completed
- limited piece available
- appointment reminder
- passport update
- collection launch

No spam or artificial urgency.

# 22. TUX GEN — INTERNAL BESPOKE + ATELIER PLATFORM

Modules:
- client profile
- measurement/body scan data
- design concepts
- garment configuration
- material library
- hardware library
- pattern records
- fitting records
- alterations
- production workflow
- QC
- digital passport
- provenance
- archive

## Data principles
- unique garment ID
- versioned patterns
- timestamped measurements
- design revision history
- access control
- minimum necessary personal data
- production data separated from unnecessary personal data
- immutable historical records with documented corrections/versioning

## Bespoke workflow
Consultation → measurement → design brief → material selection → configuration → pattern → toile/prototype → fitting → correction → final construction → QC → delivery → passport → archive → aftercare.

---

# 23. DIGITAL PASSPORT / PROVENANCE

Each significant object can record:
- piece ID
- category
- collection
- creation date
- materials
- hardware
- construction notes
- pattern/version
- craftsperson/atelier where appropriate
- fitting/alteration history
- authenticity
- provenance where consented
- care instructions
- photography
- design notes
- service history

For watches: serial, movement, service, parts, authentication.
For jewelry: metal/alloy, stone information, weight where appropriate, workshop/finish.
For tailoring: original pattern, cloth, fittings and alterations.

Privacy is mandatory.

---

# 24. CLIENT EXPERIENCE / CRM

## Client tiers should be relationship-based, not status theatre
- House client
- Bespoke client
- Collector
- Archive/one-of-one client

## Client record
Preferences, sizes, prior purchases, fitting history, style preferences and explicit communications consent.

## Clienteling
A stylist/atelier member can prepare a private edit before appointment: garments, watch, jewelry, fragrance and accessories that naturally work together.

## No manipulative luxury tactics
No fake scarcity, fake countdowns, fabricated waitlists or artificial exclusivity.

---

# 25. SOCIAL / CONTENT SYSTEM

## Content pillars
1. **THE HOUSE** — philosophy, founder, Iranian soul.
2. **THE OBJECT** — product films, macro details, construction.
3. **THE ATELIER** — hands, tools, fittings, tailoring.
4. **THE ARCHIVE** — history, references, prototypes, failures.
5. **THE WORLD** — rain, architecture, ruins, Tehran/Iranian landscapes, cinema-like environments.
6. **THE CLIENT** — finished looks and private-client stories with consent.

## Visual rules
- cinematic lighting
- tactile close-ups
- architecture
- negative space
- black retains texture
- no generic influencer grid
- no logo repetition
- no trend memes as core identity

## Short film grammar
Silence → object → human → movement → detail → reveal → house line.

**BESPOKE** — individualized through TUX GEN and atelier craft.

# 28. RETAIL / ATELIER

Physical environment:
- black stone
- dark wood
- blackened metal
- selective greenery
- warm controlled light
- Iranian symmetry interpreted through Art Deco
- monumental scale without theme-park décor

Client journey:
**arrival → consultation → material discovery → design → fitting → reveal → packaging → aftercare → archive**

The atelier is the physical heart of EDRIS.

---

# 29. QUALITY SYSTEM

Every product requires:
- technical specification
- approved material
- approved hardware
- prototype/golden sample
- defined tolerances
- QC checklist
- packaging specification
- care specification
- archive record

Reject shortcuts that are invisible in a spec sheet but visible in the hand.

---

# 30. REGULATORY GATE

Before commercial release, qualified specialists must validate applicable requirements for:
- textile labeling/care/flammability
- jewelry alloy, hallmarking and restricted substances
- eyewear/optical/sunglass safety
- fragrance/cosmetic safety, IFRA and labeling
- leather chemical restrictions
- packaging/environmental requirements
- market-specific consumer/product rules
- privacy/data protection
- smart-device/recording functionality if applicable

This catalogue is a design and strategy master, not a substitute for legal/regulatory approval.

---

# 31. FORBIDDEN DIRECTIONS

EDRIS must never become:
- generic Persian luxury
- Persian costume
- costume Gothic
- medieval fantasy
- literal steampunk
- cyberpunk cosplay
- military cosplay
- generic streetwear
- logo-covered luxury
- fake aristocracy
- generic quiet luxury
- trend-chasing
- movie/game merchandise
- excessive skulls/gears/claws
- cheap dark aesthetic
- superficial AI gimmicks
- surveillance-heavy personalization

---

# 32. PRODUCT NAMING SYSTEM

EDRIS product names are permanent design identities, not generic SKU descriptions. Names draw from architecture, ritual, civilization, geography, time, objects and atmosphere.

Naming rules:
- Avoid generic category names as the primary identity.
- Avoid direct film, character or competitor references.
- Prefer one strong noun or a concise two-word construction.
- Numbers identify lineage; the name carries the soul.
- Names must work internationally and remain compatible with the Iranian foundation.
- Names must sound natural when spoken, engraved, photographed or archived.

The names below are the canonical EDRIS product names.

---

# 33. HOUSE NON-NEGOTIABLES

- EDRIS is the public house.
- TUX GEN is the internal bespoke engine.
- The Masked Rabbit is the house crest.
- BUILD AGAINST TIME. is the house line.
- Iran is the soul, not costume.
- 001 uses a 12 cm straight peak lapel.
- 001 uses a white formal shirt.
- Aged-silver hardware is the principal formal hardware language.
- Beauty comes first; darkness is atmosphere, not a gimmick.
- Craftsmanship must be real.
- Products must be worthy of becoming artifacts.
- Trends are translated, never copied.
- Technology must serve the object and the client, never dominate the house.
- Privacy is a luxury and must be treated as one.

---

# 34. MASTER HOUSE STATEMENT

EDRIS explores the tension between civilization and the feral human through clothing, objects, fragrance, jewelry, eyewear, horology and architecture. Iranian soul is hidden within an international language of Art Deco geometry, Gothic monumentality, cinematic glamour, engineered craft and controlled darkness.

We are born feral.
We build civilization around ourselves.
We make beauty, machines, architecture and ritual to give meaning to existence.
But civilization decays.
The body returns to nature.
The object becomes an artifact.
The memory becomes a story.
The story becomes legacy.

**We civilize the wild. We build against time. We know everything eventually returns to nature. So we make something worth leaving behind.**

---

# EDRIS — HOUSE ECOSYSTEM MASTER v4.2
## A HOUSE OF FASHION — IRAN
### BUILD AGAINST TIME.

**Status:** Canonical design + product + experience ecosystem master  
**House:** EDRIS  
**Founder / Creative Director:** Edris Nazparvar  
**Public house:** EDRIS  
**Internal bespoke / atelier engine:** TUX GEN  
**House crest:** THE MASKED RABBIT

---

## 1. THE COMPLETED HOUSE

EDRIS is not a pile of products. It is one connected house covering the client's life:

**CEREMONY → TAILORING → EVERYDAY → WEATHER → TRAVEL → OBJECTS → JEWELRY → TIME → FRAGRANCE → HOME → ARCHIVE**

The house is complete when every major need has a credible EDRIS answer without artificial SKU expansion.

Every product must:
1. Solve a real need.
2. Express EDRIS identity.
3. Connect naturally to other EDRIS products.
4. Remain desirable without the logo.

**Timeless first. Current second.** Trends may change expression, never identity.

---

## 2. PERMANENT HOUSE DNA

### Six pillars
- Ancient Soul — Iran
- Dark Architecture
- Cinematic Glamour
- The Feral Human
- The Machine
- Mortality

### Permanent contrasts
black × white; polished × oxidized; soft wool × hard metal; animal × mask; ancient × futuristic; Iran × Hollywood; beauty × unease; life × death; hand × machine; nature × civilization.

### Design test
1. Beautiful at first glance.
2. Slightly strange at second glance.
3. Meaningful at third glance.
4. Recognizable without the logo.
5. Iranian in soul, never costume.
6. Useful or emotionally necessary.
7. Durable enough to become an artifact.

### Forbidden
Generic Persian luxury, Persian costume, costume Gothic, medieval fantasy, literal steampunk, cyberpunk cosplay, military cosplay, generic streetwear, logo-covered luxury, fake aristocracy, generic quiet luxury, trend-chasing, movie/game merchandise, excessive skulls/gears/claws, cheap dark aesthetic, superficial AI gimmicks, surveillance-heavy personalization.

---

## 3. PRODUCT HIERARCHY

**O1 ONE OF ONE** — unique and permanently archived.  
**B BESPOKE** — individualized through TUX GEN.  
**MTM MADE TO MEASURE** — personalized EDRIS blocks.  
**RTW READY TO WEAR** — scalable house blocks.  
**LE LIMITED EDITION** — fixed quantity and numbered.  
**CORE** — permanent house products.

Production gate:
**DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE**

---

# 4. MASTER PRODUCT UNIVERSE

## FORMAL / TAILORING

### 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO — O1
**LOCKED.** Midnight / forest-black luxury wool; single-breasted one-button Hollywood body; sculpted Hollywood shoulder; high armhole; full canvas; horsehair chest; structured shoulder/sleeve head; double-jetted pockets; double vents; **12 cm straight peak lapel**; deep black satin facing. White fine formal cotton shirt, formal collar, French cuffs, concealed/clean front, aged-silver studs. Large architectural black butterfly bow. High-rise flat-front trousers, side adjusters, no belt loops, black satin side stripe. White boutonniere/pocket square. Aged-silver buttons/cufflinks/studs. Hidden Iranian geometry.

### THE COLUMN — CORE / MTM
Main EDRIS suit: two-button, long controlled body, high armhole, extended controlled shoulder, **9.5–10 cm straight peak**, controlled waist, high-rise trousers, strong crease, double vents. Forest-black, charcoal-black, midnight.

### THE PATRON — MTM
Authority suit: fuller chest, softer sculpted shoulder, 9–9.5 cm straight peak, longer jacket, high-rise fuller trouser. Forest-black, deep bottle green, very dark brown-black.

### THE NOCTURNE — LE
Black velvet evening tuxedo; one button, long body, strong shoulder, black silk facing, white shirt, large black bow. Main lapel remains architectural.

### THE SALON — LE
Deep bottle-green or black-burgundy velvet evening jacket; one button; long body; black satin facing; aged-silver button; white shirt; black trousers. No embroidery.

### THE AFTERHOURS — CORE
Black-brown wool bridge between formal and everyday; two-button; 8.5–9 cm straight peak; slightly shorter body; high-rise trousers; white shirt or fine black knit.

### THE COLUMN TROUSER — CORE
High rise, flat front, long leg, controlled thigh, strong crease, slight taper, side adjusters, no belt loops.

**THE VELVET HOUR is deleted as a standalone SKU; its strongest ideas live in NOCTURNE and SALON.**

---

## SHIRTS / KNITWEAR / CASUAL

**THE FOUNDATION TEE — CORE:** heavy compact cotton, strong shoulder, clean crew, slightly elongated straight body; black/ivory/forest/washed charcoal; no external logo.

**THE STRUCTURE SHIRT — CORE / SIGNATURE:** concealed placket, architectural collar, controlled body, long cuff, rear pleat, internal aged-silver buttons, stepped collar stand; black/ivory.

**THE MONSOON SHIRT — LE / WEATHER:** dense weather cloth, high collar, concealed zip, framed pockets, hidden travel pocket, dark hardware; functional, never military.

**THE HEIRLOOM KNIT — CORE:** heavy cashmere/wool, relaxed shoulder, long body, architectural cuff; black/ivory/forest; rabbit hidden inside garment.

**THE CLUB POLO — CORE:** mercerized cotton/silk, severe collar, controlled fit, three concealed buttons; no obvious branding.

**THE VEIL SCARF — CORE:** narrow black silk-wool, one architectural aged-silver terminal, hidden geometry.

**THE DISTRICT DENIM — CORE:** near-black heavy denim, high rise, straight/slightly tapered; no fake distressing, rips or contrast stitching.

---

## OUTERWEAR

**THE RUIN — CORE:** below-knee black wool, controlled shoulder, straight front, high collar, deep internal pockets, hidden back-vent geometry.

**THE PORTAL — CORE:** long architectural trench, high collar, clean storm construction, controlled belt, framed pockets, gunmetal/blackened hardware; no epaulettes.

**THE WILD — LE:** premium black horsehide or equivalent; slightly cropped, sculpted shoulder, close waist, minimal pockets; no biker clichés or excessive studs.

**THE OBSIDIAN — CORE:** tailored bomber in technical wool, clean rib/tailored hem, concealed zip, aged-silver pull; no graphics.

**THE FRONTIER — LE:** long civilized field coat in forest-black/mineral brown; severe collar, hidden utility, removable liner; survival without military cosplay.

**THE RAIN — CORE:** matte-black waterproof long silhouette, hidden hood, concealed/magnetic closure; modern technical EDRIS.

---

## FOOTWEAR

**THE MONUMENT — CORE:** formal wholecut; natural partner to 001 and COLUMN.  
**THE SPIRE — CORE:** Chelsea-derived architectural boot.  
**THE RELIC — LE:** genuinely rugged EDRIS boot; substantial leather, architectural lug sole, heavy welt, blackened hardware.  
**THE VECTOR — CORE:** minimal black sneaker, no logo wall or artificial futurism.  
**THE DECO — CORE:** architectural loafer with restrained metal bar.  
**THE OUTPOST — FUTURE / LE:** held back because of overlap with RELIC; release only if it earns a distinct role.

---

## LEATHER GOODS

**THE DOSSIER — CORE:** rigid black architectural briefcase with mechanical aged-silver lock.  
**THE MIDNIGHT — LE:** compact rectangular evening bag with one substantial silver closure.  
**THE VOYAGE — CORE:** travel case with suit/shoe/watch/jewelry/fragrance/document organization.  
**THE TWENTY-FOUR — CORE:** daily bag between briefcase and weekend bag; signature silhouette.  
**THE VAULT — CORE:** personal-object case for watch, jewelry, fragrance, passport and keys.  
**THE FOLD — CORE:** ultra-thin bifold.  
**THE CARD — CORE:** minimal cardholder.  
**THE PASSPORT — CORE:** passport holder.  
**THE CINCTURE — CORE:** house belt; portal-geometry buckle, never rabbit-shaped.  
**THE HAND — LE:** clean formal glove.  
**THE WARDROBE — CORE:** garment bag and part of the travel system.

---

## EYEWEAR

**THE NOIR — CORE:** thick black acetate angular rectangular signature frame.  
**THE DECO — LE:** thin gunmetal architectural frame.  
**THE MASK — LE:** abstract rabbit influence only; no literal rabbit face.  
**THE ORACLE — FUTURE:** smart eyewear platform; remove the electronics and it must still be unmistakably EDRIS.

---

## JEWELRY / HOUSE HARDWARE

Primary metal: aged/blackened sterling silver. Secondary: brushed gunmetal and polished silver.

House geometry: **narrow vertical incision crossing a framed/stepped architectural form.**

**THE RABBIT — ICON:** substantial sterling silver, blackened recesses, polished high points.  
**THE PORTAL — CORE:** everyday architectural ring.  
**THE LINK — LE:** heavy articulated silver chain with vertical incision in links.  
**THE RELIC — LE:** vertical pendant using simplified rabbit geometry.  
**THE BASTION — LE:** wide architectural cuff.  
**THE SEAL — CORE:** quiet architectural brooch for tailoring.  
**THE NEEDLE — CORE:** thin tie pin.  
**THE AXIS — CORE:** collar-bar system.  
**THE CREST — CORE:** quiet signet; rabbit inside rather than displayed literally.  
**HOUSE CUFFLINKS — CORE:** designed for 001.  
**HOUSE STUDS — CORE:** designed for 001.  
**THE RELIC BRACELET — LE:** three articulated architectural plates with hidden rabbit on central plate.

**Rule: one excellent hardware piece beats five decorative pieces.**

---

## HOROLOGY

**THE OPERATIVE — FLAGSHIP:** 40–41 mm automatic; black dial, instrument bezel, strong water resistance, screw-down crown, slim enough for formal wear; original architecture; instrument honesty + refined finishing + versatile evening/day use without copying existing watches.

**THE HOUSE — LE:** 39–40 mm formal watch; thin, black/ivory dial, precious-metal option, fine movement.

**THE FIELD — FUTURE:** 40–41 mm rugged field companion; distinct from OPERATIVE and not military cosplay.

---

## FRAGRANCE

**001 AFTER THE RAIN — FLAGSHIP:** midnight tuxedo + tropical/dark rainforest after heavy rain + derelict shipyard + black ocean + wet concrete + rusting machinery + vegetation reclaiming civilization + campfire + smoke + cognac + cacao + honey + old wood. Signature accord: **cardamom × vetiver × mineral rain**. Tall heavy architectural black/dark translucent glass with aged-silver Masked Rabbit cap.

**THE ARCHIVE:** dry woods, paper, iris, incense, leather, mineral, tobacco nuance; old-room atmosphere.

**THE NOCTURNE:** black pepper, cardamom, dark rose, amber, sandalwood, cacao, vanilla, leather.

**RAIN ON STONE — FUTURE:** only released if clearly colder and more austere than AFTER THE RAIN; otherwise deleted.

---

## ACCESSORIES

**THE 12 CM BOW — ICON:** oversized architectural butterfly bow, black silk.  
**THE TIE — CORE:** wide controlled silk, black/forest/midnight.  
**THE POCKET SQUARE — CORE:** white silk with black border; optional hidden rabbit.  
**THE SUSPENDER — CORE:** black silk/wool, leather tabs, aged-silver adjusters.  
**THE LAPEL PIN — CORE:** narrow architectural silver mark, not rabbit.  
**THE AXIS — CORE:** collar-bar system; separate COLLAR BAR SKU eliminated.  
**THE TRAVEL OBJECT SET — LE:** passport holder + cardholder + watch pouch + jewelry pouch + fragrance atomizer + garment tags.

---

## GROOMING — FUTURE RITUAL

Hand/body cleanser; hair pomade; beard oil; aftershave balm; solid fragrance; incense.

Grooming does not launch before the fragrance world is established.

---

## HOME / OBJECTS

**THE OBSERVATORY:** architectural lamp.  
**THE ALTAR:** object tray.  
**THE RELIQUARY:** archive/storage box.  
**THE WET STONE:** candle.  
**THE BAR — FUTURE:** restrained hospitality object.  
**THE DESK — FUTURE:** writing/work object.

Home remains small, useful and collectible; never a lifestyle-store filler category.

---

# 5. PRODUCT FAMILY / SUGGESTION SYSTEM

## 001 — THE CEREMONY SYSTEM
001 TUXEDO → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → HOUSE STUDS → HOUSE CUFFLINKS → MONUMENT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN → VAULT.

## THE COLUMN — THE HOUSE SUIT
COLUMN → STRUCTURE SHIRT → NEEDLE → SEAL → MONUMENT → DOSSIER → OPERATIVE.
Alternative: COLUMN → HEIRLOOM KNIT → SPIRE → TWENTY-FOUR.

## THE PATRON — AUTHORITY
PATRON → STRUCTURE SHIRT → CREST → DECO → DOSSIER → HOUSE → ARCHIVE.

## THE NOCTURNE — NIGHT
NOCTURNE → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → SEAL → DECO → MIDNIGHT → NOCTURNE FRAGRANCE.

## THE RUIN — WINTER
RUIN → HEIRLOOM KNIT → COLUMN TROUSER → SPIRE → VAULT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN.

## THE RAIN — WEATHER
RAIN → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → VOYAGE → AFTER THE RAIN.

## THE WILD — FERAL
WILD → FOUNDATION TEE → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → LINK → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN.

## THE FRONTIER — TRAVEL
FRONTIER → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RAIN → RELIC → VOYAGE → FIELD.

## THE ARTIFACT SYSTEM
RABBIT → SEAL → VAULT → RELIQUARY → ARCHIVE → OPERATIVE.

## THE WEATHER SYSTEM
MONSOON → RAIN → AFTER THE RAIN → RAIN ON STONE.

---

# 6. PRODUCT-PAGE RECOMMENDATION ENGINE

Every EDRIS product page uses four relationship layers:

**WEAR WITH** — garments that complete the look.  
**COMPLETE THE OBJECT** — accessories, jewelry, footwear or supporting objects.  
**THE EDRIS WORLD** — products sharing the same conceptual family.  
**NEXT OBJECT** — one natural next purchase, never arbitrary cross-selling.

Example — STRUCTURE SHIRT:
- Wear with: COLUMN · PATRON · DISTRICT DENIM
- Complete with: SEAL · AXIS · MONUMENT
- Travel with: DOSSIER
- Scent with: AFTER THE RAIN

This logic powers website, app, commerce and clienteling.

---

# 7. CLIENT JOURNEY

**DISCOVER → DESIRE → ENTER → BUILD → BESPOKE → ACQUIRE → WEAR → TRAVEL → CARE → REPAIR → ARCHIVE → RETURN**

A client may enter through tailoring, a shirt, fragrance, jewelry, eyewear, leather or watch. The ecosystem then naturally introduces adjacent worlds.

No forced cross-selling.

---

# 8. TUX GEN — INTERNAL BESPOKE ENGINE

TUX GEN connects consultation, body measurements, pattern blocks, material selection, hardware selection, fitting, alterations, construction records, golden samples, care, repair, provenance, client archive and future re-orders.

TUX GEN is not a public sub-brand. The customer experiences **EDRIS**.

---

# 9. PROVENANCE / DIGITAL PASSPORT / ARCHIVE

For significant objects:

**OBJECT ID → PRODUCT FAMILY → MATERIALS → HARDWARE → CRAFT RECORD → EDITION → CLIENT RECORD WHERE LEGALLY APPROPRIATE → CARE → REPAIR → ALTERATION → AUTHENTICITY → ARCHIVE**

Physical craft remains primary. Digital technology supports provenance; it does not become the product.

---

# 10. PACKAGING SYSTEM

Universal architecture:

**OUTER SHELL → REVEAL → OBJECT CHAMBER → PROVENANCE → CARE → DIGITAL PASSPORT**

Near-black board, black textile, selective aged silver, blind/debossed crest, hidden Iranian geometry, magnetic/mechanical closures. No glossy logo-heavy packaging.

---

# 11. ATELIER / RETAIL EXPERIENCE

Black stone; dark wood; blackened metal; selective greenery; warm controlled light; Iranian symmetry interpreted through Art Deco; monumental scale without theme-park décor.

Client journey:
**arrival → consultation → material discovery → design → fitting → reveal → packaging → aftercare → archive**

The atelier is the physical heart of EDRIS.

---

# 12. DIGITAL HOUSE

Primary digital worlds:
- SHOP
- TAILORING / TUX GEN
- OBJECTS
- JEWELRY
- WATCHES
- FRAGRANCE
- TRAVEL
- HOUSE
- ARCHIVE
- ATELIER

Discovery works by product, outfit, family, occasion, material, weather, journey, object and story.

AI may improve discovery and client assistance but must not become the identity of the house or enable intrusive surveillance.

---

# 13. CONTENT SYSTEM

**THE HOUSE** — identity, architecture, craft.  
**THE GARMENT** — tailoring, construction, material.  
**THE OBJECT** — watches, jewelry, leather, eyewear.  
**THE WORLD** — Iran, rain, ruins, nature, cities, night.  
**THE HUMAN** — desire, ceremony, solitude, movement.  
**THE ARCHIVE** — provenance, repair, aging, memory, legacy.

Products appear inside worlds, not as endless isolated advertisements.

---

# 14. LAUNCH ARCHITECTURE

## FOUNDATION
001 TUXEDO; COLUMN; STRUCTURE SHIRT; FOUNDATION TEE; DISTRICT DENIM; RUIN; MONUMENT; DOSSIER; RABBIT; SEAL; HOUSE CUFFLINKS; HOUSE STUDS; OPERATIVE; AFTER THE RAIN; 12 CM BOW; TIE; POCKET SQUARE; VAULT.

## SECOND MOVEMENT
PATRON; AFTERHOURS; HEIRLOOM KNIT; MONSOON; PORTAL; WILD; OBSIDIAN; SPIRE; DECO; TWENTY-FOUR; NOIR; LINK; CREST; AXIS; HOUSE; ARCHIVE; MIDNIGHT; VOYAGE.

## THIRD MOVEMENT
NOCTURNE; SALON; RAIN; FRONTIER; RELIC; VECTOR; FOLD; CARD; PASSPORT; CINCTURE; WARDROBE; MASK; BASTION; RELIC PENDANT; RELIC BRACELET; NOCTURNE FRAGRANCE; OBSERVATORY; ALTAR; RELIQUARY; WET STONE.

## FUTURE / CONDITIONAL
OUTPOST; ORACLE; FIELD; RAIN ON STONE; GROOMING; BAR; DESK.

Future products enter production only when they add a genuinely new function, silhouette or emotional world.

---

# 15. HOUSE COMPLETENESS CHECK

EDRIS now has a deliberate answer for:

- black-tie ceremony
- formal tailoring
- smart everyday
- casual everyday
- knitwear
- denim
- rain
- winter
- travel
- rugged/feral expression
- footwear
- business/work
- leather goods
- eyewear
- jewelry
- watch
- fragrance
- grooming
- home/object
- bespoke service
- provenance
- archive

The house is therefore **complete as an ecosystem without needing endless categories**.

---

# 16. QUALITY / RELEASE GATE

Every product requires:
- technical specification
- approved material
- approved hardware
- prototype / golden sample
- defined tolerances
- QC checklist
- packaging specification
- care specification
- archive record

Commercial release additionally requires applicable regulatory, labeling, safety, costing, pricing, photography, product-story, provenance and aftercare records.

---

# 17. FINAL HOUSE PRINCIPLE

**EDRIS sells a world, not a pile of products.**

A tuxedo leads to a shirt.  
A shirt leads to a suit.  
A suit leads to a watch.  
A watch leads to a leather object.  
A leather object leads to travel.  
Travel leads to fragrance.  
Fragrance leads to memory.  
Memory becomes archive.  
Archive becomes legacy.

**BUILD AGAINST TIME.**

# EDRIS HOUSE — EXECUTION MASTER v5.0
## From Designed House to Executable House
**House:** EDRIS  
**Internal atelier engine:** TUX GEN  
**Crest:** THE MASKED RABBIT  
**Motto:** BUILD AGAINST TIME.  
**Status:** Canonical execution layer following Ecosystem Master v4.2

---

# 1. EXECUTIVE DECISION

The EDRIS ecosystem is conceptually complete. v5.0 converts that completed concept into an executable operating system.

The house is governed by five gates:

**DESIGN → ENGINEERING → CRAFT → HOUSE → RELEASE**

No product moves to commercial release without passing all five.

The house principle remains:

> EDRIS sells a world, not a pile of products.

---

# 2. FINAL ECOSYSTEM AUDIT

## 2.1 Result

**HOUSE STATUS: COMPLETE.**

No new major category is required. The remaining work is specification, sampling, costing, supplier development, legal/regulatory preparation, digital implementation, launch sequencing and archival discipline.

## 2.2 Deliberate exclusions / holds

| Product | Decision | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| THE VELVET HOUR | Deleted | Ideas absorbed into NOCTURNE and SALON |
| COLLAR BAR | Merged | Becomes AXIS system |
| THE OUTPOST | Hold | Must earn a role distinct from RELIC |
| THE ORACLE | Hold | Smart eyewear only if design remains EDRIS without electronics |
| THE FIELD | Hold | Future rugged watch; must not duplicate OPERATIVE |
| RAIN ON STONE | Conditional | Only if materially colder/austerer than AFTER THE RAIN |
| GROOMING | Hold | Launch after fragrance world is established |
| THE BAR | Future | Useful hospitality object, not filler |
| THE DESK | Future | Useful writing/work object, not filler |

## 2.3 Overlap rules

1. One product = one primary job.
2. A new SKU must add a new function, silhouette or emotional world.
3. If a new product merely changes color/material, it is a variant—not a new house product.
4. If two products solve the same problem, keep the stronger one.
5. LE products may be more expressive, but may not violate house DNA.
6. Future products remain outside launch until they pass the same audit.

---

Every EDRIS product receives one canonical Product Record.

- Product ID

- Family
- Tier: O1 / B / MTM / RTW / LE / CORE
- Primary customer job

- Hardware

- Key measurements
- Tolerances

- Golden sample status

- Repairability

- Photography requirements
- Product story
- Recommended products
- Next Object
- Launch movement
- Cost target
- Wholesale target if applicable
- Retail target
- Margin target
- Regulatory / labeling requirements
- Archive status

### Product release statuses
**CONCEPT → DESIGNED → ENGINEERED → PROTOTYPE → FIT APPROVED → GOLDEN SAMPLE → COSTED → QC APPROVED → CONTENT READY → RELEASED → ARCHIVED**

# 4. TECHNICAL PRODUCT FAMILY STANDARD

## 4.1 Tailoring

Every jacket record must specify:
- body length
- chest
- waist
- seat
- shoulder width
- back width
- armhole depth
- sleeve length
- biceps
- elbow
- cuff
- lapel width
- lapel break position
- gorge height
- button position
- pocket position
- vent length
- canvas specification
- chest piece
- shoulder construction
- sleeve head
- lining
- seam allowances
- pressing map
- alteration limits

Every trouser record must specify:
- waist
- front rise
- back rise
- seat
- thigh
- knee
- hem
- inseam
- crease position
- waistband construction
- closure
- side adjuster position
- pocket construction
- satin stripe where applicable

## 4.2 Shirts
Specify collar geometry, collar stand, chest, waist, back pleat, cuff, placket, button/stud system, sleeve pitch, hem and shrinkage tolerance.

## 4.3 Outerwear
Specify shell, membrane or weather resistance, seam sealing, insulation/liner, closure system, pocket architecture, hood, cuff, hem, hardware and repair strategy.

## 4.4 Footwear
Specify last, toe shape, heel, sole, welt, upper, lining, insole, shank, hardware, construction method, flex point, outsole compound and resoling plan.

## 4.5 Leather
Specify leather species/type, tannage, thickness, reinforcement, edge paint/burnish, stitching, hardware, lining, compartments, closure cycles and repairability.

## 4.6 Jewelry / hardware
Specify metal alloy, dimensions, weight, finish, oxidation treatment, polish zones, engraving/geometry, stone policy if any, clasp/hinge cycles and surface tolerance.

## 4.7 Watches
Specify case dimensions, lug geometry, crown, water resistance target, crystal, dial, hands, movement, bracelet/strap, clasp, service interval and originality review.

## 4.8 Fragrance
Specify formula brief, concentration, stability testing, bottle dimensions, closure, pump, compatibility, packaging, batch code, safety and labeling requirements.

---

# 5. EDRIS 001 — ONE OF ONE TUXEDO: TECHNICAL MASTER

## Locked identity
- Midnight / forest-black luxury wool
- Single-breasted
- One button
- Long Hollywood body
- Sculpted Hollywood shoulder
- High armhole
- Full canvas
- Horsehair chest
- Structured shoulder and sleeve head
- Double-jetted pockets
- Double vents
- **12 cm straight peak lapel — maximum**
- Deep black satin facing
- White fine formal cotton shirt
- Formal collar
- French cuffs
- Concealed / clean front
- Aged-silver shirt studs
- Large architectural black butterfly bow
- High-rise flat-front trouser
- Side adjusters
- No belt loops
- Black satin side stripe
- White boutonniere
- White pocket square
- Aged-silver jacket button and cufflinks
- Hidden Iranian geometry

## User-provided body measurements — preserved
1. 49 cm  
2. 54 cm  
3. 30 cm  
4. 56 cm  
5. 105 cm  
6. 73 cm  
7. 35 cm  
8. 18 cm  
9. 70 cm shoulder-to-wrist

**Important:** these raw measurements must be reconciled against the tailor's measurement chart before cutting. They are not automatically finished-garment measurements.

## Lapel engineering
The lapel must read as an architectural line, not a soft curve.

Rules:
- 12 cm finished width at the widest controlled point.
- Straight outer edge from break toward peak.
- Straight/controlled inner edge.
- Peak tip sharp and symmetrical.
- No decorative belly.
- No exaggerated roll that visually curves the line.
- Gorge and break must be aligned to preserve the long Hollywood proportion.
- Satin facing follows the architectural geometry cleanly.

## Jacket construction order
1. Verify paper pattern.
2. Cut shell, canvas, chest piece and lining.
3. Mark grain, balance lines and pocket placements.
4. Build front canvas and chest.
5. Shape shoulder and chest by hand pressing.
6. Form lapel roll without creating a visibly curved outer edge.
7. Construct collar and gorge.
8. Set sleeves at high armhole with controlled pitch.
9. Construct double-jetted pockets.
10. Construct double vents.
11. Close body and lining.
12. Hand finish buttonhole and button.
13. Apply aged-silver hardware.
14. Final press.
15. Fit on body.
16. Record alterations.
17. Produce golden sample record.

## 001 QC gate
- Lapels: 12 cm ± approved production tolerance.
- Left/right peak symmetry.
- Break height symmetry.
- Shoulder balance.
- Collar clean against neck.
- Sleeve pitch natural at rest.
- No pulling across chest.
- No collapse at armhole.
- Pocket jets parallel.
- Vents equal.
- Trouser crease centered.
- Satin stripe uninterrupted.
- Shirt studs aligned.
- Silver hardware finish consistent.
- White shirt remains deliberately crisp against black tuxedo.

# 6. RECOMMENDATION ENGINE — MASTER LOGIC

Every product page contains exactly four recommendation roles:

### WEAR WITH
The garments that make the primary look work.

### COMPLETE THE OBJECT
Accessories, jewelry, footwear or supporting objects that complete the purchase.

### THE EDRIS WORLD
Conceptually related products, not necessarily commercially adjacent.

### NEXT OBJECT
Exactly one strongest next purchase.

## Ranking logic
1. Functional necessity
2. Visual compatibility
3. Same house language
4. Same use occasion
5. Family continuity
6. Customer stage
7. Purchase diversity
8. No forced upsell

## Recommendation exclusions
Do not recommend:
- unrelated categories merely to increase basket size
- multiple near-identical alternatives
- products unavailable in the client's size/region
- products that contradict the outfit
- future/held products as if they are purchasable

## Family graph

**001 CEREMONY:** 001 → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → STUDS → CUFFLINKS → MONUMENT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN → VAULT

**COLUMN HOUSE SUIT:** COLUMN → STRUCTURE SHIRT → NEEDLE → SEAL → MONUMENT → DOSSIER → OPERATIVE

**PATRON AUTHORITY:** PATRON → STRUCTURE SHIRT → CREST → DECO → DOSSIER → HOUSE → ARCHIVE

**NOCTURNE NIGHT:** NOCTURNE → STRUCTURE SHIRT → 12 CM BOW → SEAL → DECO → MIDNIGHT → NOCTURNE FRAGRANCE

**RUIN WINTER:** RUIN → HEIRLOOM KNIT → COLUMN TROUSER → SPIRE → VAULT → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN

**RAIN WEATHER:** RAIN → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → VOYAGE → AFTER THE RAIN

**WILD FERAL:** WILD → FOUNDATION TEE → DISTRICT DENIM → RELIC → LINK → OPERATIVE → AFTER THE RAIN

**FRONTIER TRAVEL:** FRONTIER → MONSOON → DISTRICT DENIM → RAIN → RELIC → VOYAGE → FIELD

**ARTIFACT:** RABBIT → SEAL → VAULT → RELIQUARY → ARCHIVE → OPERATIVE

---

# 7. VISUAL DESIGN SYSTEM

## 7.1 Wordmark
EDRIS wordmark must be restrained, architectural and legible. No decorative Persian imitation. No permanent dependency on the crest.

## 7.2 Masked Rabbit
Use as a house signature, not wallpaper.

Primary forms:
- blind/debossed
- cast metal
- engraved
- embroidered only when materially justified
- architectural negative space

Never use as a cartoon mascot.

## 7.3 Typography
System should use:
- one high-contrast editorial serif or equivalent for ceremonial/editorial expression
- one neutral architectural sans for navigation, product data and utility
- one optional monospaced/technical face for provenance and atelier records

Typography must feel cinematic and engineered rather than futuristic.

## 7.4 Photography
Six recurring visual worlds:
- ceremony
- architecture
- weather
- wilderness
- machine/workshop
- archive/memory

Rules:
- human presence over sterile product-only imagery
- controlled shadow
- tactile material detail
- no excessive black crush
- no generic Persian props
- no fake aristocratic mansion clichés
- no AI-generated people presented as real campaigns

## 7.5 Color architecture
Core: black, forest-black, ivory/white, mineral grey, aged silver.
Secondary: bottle green, black-burgundy, mineral brown.
No rainbow seasonal identity.

## 7.6 Hardware
Master geometry:
**narrow vertical incision crossing a framed/stepped architectural form.**

The geometry should be recognizable across a button, clasp, ring, watch crown and fragrance cap without literally repeating the same object.

---

# 8. PACKAGING EXECUTION STANDARD

Universal sequence:
**OUTER SHELL → REVEAL → OBJECT CHAMBER → PROVENANCE → CARE → DIGITAL PASSPORT**

Materials:
- near-black board
- black textile
- aged/blackened metal
- blind/debossed marks
- concealed geometry

Packaging QC:
- closure cycle test
- abrasion test
- transit protection
- humidity considerations
- hardware tarnish control
- correct provenance record
- correct care insert
- correct product identity

Packaging must feel like opening an EDRIS object, not opening advertising.

---

# 9. TUX GEN — OPERATING SYSTEM

## Client record
- client ID

- posture observations
- fit preferences
- style preferences
- previous garments
- alterations
- material history
- hardware history

## Bespoke workflow
**CONSULT → MEASURE → DESIGN → MATERIAL → PATTERN → FIRST FIT → SECOND FIT → GOLDEN FIT → MAKE → QC → REVEAL → CARE → ARCHIVE**

## Pattern versioning
Every pattern receives:
**PRODUCT / CLIENT / BLOCK / VERSION / DATE / MAKER**

Never overwrite a proven pattern. Create a new version.

## Privacy rule
Collect only information required for service, fit, provenance or legally necessary operations. No surveillance-based personalization.

---

# 10. PROVENANCE / ARCHIVE

Every significant object receives:

**OBJECT ID → PRODUCT FAMILY → MATERIALS → HARDWARE → CRAFT RECORD → EDITION → CLIENT RECORD WHERE APPROPRIATE → CARE → REPAIR → ALTERATION → AUTHENTICITY → ARCHIVE**

Physical archive remains authoritative. Digital passport is supporting evidence.

## Repair principle
A repaired EDRIS object should become more historically valuable, not disposable.

Repairs must be logged with:
- date
- reason
- component replaced
- material used
- maker
- photographs where appropriate
- post-repair condition

---

**ARRIVAL → CONSULTATION → MATERIAL DISCOVERY → DESIGN → FITTING → REVEAL → PACKAGING → AFTERCARE → ARCHIVE**

- black stone
- dark wood
- blackened metal
- controlled warm light
- selective greenery
- Iranian symmetry translated through Art Deco
- no theme-park Persian décor

## Service behavior
EDRIS should feel calm, exact, private and human.

No aggressive sales choreography.

# 14. PRODUCTION ROADMAP

## Phase 0 — House lock
- Freeze v4.2 ecosystem.
- Approve v5.0 execution rules.
